local Module = {}

local function UnlockFPS()
    if Util and Util.FPSTracker and Util.FPSTracker.FPS > 60 then
        if setfpscap then
            setfpscap(60)
        elseif syn and syn.set_fps_cap then
            syn.set_fps_cap(60)
        elseif setfps then
            setfps(60)
        end
    end
end
task.spawn(UnlockFPS)

    function self:LoadSettings()

                return self.httpService:JSONDecode(fileContent)

        end

function Module:SetupFunctions()
    function EquipWeapon(weaponName)
        if weaponName then
            if self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild(weaponName) then
                self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild(weaponName))
            end
        end
    end
    function weaponSc(toolTip)
        for _, tool in pairs(self.localPlayer3.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA('Tool') and tool.ToolTip == toolTip then
                EquipWeapon(tool.Name)
            end
        end
    end
    if hookfunction then
        pcall(function()
            hookfunction(require(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):WaitForChild('GuideModule')).ChangeDisplayedNPC, function() end)
            hookfunction(error, function() end)
            hookfunction(warn, function() end)
        end)
    end
    local rocks = workspace:FindFirstChild('Rocks')
    if rocks then rocks:Destroy() end
    gay = (function()
        game:GetService('Lighting'):FindFirstChild('LightingLayers')
        local foam = workspace._WorldOrigin['Foam;']
        if foam and workspace._WorldOrigin['Foam;'] then
            foam:Destroy()
        end
    end)()
    self.MobUtils = {}
    self.MobUtils.__index = self.MobUtils
    function self.MobUtils.Alive(mob)
        if mob then
            local humanoid = mob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
            if humanoid then
                return humanoid.Health > 0
            end
            return false
        end
        return false
    end
    function self.MobUtils.Pos(_, mob)
        return (Root.Position - mode.Position).Magnitude <= range
    end
    function self.MobUtils.Dist(mob, range)
        return (Root.Position - mob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart').Position).Magnitude <= range
    end
    function self.MobUtils.DistH(mob, range)
        return range < (Root.Position - mob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart').Position).Magnitude
    end
    self.currentMob = nil
    local function setCharacterCollision(disable)
        for _, part in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA('BasePart') then
                part.CanCollide = not disable
            end
        end
    end
    function self.MobUtils.Kill(mob, autoFarm)
        if mob and autoFarm then
            if self.currentMob and self.currentMob.Parent and (self.currentMob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and self.currentMob.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                mob = self.currentMob
            else
                self.currentMob = mob
            end
            if not mob or (not mob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') or mob.Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
                self.currentMob = nil
                return
            end
            setCharacterCollision(true)
            if not mob:GetAttribute('Locked') then
                mob:SetAttribute('Locked', mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
            end
            PosMon = mob:GetAttribute('Locked').Position
            task.spawn(self.BringEnemy)
            EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
            local tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
            if not tool then return end
            local toolTip = tool.ToolTip
            local name = tool.Name
            local offset = CFrame.new(0, 35, 0)
            if toolTip == 'Blox Fruit' then
                if name:find('Light') or name:find('Ice') then
                    offset = CFrame.new(0, 25, 0)
                else
                    offset = CFrame.new(0, 20, 0)
                end
            end
            local targetCFrame = mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * offset * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0)
            _tp(targetCFrame)
            local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            if humanoidRootPart then
                humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                humanoidRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                humanoidRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            if RandomCFrame then
                task.wait(0.5)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 25, 25))
                task.wait(0.5)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 25, 0))
                task.wait(0.5)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 25, 0))
                task.wait(0.5)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 25, -25))
            end
        end
    end
    function self.MobUtils.Kill2(mob, autoFarm)
        if mob and autoFarm then
            if not mob:GetAttribute('Locked') then
                mob:SetAttribute('Locked', mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
            end
            PosMon = mob:GetAttribute('Locked').Position
            self.BringEnemy()
            EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
            if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool').ToolTip == 'Blox Fruit' then
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0))
            else
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 8) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
            end
            if RandomCFrame then
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
            end
        end
    end
    _G.SeaSafeMode = false
    function self.MobUtils.KillSea(mob, autoFarm)
        if mob and autoFarm then
            if not mob:GetAttribute('Locked') then
                mob:SetAttribute('Locked', mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
            end
            PosMon = mob:GetAttribute('Locked').Position
            self.BringEnemy()
            EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
            local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
            local characterHumanoid = character and character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
            if not characterHumanoid then return end
            if characterHumanoid.Health <= 2500 then
                _G.SeaSafeMode = true
            end
            if characterHumanoid.Health >= 8000 then
                _G.SeaSafeMode = false
            end
            local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
            if tool then
                local toolTip = tool.ToolTip
                if _G.SeaSafeMode then
                    _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 400, 0))
                elseif toolTip == 'Blox Fruit' then
                    _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 0))
                else
                    _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 40, 0))
                end
            end
        end
    end
    function self.MobUtils.Sword(mob, autoFarm)
        if mob and autoFarm then
            if not mob:GetAttribute('Locked') then
                mob:SetAttribute('Locked', mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
            end
            PosMon = mob:GetAttribute('Locked').Position
            self.BringEnemy()
            weaponSc('Sword')
            _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
            if RandomCFrame then
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
                task.wait(0.1)
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
            end
        end
    end
    _G.FruitSkills = { Z = false, X = false, C = false, V = false, F = false }
    function UseFruitSkills()
        weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
        if _G.FruitSkills.Z then Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'Z') end
        if _G.FruitSkills.X then Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'X') end
        if _G.FruitSkills.C then Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'C') end
        if _G.FruitSkills.V then Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'V') end
        if _G.FruitSkills.F then
            vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'F', false, game)
            vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'F', false, game)
        end
    end
    function self.MobUtils.Mas(mob, autoFarm)
        if mob and autoFarm then
            if not mob:GetAttribute('Locked') then
                mob:SetAttribute('Locked', mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
            end
            PosMon = mob:GetAttribute('Locked').Position
            self.BringEnemy()
            if mob.Humanoid.Health <= HealthM then
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
                UseFruitSkills()
            else
                weaponSc('Melee')
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
            end
        end
    end
    function self.MobUtils.Masgun(mob, autoFarm)
        if mob and autoFarm then
            if not mob:GetAttribute('Locked') then
                mob:SetAttribute('Locked', mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
            end
            PosMon = mob:GetAttribute('Locked').Position
            self.BringEnemy()
            if mob.Humanoid.Health > HealthM then
                weaponSc('Melee')
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
            else
                _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 35, 8))
                Useskills('Gun', 'Z')
                Useskills('Gun', 'X')
            end
        end
    end
    function statsSetings(stat, points)
        if stat == 'Melee' then
            if self.localPlayer3.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Melee', points)
            end
        elseif stat == 'Defense' then
            if self.localPlayer3.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Defense', points)
            end
        elseif stat == 'Sword' then
            if self.localPlayer3.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Sword', points)
            end
        elseif stat == 'Gun' then
            if self.localPlayer3.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Gun', points)
            end
        elseif stat == 'Devil' and self.localPlayer3.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
            replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', 'Demon Fruit', points)
        end
    end
    self.broughtMobs = {}
    self.bringRange = 225
    self.players2 = game:GetService('Players')
    self.tweenService2 = game:GetService('TweenService')
    self.localPlayer4 = self.players2.LocalPlayer
    self.bossList = {
        'Gorilla King', 'Bobby', 'Yeti', 'Mob Leader', 'Vice Admiral',
        'Warden', 'Chief Warden', 'Swan', 'Magma Admiral', 'Fishman Lord',
        'Wysper', 'Thunder God', 'Cyborg', 'Saber Expert', 'Greybeard',
        'Diamond', 'Jeremy', 'Fajita', 'Don Swan', 'Smoke Admiral',
        'Cursed Captain', 'Darkbeard', 'Order', 'Awakened Ice Admiral',
        'Tide Keeper', 'Stone', 'Island Empress', 'Kilo Admiral',
        'Captain Elephant', 'Beautiful Pirate', 'Longma', 'Cake Queen',
        'Soul Reaper', 'rip_indra', 'Cake Prince', 'Dough King',
        'Tyrant Of The Skies'
    }
    function self:isBoss(mob)
        for _, name in ipairs(self.bossList) do
            if mob.Name == name then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    function self:BringEnemy()
        if _B then
            if PosMon then
                local humanoidRootPart = self.localPlayer4.Character and self.localPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if humanoidRootPart then
                    local mobsToBring = {}
                    for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if #mobsToBring < 6 then
                            if not self:isBoss(enemy) then
                                local humanoid = enemy:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
                                local primaryPart = enemy.PrimaryPart or enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                                if humanoid and (primaryPart and (humanoid.Health > 0 and (not self.broughtMobs[enemy] and (primaryPart.Position - PosMon).Magnitude <= self.bringRange))) then
                                    self.broughtMobs[enemy] = true
                                    table.insert(mobsToBring, {
                                        mob = enemy,
                                        hum = humanoid,
                                        pp = primaryPart
                                    })
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if #mobsToBring > 1 then
                        pcall(function()
                            sethiddenproperty(self.localPlayer4, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
                        end)
                        for _, mobData in ipairs(mobsToBring) do
                            task.spawn(function()
                                local mob = mobData.mob
                                local hum = mobData.hum
                                local pp = mobData.pp
                                pp.CanCollide = false
                                hum.WalkSpeed = 0
                                hum.JumpPower = 0
                                hum.AutoRotate = false
                                if hum:FindFirstChild('Animator') then
                                    hum.Animator:Destroy()
                                end
                                local distance = (pp.Position - PosMon).Magnitude
                                local duration = math.max(0.05, distance / 300)
                                self.tweenService2:Create(pp, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                                    CFrame = CFrame.new(PosMon)
                                }):Play()
                                while _B and (hum.Health > 0 and mob.Parent) do
                                    pp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    pp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    pp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                    pp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    pp.CFrame = CFrame.new(PosMon)
                                    task.wait()
                                end
                                self.broughtMobs[mob] = nil
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                else
                    return
                end
            else
                return
            end
        else
            return
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            while _B do
                self:BringEnemy()
                task.wait(1)
            end
            task.wait(10)
        end
    end)
    function Useskills(weaponType, skillKey)
        if weaponType == 'Melee' then
            weaponSc('Melee')
            if skillKey == 'Z' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
            elseif skillKey == 'X' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
            elseif skillKey == 'C' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'C', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'C', false, game)
            end
        elseif weaponType == 'Sword' then
            weaponSc('Sword')
            if skillKey == 'Z' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
            elseif skillKey == 'X' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
            end
        elseif weaponType == 'Blox Fruit' then
            weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
            if skillKey == 'Z' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
            elseif skillKey == 'X' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
            elseif skillKey == 'C' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'C', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'C', false, game)
            elseif skillKey == 'V' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'V', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'V', false, game)
            end
        elseif weaponType == 'Gun' then
            weaponSc('Gun')
            if skillKey == 'Z' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
            elseif skillKey == 'X' then
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
            end
        end
        if weaponType == 'nil' and skillKey == 'Y' then
            vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Y', false, game)
            vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Y', false, game)
        end
    end
    if getrawmetatable and (setreadonly and getnamecallmethod) then
        local oldMeta = getrawmetatable(game)
        local namecall = oldMeta.__namecall
        setreadonly(oldMeta, false)
        oldMeta.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            local args = { ... }
            if method == 'FireServer' and (tostring(args[1]) == 'RemoteEvent' and (args[2] ~= 'true' and args[2] ~= 'false')) and (_G.FarmMastery_G and not SoulGuitar or (_G.FarmMastery_Dev or (_G.FarmBlazeEM or (_G.Prehis_Skills or (_G.SeaBeast1 or (_G.FishBoat or (_G.PGB or (_G.Leviathan1 or (_G.Complete_Trials or _G.AimMethod and ABmethod == 'AimBots Skill'))))))) or _G.AimMethod and ABmethod == 'Auto Aimbots')) then
                args[2] = MousePos
                return namecall(unpack(args))
            else
                if method == 'InvokeServer' and (tostring(args[2]) == 'StoreFruit' and (_G.WH_StoreFruit and (_G.WebhookURL and _G.WebhookURL ~= ''))) then
                    local fruitName = args[3]
                    local fruitDisplayName = type(fruitName) == 'string' and fruitName and fruitName or (type(fruitName) == 'Instance' and fruitName.Name or 'Unknown')
                    task.spawn(function()
                        if SendStoreFruitWebhook then
                            local fruitDisplayName2 = fruitDisplayName
                            if type(fruitDisplayName) == 'string' and string.find(fruitDisplayName, '-') then
                                fruitDisplayName2 = string.split(fruitDisplayName, '-')[1]
                            end
                            local rarity = _G.FruitRarity and _G.FruitRarity[fruitDisplayName2] or 'Unknown'
                            if not _G.WH_StoreFruitRarities or _G.WH_StoreFruitRarities[rarity] then
                                SendStoreFruitWebhook(fruitDisplayName)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
                return namecall(...)
            end
        end)
    end
    function GetConnectionEnemies(name)
        for _, obj in pairs(replicated:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA('Model') and (typeof(name) == 'table' and table.find(name, obj.Name) or obj.Name == name) and (obj:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and obj.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                return obj
            end
        end
        for _, obj in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA('Model') and (typeof(name) == 'table' and table.find(name, obj.Name) or obj.Name == name) and (obj:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and obj.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                return obj
            end
        end
    end
    function LowCpu()
        local terrain = workspace.Terrain
        terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
        terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
        terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
        terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
        self.lighting3.GlobalShadows = false
        self.lighting3.FogEnd = 9000000000
        self.lighting3.Brightness = 1
        settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = 'Level01'
        for _, obj in pairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA('Part') or (obj:IsA('Union') or (obj:IsA('CornerWedgePart') or obj:IsA('TrussPart'))) then
                obj.Material = 'Plastic'
                obj.Reflectance = 0
            elseif obj:IsA('Decal') or obj:IsA('Texture') then
                obj.Transparency = 1
            elseif obj:IsA('ParticleEmitter') or obj:IsA('Trail') then
                obj.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
            elseif obj:IsA('Explosion') then
                obj.BlastPressure = 1
                obj.BlastRadius = 1
            elseif obj:IsA('Fire') or (obj:IsA('SpotLight') or (obj:IsA('Smoke') or obj:IsA('Sparkles'))) then
                obj.Enabled = false
            elseif obj:IsA('MeshPart') then
                obj.Material = 'Plastic'
                obj.Reflectance = 0
                obj.TextureID = 1.0385902758728956e16
            end
        end
        for _, effect in pairs(self.lighting3:GetChildren()) do
            if effect:IsA('BlurEffect') or (effect:IsA('SunRaysEffect') or (effect:IsA('ColorCorrectionEffect') or (effect:IsA('BloomEffect') or effect:IsA('DepthOfFieldEffect')))) then
                effect.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end

    _G.AttemptedFruits = {}
    function UpdStFruit()
        local localPlayer5 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local backpack = localPlayer5.Backpack
        local character = localPlayer5.Character
        local currentFruits = {}
        local function processContainer(container)
            for _, item in pairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if item:IsA('Tool') and item:FindFirstChild('EatRemote', true) then
                    currentFruits[item] = true
                    if not _G.AttemptedFruits[item] then
                        _G.AttemptedFruits[item] = true
                        if container == character then
                            character.Humanoid:UnequipTools()
                            task.wait(0.1)
                        end
                        pcall(function()
                            local originalName = item:GetAttribute('OriginalName') or item.Name
                            game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('StoreFruit', originalName, item)
                            if _G.WH_StoreFruit and (_G.WebhookURL and _G.WebhookURL ~= '') then
                                task.spawn(function()
                                    if SendStoreFruitWebhook then
                                        local originalName2 = originalName
                                        if type(originalName) == 'string' and string.find(originalName, '-') then
                                            originalName2 = string.split(originalName, '-')[1]
                                        end
                                        local rarity = _G.FruitRarity and _G.FruitRarity[originalName2] or 'Unknown'
                                        if not _G.WH_StoreFruitRarities or _G.WH_StoreFruitRarities[rarity] then
                                            SendStoreFruitWebhook(originalName)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        processContainer(character)
        processContainer(backpack)
        for fruit, _ in pairs(_G.AttemptedFruits) do
            if not currentFruits[fruit] then
                _G.AttemptedFruits[fruit] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    function collectFruits(enabled)
        if enabled then
            local character = self.localPlayer3.Character
            for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if string.find(obj.Name, 'Fruit') then
                    obj.Handle.CFrame = character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function DropFruits()
        for _, tool in pairs(self.localPlayer3.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if string.find(tool.Name, 'Fruit') then
                EquipWeapon(tool.Name)
                task.wait(0.1)
                if self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
                    self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
                end
                EquipWeapon(tool.Name)
                self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild(tool.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer('Drop')
            end
        end
        for _, tool in pairs(self.localPlayer3.Character:GetChildren()) do
            if string.find(tool.Name, 'Fruit') then
                EquipWeapon(tool.Name)
                task.wait(0.1)
                if self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
                    self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
                end
                EquipWeapon(tool.Name)
                self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild(tool.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer('Drop')
            end
        end
    end

        return self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild(itemName) or self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild(itemName)
    end
    function GetIn(itemName)
        local inventory = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')
        for _, item in pairs(inventory) do
            if type(item) == 'table' and (item.Name == itemName or (self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild(itemName) or self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild(itemName))) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    function GetM(materialName)
        local inventory = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')
        for _, item in pairs(inventory) do
            if type(item) == 'table' and (item.Type == 'Material' and item.Name == materialName) then
                return item.Count
            end
        end
        return 0
    end

        local inventory = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')
        for _, item in pairs(inventory) do
            if type(item) == 'table' and item.Type == 'Sword' and (item.Name == weaponName or (self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild(weaponName) or self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild(weaponName))) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    function getInfinity_Ability(ability, enabled)
        if Root then
            if ability == 'Soru' and enabled then
                for _, func in pairs(getgc()) do
                    if self.localPlayer3.Character.Soru and (typeof(func) == 'function' and getfenv(func).script == self.localPlayer3.Character.Soru) then
                        for _, upvalue in pairs(getupvalues(func)) do
                            if typeof(upvalue) == 'table' then
                                repeat
                                    task.wait(Sec)
                                    upvalue.LastUse = 0
                                until not enabled or self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif ability == 'Energy' and enabled then
                self.localPlayer3.Character.Energy.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if enabled then
                        self.localPlayer3.Character.Energy.Value = Energy
                    end
                end)
            elseif ability == 'Observation' and enabled then
                self.localPlayer3.VisionRadius.Value = math.huge
            end
        end
    end
    function Hop()
        pcall(function()
            for i = math.random(1, math.random(40, 75)), 100 do
                local servers = replicated.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(i)
                for _, server in pairs(servers) do
                    if tonumber(server.Count) < 12 then
                        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, server)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    self.TeleportUtils = {}
    getgenv().UseUpY = false
    function self.TeleportUtils.GetBypassCFrame(targetCFrame)
        local closestSpawn = nil
        local closestDist = math.huge
        local localPlayer5 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        if localPlayer5.Character and localPlayer5.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
            local position = localPlayer5.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
            for _, spawn in pairs(workspace._WorldOrigin.PlayerSpawns.Pirates:GetChildren()) do
                local distToTarget = (spawn.Part.Position - targetCFrame.Position).Magnitude
                local distToPlayer = (spawn.Part.Position - position).Magnitude
                if (targetCFrame.Position - position).Magnitude >= 3000 and distToPlayer <= 10000 then
                    if distToTarget <= closestDist then
                        closestSpawn = spawn
                        closestDist = distToTarget
                    end
                end
            end
            return closestSpawn
        end
    end
    function self.TeleportUtils.CanBypassTeleport(targetCFrame)
        if not _G.Bypass or _G.SailBoats ~= nil and _G.SailBoats ~= false then
            return false
        end
        local localPlayer5 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        return (targetCFrame.Position - localPlayer5.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 3500
    end
    function self.TeleportUtils.BypassTP(targetCFrame)
        local localPlayer5 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local spawn = localPlayer5 and localPlayer5.Character and (localPlayer5.Character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (localPlayer5.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (self.TeleportUtils.CanBypassTeleport(targetCFrame) and self.TeleportUtils.GetBypassCFrame(targetCFrame))))
        if not spawn then
            return false
        end
        localPlayer5.Character.LastSpawnPoint.Disabled = true
        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('SetLastSpawnPoint', spawn.Name)
        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('SetSpawnPoint')
        localPlayer5.Character:PivotTo(spawn.Part.CFrame)
        localPlayer5.Character.Humanoid:ChangeState(15)
        repeat
            task.wait()
        until localPlayer5.Character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and localPlayer5.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0
        return true
    end
    function CheckNearestTeleporter(targetCFrame)
        local position = targetCFrame.Position
        local closestTeleporter = nil
        local closestDist = math.huge
        local teleportPoints = {}
        if World3 then
            teleportPoints = { Vector3.new(-5096.27, 314.74, -3142.8), Vector3.new(-12471.17, 375.14, -7551.68), Vector3.new(5643.49, 1014.52, -341.89) }
        elseif World2 then
            teleportPoints = {
                Vector3.new(2285, 15, 905),
                Vector3.new(923, 126, 32852),
                Vector3.new(-6508.55, 83.44, -132.84),
                Vector3.new(-286.99, 306.35, 597.79),
                Vector3.new(3028.59, 2281.08, -7324.82),
                Vector3.new(28286.36, 14896.73, 102.62)
            }
        else
            teleportPoints = World1 and {
                Vector3.new(-4652, 873, -1754),
                Vector3.new(-7895, 5547, -380),
                Vector3.new(61164, 5, 1820),
                Vector3.new(3865, 5, -1926)
            } or {}
        end
        for _, point in ipairs(teleportPoints) do
            local dist = (point - position).Magnitude
            if dist < closestDist then
                closestTeleporter = point
                closestDist = dist
            end
        end
        local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if humanoidRootPart and (closestTeleporter and closestDist <= (position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude) then
            return closestTeleporter
        end
    end
    function requestEntrance(position)
        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', position)
        local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if humanoidRootPart then
            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 50, 0)
        end
        task.wait(0.35)
    end
    function BTP(targetCFrame)
        local localPlayer5 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer5.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        local humanoid = localPlayer5.Character.Humanoid
        local main = localPlayer5.PlayerGui.Main
        local position = targetCFrame.Position
        local lastPosition = humanoidRootPart.Position
        while true do
            humanoid.Health = 0
            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
            main.Quest.Visible = false
            if (humanoidRootPart.Position - lastPosition).Magnitude > 1 then
                lastPosition = humanoidRootPart.Position
                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
            if (targetCFrame.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 2000 then
                return
            end
        end
    end
    getgenv().OnFarm = false
    getgenv().currentTween = nil
    getgenv().currentTarget = nil
    local runService = game:GetService('RunService')
    local tweenService3 = game:GetService('TweenService')
    local players3 = game:GetService('Players')
    function _tp(targetCFrame)
        local localPlayer5 = players3.LocalPlayer
        local character = localPlayer5.Character
        if not (character and character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')) then
            return
        end
        local humanoidRootPart = character.HumanoidRootPart
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
        local targetPart = humanoidRootPart
        local isBoatMode = _G.SailBoats ~= nil and _G.SailBoats ~= false or _G.FindMirage or (_G.AutofindKitIs or _G.Prehis_Find)
        if humanoid and (humanoid.Sit and not isBoatMode) then
            humanoid.Jump = true
            humanoid.Sit = false
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
            for _, boat in pairs(Workspace.Boats:GetChildren()) do
                if boat:FindFirstChild('Owner') and tostring(boat.Owner.Value) == tostring(localPlayer5.Name) then
                    targetPart = boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat') or (boat:FindFirstChild('Engine') or boat.PrimaryPart)
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if targetPart then
            local distance = (targetCFrame.Position - targetPart.Position).Magnitude
            local tweenPart = targetPart
            if _G.Bypass and (3500 < distance and not isBoatMode) and self.TeleportUtils.BypassTP(targetCFrame) then
                task.wait(0.1)
                local newRoot = localPlayer5.Character and localPlayer5.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if newRoot and not (humanoid and humanoid.Sit) then
                    humanoidRootPart = newRoot
                    tweenPart = newRoot
                else
                    tweenPart = targetPart
                end
            else
                tweenPart = targetPart
            end
            if getgenv().UsePortals and not (humanoid and humanoid.Sit) then
                local portal = CheckNearestTeleporter(targetCFrame)
                if portal then
                    requestEntrance(portal)
                    local newRoot = localPlayer5.Character and localPlayer5.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    tweenPart = newRoot or tweenPart
                end
            end
            getgenv().OnFarm = true
            shouldTween = true
            if tweenPart == humanoidRootPart and not humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('NightMysticTweenVelocity') then
                local bodyVelocity = Instance.new('BodyVelocity')
                bodyVelocity.Name = 'NightMysticTweenVelocity'
                bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9000000000, 9000000000, 9000000000)
                bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                bodyVelocity.Parent = humanoidRootPart
            end
            if humanoid and not (isBoatMode or humanoid.Sit) then
                humanoid.Sit = false
            end
            if tweenPart == humanoidRootPart then
                humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            local distToTarget = (targetCFrame.Position - tweenPart.Position).Magnitude
            local distFlat = (Vector3.new(targetCFrame.X, 0, targetCFrame.Z) - Vector3.new(tweenPart.Position.X, 0, tweenPart.Position.Z)).Magnitude
            if distToTarget <= 15 then
                if getgenv().currentTween then
                    getgenv().currentTween:Cancel()
                    getgenv().currentTween = nil
                    getgenv().currentTarget = nil
                end
                if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
                    local direction = (targetCFrame.Position - tweenPart.Position).Unit
                    local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z).Unit
                    tweenPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetCFrame.Position, targetCFrame.Position + flatDir)
                else
                    tweenPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
                end
                return
            else
                if not (humanoid and humanoid.Sit) and (not isBoatMode and getgenv().UseUpY) then
                    if distFlat >= 100 then
                        tweenPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(tweenPart.CFrame.X, targetCFrame.Y + 120, tweenPart.CFrame.Z)
                    end
                end
                local targetPos = CFrame.new(targetCFrame.X, targetCFrame.Y + 120, targetCFrame.Z)
                local distToTargetPos = (targetPos.Position - tweenPart.Position).Magnitude
                if not getgenv().currentTween or (not getgenv().currentTarget or ((getgenv().currentTarget.Position - targetPos.Position).Magnitude >= 15 or getgenv().currentTween.PlaybackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Playing)) then
                    local threshold = (humanoid and humanoid.Sit and 5 or 15)
                    if threshold < distToTargetPos then
                        local speed = humanoid and humanoid.Sit and (_G.SetSpeedBoat or 300) or (distToTargetPos <= 1000 and (getgenv().TweenSpeedNear or 300) or (getgenv().TweenSpeedFar or 350))
                        local duration = distToTargetPos / speed
                        if getgenv().currentTween then
                            getgenv().currentTween:Cancel()
                        end
                        local endCFrame
                        if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
                            local pos = targetPos.Position
                            local direction = (pos - tweenPart.Position).Unit
                            local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z).Unit
                            endCFrame = CFrame.new(pos, pos + flatDir)
                        else
                            endCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.Position)
                        end
                        local tween = tweenService3:Create(tweenPart, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                            CFrame = endCFrame
                        })
                        getgenv().currentTween = tween
                        tween:Play()
                        task.spawn(function()
                            while getgenv().currentTween == tween and tween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing do
                                if not (getgenv().OnFarm and shouldTween) then
                                    tween:Cancel()
                                    getgenv().currentTween = nil
                                    getgenv().currentTarget = nil
                                    break
                                end
                                local currentDist = (targetCFrame.Position - tweenPart.Position).Magnitude
                                local currentFlat = (Vector3.new(targetCFrame.X, 0, targetCFrame.Z) - Vector3.new(tweenPart.Position.X, 0, tweenPart.Position.Z)).Magnitude
                                if currentDist <= 15 then
                                    tween:Cancel()
                                    if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
                                        local direction = (targetCFrame.Position - tweenPart.Position).Unit
                                        local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z).Unit
                                        tweenPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetCFrame.Position, targetCFrame.Position + flatDir)
                                    else
                                        tweenPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
                                    end
                                    break
                                end
                                if not (humanoid and humanoid.Sit) and (not isBoatMode and (currentFlat <= 18 and tweenPart.Position.Y > targetCFrame.Y + 50)) then
                                    tween:Cancel()
                                    _tp(targetCFrame)
                                    break
                                end
                                task.wait()
                            end
                        end)
                        local connection = nil
                        connection = tween.Completed:Connect(function()
                            if connection then
                                connection:Disconnect()
                            end
                            if getgenv().currentTween == tween then
                                getgenv().currentTween = nil
                                getgenv().currentTarget = nil
                            end
                        end)
                    else
                        if getgenv().currentTween then
                            getgenv().currentTween:Cancel()
                            getgenv().currentTween = nil
                            getgenv().currentTarget = nil
                        end
                        if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
                            local pos = targetCFrame.Position
                            local direction = (pos - tweenPart.Position).Unit
                            local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z).Unit
                            tweenPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, pos + flatDir)
                        else
                            tweenPart.CFrame = tweenPart.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, 0.6)
                            tweenPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            return
        end
    end
    function TeleportToTarget(targetCFrame)
        _tp(targetCFrame)
    end
    function notween(targetCFrame)
        local humanoidRootPart = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if humanoidRootPart then
            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
        end
    end
    function servhop()
        local serverBrowser = replicated:FindFirstChild('__ServerBrowser')
        if serverBrowser then
            local jobId = game.JobId
            local servers = {}
            for i = 1, 100 do
                local success, result = pcall(function()
                    return serverBrowser:InvokeServer(i)
                end)
                if success and type(result) == 'table' then
                    for id, data in pairs(result) do
                        if id ~= jobId and type(data) == 'table' then
                            local playerCount = tonumber(data.Count) or 0
                            if playerCount >= 1 and playerCount <= 4 then
                                table.insert(servers, {
                                    id = id,
                                    players = playerCount
                                })
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if #servers > 0 then
                table.sort(servers, function(a, b)
                    return a.players < b.players
                end)
                pcall(function()
                    serverBrowser:InvokeServer('teleport', servers[1].id)
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    runService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        local character = players3.LocalPlayer.Character
        if character then
            local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
            if humanoid and humanoid.Sit then
                if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('NightMysticTweenVelocity') then
                    humanoidRootPart.NightMysticTweenVelocity:Destroy()
                end
                return
            end
            if getgenv().OnFarm or shouldTween then
                if humanoidRootPart then
                    humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end
                for _, part in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA('BasePart') then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            else
                if humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild('NightMysticTweenVelocity') then
                    humanoidRootPart.NightMysticTweenVelocity:Destroy()
                end
                if getgenv().currentTween then
                    getgenv().currentTween:Cancel()
                    getgenv().currentTween = nil
                    getgenv().currentTarget = nil
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.1) do
            local isFarming = _G.SailBoat_Hydra or (_G.WardenBoss or _G.AutoFactory or (_G.HighestMirage or _G.HCM)) or (_G.PGB or _G.Leviathan1 or (_G.UPGDrago or _G.Complete_Trials) or (_G.TpDrago_Prehis or _G.BuyDrago or (_G.AutoFireFlowers or _G.DT_Uzoth))) or (_G.AutoBerry or _G.Prehis_Find or (_G.Prehis_Skills or _G.Prehis_DB) or (_G.Prehis_DE or _G.FarmBlazeEM or (_G.Dojoo or _G.CollectPresent)) or (_G.AutoLawKak or _G.TpLab or (_G.AutoPhoenixF or _G.AutoFarmChest) or (_G.AutoHytHallow or _G.LongsWord or (_G.BlackSpikey or _G.AutoHolyTorch))) or (_G.TrainDrago or _G.AutoSaber or (_G.FarmMastery_Dev or _G.CitizenQuest) or (_G.AutoEctoplasm or _G.KeysRen or (_G.Auto_Rainbow_Haki or _G.obsFarm)) or (_G.AutoBigmom or _G.Doughv2 or (_G.AuraBoss or _G.Raiding) or (_G.Auto_Cavender or _G.TpPly or (_G.Bartilo_Quest or _G.Level)))) or (_G.FarmEliteHunt or _G.AutoZou or (_G.AutoFarm_Bone or getgenv().AutoMaterial) or (_G.CraftVM or _G.FrozenTP or (_G.TPDoor or _G.AcientOne)) or (_G.AutoFarmNear or _G.AutoRaidCastle or (_G.DarkBladev3 or _G.AutoFarmRaid) or (_G.Auto_Cake_Prince or _G.Addealer or (_G.TPNpc or _G.TwinHook))) or (_G.FindMirage or _G.FarmChestM or (_G.Shark or _G.TerrorShark) or (_G.Piranha or _G.MobCrew or (_G.SeaBeast1 or _G.FishBoat)) or (_G.Auto or _G.AutoPoleV2 or (_G.Auto_SuperHuman or _G.AutoDeathStep) or (_G.Auto_SharkMan_Karate or _G.Auto_Electric_Claw or (_G.AutoDragonTalon or _G.Auto_Def_DarkCoat))))) or (_G.Auto_God_Human or _G.Auto_Tushita or (_G.AutoMatSoul or _G.AutoKenVTWO) or (_G.AutoSerpentBow or _G.AutoFMon or (_G.Auto_Soul_Guitar or _G.TPGEAR)) or (_G.AutoSaw or _G.AutoTridentW2 or (_G.Auto_StartRaid or _G.AutoEvoRace) or (_G.AutoGetQuestBounty or _G.MarinesCoat or (_G.TravelDres or _G.Defeating))) or (_G.DummyMan or _G.Auto_Yama or (_G.Auto_SwanGG or _G.SwanCoat) or (_G.AutoEcBoss or _G.Auto_Mink or (_G.Auto_Human or _G.Auto_Skypiea)) or (_G.Auto_Fish or _G.CDK_TS or (_G.CDK_YM or _G.CDK) or (_G.AutoFarmGodChalice or _G.AutoFistDarkness or (_G.AutoMiror or _G.Teleport)))) or (_G.AutoKilo or _G.AutoGetUsoap or (_G.Praying or _G.TryLucky) or (_G.AutoColShad or _G.AutoUnHaki or (_G.DangerLV or _G.Relic123) or (_G.tweenKitsune or _G.Collect_Ember or (_G.AutofindKitIs or _G.snaguine)) or (_G.TwFruits or _G.tweenKitShrine or (_G.Tp_LgS or _G.Tp_MasterA) or (_G.tweenShrine or             getgenv().OnFarm = isFarming

function Module:SetupUI()
    local Window = Library:MakeWindow({

        ScriptFolder = "LightWyn"
    })

    local WebhookTab = Window:MakeTab({ Title = "Webhook", Icon = "link" })
    local DragonTab = Window:MakeTab({ Title = "Dragon Quests", Icon = "dragon" })
    local VisualsTab = Window:MakeTab({ Title = "Visuals & ESP", Icon = "eye" })

        end
    })

        Name = "Rejoin Server",
        Callback = function()

            local success, err = pcall(function()
                teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, players4.LocalPlayer)
            end)

            else
                warn('Error:', err)
                pcall(function()
                    teleport(game.PlaceId)
                end)
            end

    StatusTab:AddButton({
        Name = "Hop Server",
        Callback = function()
            local players4 = game:GetService('Players')
            local teleportService = game:GetService('TeleportService')
            local success, err = pcall(function()
                teleportService:Teleport(2753915549, players4.LocalPlayer)
            end)
            if success then
                print('Teleporting...')
            else
                warn('Error:', err)
                pcall(function()
                    teleport(2753915549)
                end)
            end
        end
    })
    StatusTab:AddButton({
        Name = "Server Hop Low Player",
        Callback = function()
            local serverBrowser = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):WaitForChild('__ServerBrowser')
            local jobId = game.JobId
            Window:Notify({
                Title = "Server Hop",
                Content = "Searching server...",
                Duration = 5,
                Image = "rbxassetid://73539136006332"
            })
            local minPlayers = 1
            local maxPlayers = 4
            while task.wait() do
                local servers = {}
                for i = 1, 100 do
                    local success, result = pcall(function()
                        return serverBrowser:InvokeServer(i)
                    end)
                    if success and type(result) == 'table' then
                        for id, data in pairs(result) do
                            if id ~= jobId and type(data) == 'table' then
                                local playerCount = data.Count or 0
                                if minPlayers <= playerCount and playerCount <= maxPlayers then
                                    table.insert(servers, { id = id, players = playerCount })
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
                table.sort(servers, function(a, b) return a.players < b.players end)
                if #servers > 0 then
                    local targetServer = servers[1]
                    Window:Notify({
                        Title = "Server Hop",
                        Content = "Switching server...",
                        Duration = 5,
                        Image = "rbxassetid://73539136006332"
                    })
                    serverBrowser:InvokeServer('teleport', targetServer.id)
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    })
    StatusTab:AddTextBox({
        Name = "Job Id",
        Placeholder = "",
        Default = "",
        Callback = function(value)
            local jobId = value:gsub('`', ''):gsub('discord%.gg/night%-mystic%s*', ''):match('^%s*(.-)%s*$')
            local isEncoded = false
            if jobId ~= '' and not jobId:find('-') then
                local base64 = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/'
                local success, decoded = pcall(function()
                    return jobId:gsub('[^' .. base64 .. '=]', ''):gsub('.', function(c)
                        if c == '=' then return '' end
                        local index = base64:find(c) - 1
                        local bits = ''
                        for i = 6, 1, -1 do
                            if index % 2 ^ i - index % 2 ^ (i - 1) <= 0 then
                                bits = bits .. '0'
                            else
                                bits = bits .. '1'
                            end
                        end
                        return bits
                    end):gsub('%d%d%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?%d?', function(byteBits)
                        if #byteBits ~= 8 then return '' end
                        local byte = 0
                        for i = 1, 8 do
                            if byteBits:sub(i, i) == '1' then
                                byte = byte + 2 ^ (8 - i)
                            end
                        end
                        return string.char(byte)
                    end)
                end)
                if success and (decoded and decoded:find('-')) then
                    jobId = decoded
                    isEncoded = true
                end
            end
            if jobId == '' then
                _G.JobId = nil
            else
                _G.JobId = jobId
                if isEncoded then
                    game.StarterGui:SetCore('SendNotification', {
                        Title = 'LightWyn',
                        Text = 'entering the server',
                        Duration = 3
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    })
    StatusTab:AddButton({
        Name = "Join Server",
        Callback = function()
            replicated.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer('teleport', _G.JobId)
        end
    })
    StatusTab:AddButton({
        Name = "Copy Job Id Server",
        Callback = function()
            setclipboard(tostring(game.JobId))
        end
    })

    StatusTab:AddSection("Server - Status")
    local timeZoneLabel = StatusTab:AddParagraph("Time Zone", "")
    function UpdateOS()
        local date = os.date('*t')
        local hour = date.hour % 24
        local ampm = hour < 12 and 'AM' or 'PM'
        local timeString = string.format('%02i:%02i:%02i %s', (hour - 1) % 12 + 1, date.min, date.sec, ampm)
        local dateString = string.format('%02d/%02d/%04d', date.day, date.month, date.year)
        local localizationService = game:GetService('LocalizationService')
        local localPlayer6 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
        local success, country = pcall(function()
            return localizationService:GetCountryRegionForPlayerAsync(localPlayer6)
        end)
        timeZoneLabel:SetTitle(dateString .. ' - ' .. timeString .. ' [ ' .. (country or '??') .. ' ]')
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(1) do
            UpdateOS()
            game:GetService('RunService').RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end)
    local timeLabel = StatusTab:AddParagraph("Time", "")
    function UpdateTime()
        local gameTime = math.floor(workspace.DistributedGameTime + 0.5)
        timeLabel:SetTitle(math.floor(gameTime / 3600) % 24 .. ' Hour (h) ' .. math.floor(gameTime / 60) % 60 .. ' Minute (m) ' .. gameTime % 60 .. ' Second (s)')
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(1) do
            UpdateTime()
        end
    end)

    local function addStatusNotifier(title, container, childName)
        local label = StatusTab:AddParagraph(title, "Status: ")
        local wasFound = false
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()

                        label:SetTitle("false")

                        Window:Notify({
                            Title = "Night Mystic",
                            Content = title .. " Spawned!",
                            Duration = 7,
                            Image = "rbxassetid://12598594243"
                        })
                    end
                    wasFound = found
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    _G.StartFarm = false
    _G.Level = false
    _G.AutoFarm_Bone = false
    _G.AutoFarm_Cake = false
    _G.AutoTyrant = false
    _G.AcceptQuest = false
    local tweenRange = 15

    MainFarmTab:AddDropdown({
        Name = "Select Farm Mode",
        Options = { "Level", "Bone", "Cake Prince", "Tyrant Of The Skies" },
        Default = self:GetSetting('SelectedFarmMode_Save', 'Level'),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.SelectedFarmMode = value
            _G.SaveData.SelectedFarmMode_Save = value
            self:SaveSettings()
        end
    })

    MainFarmTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Start Farm",
        Default = self:GetSetting('StartFarm_Save', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.StartFarm = value
            _G.Level = false
            _G.AutoFarm_Bone = false
            _G.AutoFarm_Cake = false
            _G.AutoTyrant = false
            if value then
                if _G.SelectedFarmMode ~= 'Level' then
                    if _G.SelectedFarmMode == 'Bone' then
                        _G.AutoFarm_Bone = true
                    elseif _G.SelectedFarmMode == 'Cake Prince' then
                        _G.AutoFarm_Cake = true
                    elseif _G.SelectedFarmMode == 'Tyrant Of The Skies' then
                        _G.AutoTyrant = true
                    end
                else
                    _G.Level = true
                end
            end
            _G.SaveData.StartFarm_Save = value
            self:SaveSettings()
        end
    })

    MainFarmTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Accept Quests",
        Default = self:GetSetting('AcceptQuest_Save', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.AcceptQuest = value
            _G.SaveData.AcceptQuest_Save = value
            self:SaveSettings()
        end
    })

    MainFarmTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Use Skull Guitar Summon Tyrant",
        Default = self:GetSetting('UseSkullSummon_Save', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.UseSkullSummon = value
            _G.SaveData.UseSkullSummon_Save = value
            self:SaveSettings()
        end
    })

    local function getTikiQuest()
        local level = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.Level.Value
        if level >= 2575 then
            return {
                Name = 'Skull Slayer',
                QuestArgs = { 'StartQuest', 'TikiQuest3', 2 },
                QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16665.0879, 105.27478, 1577.61743, -0.999621451, 3.5280582e-8, 0.0275127869, 3.5990368e-8, 1, 2.53032191e-8, -0.0275127869, 2.62838356e-8, -0.999621451),
                FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16709.49, 419.68, 1751.09)
            }
        elseif level > 2550 then
            return {
                Name = 'Serpent Hunter',
                QuestArgs = { 'StartQuest', 'TikiQuest3', 1 },
                QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16665.0879, 105.27478, 1577.61743, -0.999621451, 3.5280582e-8, 0.0275127869, 3.5990368e-8, 1, 2.53032191e-8, -0.0275127869, 2.62838356e-8, -0.999621451),
                FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16645.64, 163.09, 1352.87)
            }
        elseif level >= 2525 then
            return {
                Name = 'Isle Champion',
                QuestArgs = { 'StartQuest', 'TikiQuest2', 2 },
                QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16546.748, 55.7216759, -172.865311, -0.0595058464, 2.45485676e-8, 0.998227954, -5.3272025e-8, 1, -2.77677703e-8, -0.998227954, -5.48299717e-8, -0.0595058464),
                FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16602.1015625, 130.38734436035, 1087.2456054688)
            }
        elseif level >= 2500 then
            return {
                Name = 'Sun-kissed Warrior',
                QuestArgs = { 'StartQuest', 'TikiQuest2', 1 },
                QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16546.748, 55.7216759, -172.865311, -0.0595058464, 2.45485676e-8, 0.998227954, -5.3272025e-8, 1, -2.77677703e-8, -0.998227954, -5.48299717e-8, -0.0595058464),
                FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16347, 64, 984)
            }
        elseif level >= 2475 then
            return {
                Name = 'Island Boy',
                QuestArgs = { 'StartQuest', 'TikiQuest1', 2 },
                QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16546.748, 55.7216759, -172.865311, -0.0595058464, 2.45485676e-8, 0.998227954, -5.3272025e-8, 1, -2.77677703e-8, -0.998227954, -5.48299717e-8, -0.0595058464),
                FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16670, 43, -270)
            }
        else
            return {
                Name = 'Isle Outlaw',
                QuestArgs = { 'StartQuest', 'TikiQuest1', 1 },
                QuestPos = CFrame.new(-16546.748, 55.7216759, -172.865311, -0.0595058464, 2.45485676e-8, 0.998227954, -5.3272025e-8, 1, -2.77677703e-8, -0.998227954, -5.48299717e-8, -0.0595058464),
                FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16350, 45, -180)
            }
        end
    end

    local function getTikiEyes()
        local tikiModel = workspace:FindFirstChild('Map') and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('TikiOutpost')
        if tikiModel then
            tikiModel = workspace.Map.TikiOutpost:FindFirstChild('IslandModel')
        end
        local count = 0
        if tikiModel then
            local eye1 = tikiModel:FindFirstChild('Eye1')
            local eye2 = tikiModel:FindFirstChild('Eye2')
            local islandChunks = tikiModel:FindFirstChild('IslandChunks')
            local eye3 = islandChunks and islandChunks:FindFirstChild('E')
            if eye3 then
                eye3 = islandChunks.E:FindFirstChild('Eye3')
            end
            local eye4 = islandChunks and islandChunks:FindFirstChild('E')
            if eye4 then
                eye4 = islandChunks.E:FindFirstChild('Eye4')
            end
            if eye1 and eye1.Transparency == 0 then
                count = count + 1
            end
            if eye2 and eye2.Transparency == 0 then
                count = count + 1
            end
            if eye3 and eye3.Transparency == 0 then
                count = count + 1
            end
            if eye4 and eye4.Transparency == 0 then
                count = count + 1
            end
        end
        return count
    end

    local function attackWithAllSkills(targetCFrame)
        local virtualInputManager = game:GetService('VirtualInputManager')
        local virtualUser = game:GetService('VirtualUser')
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 'RightControl', false, game)
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, -1, 0))
        end)
        local weaponTypes = { 'Melee', 'Blox Fruit', 'Sword', 'Gun' }
        local skillKeys = { 'Z', 'X', 'C' }
        for _, weaponType in ipairs(weaponTypes) do
            local tool = nil
            for _, item in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                if item:IsA('Tool') and item.ToolTip == weaponType then
                    tool = item
                    break
                end
            end
            if not tool then
                tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
                if tool then
                    if tool.ToolTip ~= weaponType then
                        tool = nil
                    end
                end
            end
            if tool then
                game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
                task.wait(0.1)
                for i = 1, 3 do
                    if targetCFrame and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
                    end
                    virtualUser:CaptureController()
                    virtualUser:ClickButton1(Vector2.new(851, 158))
                    local skill = skillKeys[i]
                    if skill then
                        virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, skill, false, game)
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, skill, false, game)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local positionIndex = 1
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if not (_G.AutoTyrant and _G.StartFarm) then
                    return
                end
                local localPlayer6 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                local character = localPlayer6.Character or localPlayer6.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                if not humanoidRootPart or (not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0) then
                    return
                end
                local tyrant = workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild('Tyrant of the Skies')
                local eyes = getTikiEyes()
                if tyrant and (tyrant:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (tyrant.Humanoid.Health > 0 and tyrant:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart'))) then
                    while true do
                        task.wait()
                        if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                            self.MobUtils.Kill(tyrant, _G.AutoTyrant)
                        end
                        if not _G.StartFarm or (not _G.AutoTyrant or (not tyrant.Parent or tyrant:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and tyrant.Humanoid.Health <= 0)) or (not humanoidRootPart.Parent or humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0) then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    return
                end
                if eyes == 4 then
                    local positions = {
                        CFrame.new(-16335.1, 158.1, 1465.6),
                        CFrame.new(-16288.6, 158.1, 1470.3),
                        CFrame.new(-16258, 156.7, 1461.4),
                        CFrame.new(-16212.4, 158.1, 1466.3),
                        CFrame.new(-16335, 159.3, 1324.8),
                        CFrame.new(-16286, 155.9, 1323.8),
                        CFrame.new(-16250.3, 159.3, 1316.3)
                    }
                    local targetPos = positions[positionIndex]
                    if not targetPos then
                        return
                    end
                    if (humanoidRootPart.Position - targetPos.Position).Magnitude < 10 then
                        humanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetPos
                        local elapsed = 0
                        if _G.UseSkullSummon then
                            local skullGuitar = character:FindFirstChild('Skull Guitar')
                            if not skullGuitar then
                                skullGuitar = localPlayer6.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Skull Guitar')
                                if skullGuitar then
                                    humanoid:EquipTool(skullGuitar)
                                else
                                    game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('LoadItem', 'Skull Guitar')
                                end
                            end
                            if skullGuitar and (skullGuitar.Parent == character and skullGuitar:FindFirstChild('RemoteEvent')) then
                                skullGuitar.RemoteEvent:FireServer('TAP', targetPos.Position)
                                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.Validator2:FireServer(8359618, 0)
                                elapsed = 0
                            else
                                elapsed = 0
                            end
                        else
                            attackWithAllSkills(targetPos)
                            elapsed = 0
                        end
                        while true do
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            if character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                                character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetPos
                            end
                            elapsed = elapsed + 0.1
                            if elapsed >= 1.5 then
                                positionIndex = positionIndex + 1
                                if positionIndex > #positions then
                                    positionIndex = 1
                                end
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        return
                    end
                    if _tp then
                        _tp(targetPos)
                    end
                    return
                end
                local quest = getTikiQuest()
                local questVisible = localPlayer6.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible
                if _G.AcceptQuest and not questVisible then
                    if (humanoidRootPart.Position - quest.QuestPos.Position).Magnitude <= 5 then
                        humanoidRootPart.CFrame = quest.QuestPos
                        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(unpack(quest.QuestArgs))
                        task.wait(0.3)
                    elseif _tp then
                        _tp(quest.QuestPos)
                    end
                    return
                end
                local targetMob = nil
                local closestDist = math.huge
                local mobNames = {
                    'Skull Slayer', 'Serpent Hunter', 'Isle Champion',
                    'Sun-kissed Warrior', 'Island Boy', 'Isle Outlaw'
                }
                if workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies') then
                    for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if table.find(mobNames, enemy.Name) and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 and enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart'))) then
                            local dist = (humanoidRootPart.Position - enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < closestDist then
                                targetMob = enemy
                                closestDist = dist
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if targetMob then
                    while true do
                        task.wait()
                        if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                            self.MobUtils.Kill(targetMob, _G.AutoTyrant)
                        end
                        if not _G.StartFarm or (not _G.AutoTyrant or (not targetMob.Parent or targetMob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and targetMob.Humanoid.Health <= 0)) or (not humanoidRootPart.Parent or humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0) then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    return
                else
                    if _tp then
                        _tp(quest.FarmPos)
                    end
                    return
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoFarm_Bone and _G.StartFarm then
                pcall(function()
                    local localPlayer6 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer6.Character and localPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if humanoidRootPart then
                        local quest = localPlayer6.PlayerGui.Main.Quest
                        local mobs = {
                            'Reborn Skeleton', 'Living Zombie', 'Demonic Soul', 'Posessed Mummy'
                        }
                        local questPos = CFrame.new(-9516.99, 172.01, 6078.46)
                        if _G.AcceptQuest and not quest.Visible then
                            if (questPos.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                _tp(questPos)
                            else
                                task.wait(0.3)
                                local quests = {
                                    { 'StartQuest', 'HauntedQuest1', 1 },
                                    { 'StartQuest', 'HauntedQuest1', 2 },
                                    { 'StartQuest', 'HauntedQuest2', 1 },
                                    { 'StartQuest', 'HauntedQuest2', 2 }
                                }
                                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(unpack(quests[math.random(1, #quests)]))
                                task.wait(0.5)
                            end
                        else
                            local function getClosestBoneMob()
                                local closest = nil
                                local closestDist = math.huge
                                for _, mobName in pairs(mobs) do
                                    for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                        if enemy.Name == mobName and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                                            local dist = (enemy.PrimaryPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                            if dist < closestDist then
                                                closest = enemy
                                                closestDist = dist
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                return closest
                            end
                            local mob = getClosestBoneMob()
                            if mob then
                                _tp(mob.PrimaryPart.CFrame)
                                EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
                                self.MobUtils.Kill(mob, _G.AutoFarm_Bone)
                            else
                                _tp(CFrame.new(-9495.68, 453.58, 5977.34))
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    if not getgenv().DualQuestNext then
        getgenv().DualQuestNext = {}
    end
