            PosM = CFrame.new(11019.1318, -2146.06812, 9342.3916)
        },
        SubmergedQuest2 = {
            Mon = 'Sea Chanter',
            Qdata = 1,
            NameMon = 'Sea Chanter',
            PosM = CFrame.new(10671.2715, -2057.59155, 10047.2588)
        },
        SubmergedQuest3 = {
            Mon = 'High Disciple',
            Qdata = 1,
            NameMon = 'High Disciple',
            PosM = CFrame.new(9750.41602, -1966.93884, 9753.36035)
        }
    }

    local lastQuestVisible = false
    local lastQuestName = ''
    local currentQuestTarget = nil
    local currentQuestPos = nil

    local function getClosestMob(mobName)
        local localPlayer6 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer6.Character and localPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if not humanoidRootPart then
            return nil
        end
        local closestMob = nil
        local closestDist = math.huge
        for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
            if self.MobUtils.Alive(enemy) and (enemy.Name == mobName and enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')) then
                local dist = (enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < closestDist then
                    closestMob = enemy
                    closestDist = dist
                end
            end
        end
        return closestMob
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.Level and _G.StartFarm then
                pcall(function()
                    local localPlayer6 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer6.Character and localPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if not humanoidRootPart then
                        return
                    end
                    local questInfo = QuestNeta()
                    local questType = questInfo[3]
                    local questVisible = localPlayer6.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible
                    local quest = questData[questType]

                    if not getgenv().DualQuestNext[questType] then
                        getgenv().DualQuestNext[questType] = 1
                    end

                    if quest and (lastQuestVisible and (not questVisible and lastQuestName == questType)) then
                        getgenv().DualQuestNext[questType] = getgenv().DualQuestNext[questType] == 1 and 2 or 1
                    end

                    lastQuestVisible = questVisible
                    lastQuestName = questType

                    local questIndex = getgenv().DualQuestNext[questType]
                    local qdata = questInfo[2]
                    local mon = questInfo[1]
                    local posM = questInfo[4]

                    if quest and questIndex == 2 then
                        qdata = quest.Qdata
                        mon = quest.Mon
                        posM = quest.PosM
                    end

                    if not questVisible then
                        local questPos = questInfo[6] * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)
                        if humanoidRootPart and questPos then
                            if (humanoidRootPart.Position - questPos.Position).Magnitude > 100 then
                                _tp(questPos)
                            else
                                task.wait(1)
                                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('StartQuest', questType, qdata)
                            end
                        end
                        return
                    end

                    local mob = getClosestMob(mon)
                    if not mob then
                        if currentQuestPos then
                            _tp(currentQuestPos)
                        else
                            _tp(posM)
                        end
                        return
                    end

                    currentQuestTarget = mob
                    while true do
                        task.wait()
                        if currentQuestTarget and currentQuestTarget:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                            local pos = currentQuestTarget.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                            currentQuestPos = CFrame.new(pos.X, pos.Y + 25, pos.Z)
                            _tp(currentQuestPos)
                        end
                        self.MobUtils.Kill(currentQuestTarget, _G.Level)
                        if not _G.StartFarm or (not _G.Level or (not localPlayer6.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible or (not currentQuestTarget.Parent or currentQuestTarget.Humanoid.Health <= 0))) then
                            currentQuestTarget = nil
                            return
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    local cakeTargetPos = nil
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoFarm_Cake and _G.StartFarm then
                pcall(function()
                    local localPlayer6 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer6.Character and localPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if not humanoidRootPart then
                        return
                    end
                    local enemies = workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies')
                    if not enemies then
                        return
                    end
                    local cakeSpawn = CFrame.new(-2091.91, 70, -12142.83)
                    local cakeMirror = CFrame.new(-2151.82, 149.32, -12404.91)
                    local cakeLoaf = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('CakeLoaf')
                    if cakeLoaf then
                        cakeLoaf = cakeLoaf:FindFirstChild('BigMirror')
                    end
                    if cakeLoaf then
                        cakeLoaf = cakeLoaf:FindFirstChild('Other')
                    end
                    local mirrorActive = cakeLoaf and cakeLoaf.Transparency == 0

                    local cakePrince = enemies:FindFirstChild('Cake Prince') or enemies:FindFirstChild('Dough King')

                    if not cakePrince and (not mirrorActive and (humanoidRootPart.Position - cakeSpawn.Position).Magnitude > 3000) then
                        _tp(cakeSpawn)
                        return
                    end

                    if cakePrince or mirrorActive then
                        cakeTargetPos = nil
                        if cakePrince and (cakePrince:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (cakePrince.Humanoid.Health > 0 and cakePrince.PrimaryPart)) then
                            local targetPos = cakePrince.PrimaryPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 25, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
                            _tp(targetPos)
                            self.MobUtils.Kill(cakePrince, _G.AutoFarm_Cake)
                        else
                            if humanoidRootPart and cakeMirror then
                                if (humanoidRootPart.Position - cakeMirror.Position).Magnitude > tweenRange then
                                    _tp(cakeMirror)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    local cakeMobs = {
                        'Cookie Crafter',
                        'Cake Guard',
                        'Baking Staff',
                        'Head Baker'
                    }

                    if _G.AcceptQuest and not localPlayer6.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible then
                        cakeTargetPos = nil
                        local questNpc = CFrame.new(-1927.92, 37.8, -12842.54)
                        if humanoidRootPart and questNpc then
                            if (humanoidRootPart.Position - questNpc.Position).Magnitude > tweenRange then
                                _tp(questNpc)
                            else
                                local quests = {
                                    { 'StartQuest', 'CakeQuest2', 2 },
                                    { 'StartQuest', 'CakeQuest2', 1 },
                                    { 'StartQuest', 'CakeQuest1', 1 },
                                    { 'StartQuest', 'CakeQuest1', 2 }
                                }
                                game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(unpack(quests[math.random(1, 4)]))
                            end
                        end
                        return
                    end

                    local closestMob = nil
                    local closestDist = math.huge
                    for _, enemy in ipairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if table.find(cakeMobs, enemy.Name) and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 and enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart'))) then
                            local dist = (enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < closestDist then
                                closestMob = enemy
                                closestDist = dist
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if closestMob then
                        while true do
                            task.wait()
                            local cakeLoaf2 = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('CakeLoaf')
                            if cakeLoaf2 then
                                cakeLoaf2 = cakeLoaf2:FindFirstChild('BigMirror')
                            end
                            if cakeLoaf2 then
                                cakeLoaf2 = cakeLoaf2:FindFirstChild('Other')
                            end
                            if cakeLoaf2 and cakeLoaf2.Transparency == 0 or (enemies:FindFirstChild('Cake Prince') or (enemies:FindFirstChild('Dough King') or (not closestMob.Parent or closestMob.Humanoid.Health <= 0))) then
                                break
                            end
                            local targetPos = closestMob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
                            if cakeTargetPos then
                                targetPos = CFrame.new(targetPos.Position.X, cakeTargetPos.Position.Y, targetPos.Position.Z) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
                            end
                            _tp(targetPos)
                            humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                            humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            if not cakeTargetPos or targetPos.Position.Y > cakeTargetPos.Position.Y then
                                cakeTargetPos = targetPos
                            end
                            self.MobUtils.Kill(closestMob, _G.AutoFarm_Cake)
                            if not _G.StartFarm or (not _G.AutoFarm_Cake or (not closestMob.Parent or closestMob.Humanoid.Health <= 0)) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        cakeTargetPos = nil
                        if humanoidRootPart then
                            local questNpc = CFrame.new(-1927.92, 37.8, -12842.54)
                            if (humanoidRootPart.Position - questNpc.Position).Magnitude > tweenRange then
                                _tp(questNpc)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    MainFarmTab:AddSeperator("Statistics point")
    if not _G.StatsList then
        _G.StatsList = {}
    end
    _G.StatsPointsAmount = 1

    MainFarmTab:AddSlider({
        Name = "Points Amount",
        Min = 1,
        Max = 100,
        Default = self:GetSetting('StatsAmount_Save', 1),
        Callback = function(value)
            local points = math.floor(value)
            _G.StatsPointsAmount = points
            _G.SaveData.StatsAmount_Save = points
            self:SaveSettings()
        end
    })

    MainFarmTab:AddDropdown({
        Name = "Select Stats to Upgrade",
        Options = { "Melee", "Defense", "Sword", "Gun", "Fruit" },
        MultiSelect = true,
        Default = self:GetSetting('SelectStatsToUpgrade', {}),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.SaveData.SelectStatsToUpgrade = value
            self:SaveSettings()
            local stats = {}
            if type(value) == 'table' then
                for _, stat in pairs(value) do
                    stats[stat] = true
                end
            end
            _G.StatsList = {
                Melee = stats.Melee or false,
                Defense = stats.Defense or false,
                Sword = stats.Sword or false,
                Gun = stats.Gun or false,
                Fruit = stats.Fruit or false
            }
            _G.SaveData.SelectedStats_Save = _G.StatsList
            self:SaveSettings()
        end
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.25) do
            pcall(function()
                local statMap = {
                    Melee = 'Melee',
                    Defense = 'Defense',
                    Sword = 'Sword',
                    Gun = 'Gun',
                    Fruit = 'Demon Fruit'
                }
                local pointsAmount = tonumber(_G.StatsPointsAmount) or 1
                if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.Points.Value > 0 and type(_G.StatsList) == 'table' then
                    for stat, enabled in pairs(_G.StatsList) do
                        if enabled then
                            local statName = statMap[stat]
                            if statName then
                                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('AddPoint', statName, pointsAmount)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    MainFarmTab:AddSeperator("Other")
    MainFarmTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Farm with the transformation of Buddha",
        Default = self:GetSetting('FarmBuddha_Save', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.FarmWithBuddha = value
            _G.SaveData.FarmBuddha_Save = value
            if self.SaveSettings then
                self:SaveSettings()
            end

    local localPlayer6 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local net = game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Net
    local rERegisterHit = net['RE/RegisterHit']
    local rERegisterAttack = net['RE/RegisterAttack']
    local sendHitsToServer = nil
    pcall(function()
        sendHitsToServer = getsenv(localPlayer6.PlayerScripts:WaitForChildOfClass('LocalScript'))._G.SendHitsToServer
    end)

    local hitParts = {
        'Head',
        'HumanoidRootPart',
        'LeftHand',
        'RightHand',
        'LeftLowerArm',
        'RightLowerArm'
    }

    local function isAlive(character)
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
        local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if humanoid then
            if humanoidRootPart then
                return humanoid.Health > 0
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function getTargets()
        local targets = {}
        local character = localPlayer6.Character
        if not character then
            return targets
        end
        for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
            if isAlive(enemy) then
                table.insert(targets, enemy)
            end
        end
        for _, player in pairs(workspace.Characters:GetChildren()) do
            if player ~= character and isAlive(player) then
                table.insert(targets, player)
            end
        end
        return targets
    end

    local function getRandomHitPart(target)
        return target:FindFirstChild(hitParts[math.random(#hitParts)]) or target:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            local shouldAttack = false
            if _G.FarmWithBuddha and localPlayer6.Character then
                local targets = getTargets()
                if #targets == 0 then
                    shouldAttack = true
                else
                    local hitData = {}
                    local primaryPart = nil
                    for _, target in pairs(targets) do
                        local part = getRandomHitPart(target)
                        if part then
                            primaryPart = primaryPart or part
                            table.insert(hitData, { target, part })
                        end
                    end
                    rERegisterAttack:FireServer(0)
                    if sendHitsToServer then
                        sendHitsToServer(unpack(hitData))
                        shouldAttack = true
                    else
                        rERegisterHit:FireServer(unpack(hitData))
                        shouldAttack = true
                    end
                end
            else
                shouldAttack = true
            end
            if not shouldAttack then
                break
            end
        end
    end)

    local function resetPartSizes(character)
        for _, part in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA('BasePart') then
                if part:FindFirstChild('OriginalSize') then
                    part.Size = part.OriginalSize.Value
                end
            elseif (part:IsA('SpecialMesh') or part:IsA('Mesh')) and part:FindFirstChild('OriginalScale') then
                part.Scale = part.OriginalScale.Value
            end
        end
    end

    local function fixBuddhaSize()
        local character = workspace.Characters:FindFirstChild(localPlayer6.Name)
        if character then
            pcall(function()
                resetPartSizes(character)
            end)
        end
    end

    fixBuddhaSize()
    localPlayer6.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(1)
        fixBuddhaSize()
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        local notified = false
        while task.wait(1) do
            local isFarming = _G.StartFarm or _G.FarmMastery_Dev or (_G.FarmMastery_G or _G.FarmMastery_S) or (_G.AutoFarmIsland or _G.Prehis_Skills or (_G.AutoBoss or _G.FarmAllBoss))
            if _G.FarmWithBuddha and isFarming then
                pcall(function()
                    local localPlayer7 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local character = localPlayer7.Character
                    if not character then
                        return
                    end
                    local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                    if not (humanoidRootPart and humanoid) then
                        return
                    end

                    local buddhaTool = nil
                    local hasBuddha = false
                    for _, tool in pairs(localPlayer7.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                        if tool:IsA('Tool') and string.find(string.lower(tool.Name), 'buddha') then
                            hasBuddha = true
                            buddhaTool = tool
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if not hasBuddha then
                        for _, tool in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                            if tool:IsA('Tool') and string.find(string.lower(tool.Name), 'buddha') then
                                hasBuddha = true
                                buddhaTool = tool
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if hasBuddha then
                        notified = false
                        local raceEnergy = character:FindFirstChild('RaceEnergy')
                        local raceTransformed = character:FindFirstChild('RaceTransformed')
                        local raceClickAutov4 = _G.RaceClickAutov4
                        if raceClickAutov4 then
                            if raceEnergy then
                                if raceEnergy.Value ~= 1 then
                                    raceTransformed = false
                                elseif raceTransformed then
                                    raceTransformed = not raceTransformed.Value
                                end
                            else
                                raceTransformed = raceEnergy
                            end
                        else
                            raceTransformed = raceClickAutov4
                        end

                        if raceTransformed or humanoidRootPart.Size.Y < 6 then
                            local savedStates = {
                                f1 = _G.StartFarm,
                                f2 = _G.FarmMastery_Dev,
                                f3 = _G.FarmMastery_G,
                                f4 = _G.FarmMastery_S,
                                f5 = _G.AutoFarmIsland,
                                f6 = _G.Prehis_Skills,
                                f7 = _G.AutoBoss,
                                f8 = _G.FarmAllBoss,
                                f9 = _G.Seriality
                            }
                            _G.StartFarm = false
                            _G.FarmMastery_Dev = false
                            _G.FarmMastery_G = false
                            _G.FarmMastery_S = false
                            _G.AutoFarmIsland = false
                            _G.Prehis_Skills = false
                            _G.AutoBoss = false
                            _G.FarmAllBoss = false
                            _G.Seriality = false
                            task.wait(0.5)

                            local anchored = humanoidRootPart.Anchored
                            humanoidRootPart.Anchored = true
                            humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

                            local virtualInputManager = game:GetService('VirtualInputManager')

                            if raceTransformed then
                                if humanoidRootPart.Size.Y > 6 then
                                    humanoid:UnequipTools()
                                    task.wait(0.2)
                                    humanoid:EquipTool(buddhaTool)
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
                                    local startTime = os.clock()
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait(0.1)
                                    until character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and character.HumanoidRootPart.Size.Y < 6 or os.clock() - startTime > 3
                                end
                                humanoid:UnequipTools()
                                task.wait(0.2)
                                virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Y', false, game)
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Y', false, game)
                                task.wait(1)
                            end

                            if character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and character.HumanoidRootPart.Size.Y < 6 then
                                humanoid:UnequipTools()
                                task.wait(0.2)
                                humanoid:EquipTool(buddhaTool)
                                task.wait(0.5)
                                virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
                                local startTime = os.clock()
                                repeat
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                until character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and character.HumanoidRootPart.Size.Y > 6 or os.clock() - startTime > 3
                            end

                            humanoid:UnequipTools()
                            task.wait(0.2)
                            local selectedWeapon = _G.SelectWeapon and (localPlayer7.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectWeapon) or character:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectWeapon))
                            if selectedWeapon then
                                humanoid:EquipTool(selectedWeapon)
                            end
                            humanoidRootPart.Anchored = anchored

                            _G.StartFarm = savedStates.f1
                            _G.FarmMastery_Dev = savedStates.f2
                            _G.FarmMastery_G = savedStates.f3
                            _G.FarmMastery_S = savedStates.f4
                            _G.AutoFarmIsland = savedStates.f5
                            _G.Prehis_Skills = savedStates.f6
                            _G.AutoBoss = savedStates.f7
                            _G.FarmAllBoss = savedStates.f8
                            _G.Seriality = savedStates.f9
                        end
                    elseif not notified then
                        if Window and Window.Notify then
                            Window:Notify({
                                Title = "Night Mystic",
                                Content = "Please equip the Buddha fruit",
                                Duration = 5,
                                Image = "rbxassetid://124914698428562"
                            })
                        end
                        notified = true
                        _G.FarmWithBuddha = false
                    end
                end)
            else
                notified = false
            end
        end
    end)

    _G.MaxFarmDistance = 325
    local players4 = game:GetService('Players')
    local replicatedStorage2 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')

    MainFarmTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Kill Mobs Nearest",
        Description = "",
        Default = self:GetSetting('AutoFarmNear_Save', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.AutoFarmNear = value
            _G.SaveData.AutoFarmNear_Save = value
            self:SaveSettings()
        end
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if not _G.AutoFarmNear then
                    return
                end
                local localPlayer7 = players4.LocalPlayer
                local humanoidRootPart = (localPlayer7.Character or localPlayer7.CharacterAdded:Wait()):FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if not humanoidRootPart then
                    return
                end
                local targetMob = nil
                local closestDist = math.huge
                if workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies') then
                    for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                            local dist = (humanoidRootPart.Position - enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < closestDist then
                                closestDist = dist
                                targetMob = enemy
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if replicatedStorage2:FindFirstChild('Enemies') then
                    for _, enemy in pairs(replicatedStorage2.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if enemy:IsA('Model') and enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') then
                            if not targetMob then
                                targetMob = enemy
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if targetMob and (not _G.MaxFarmDistance or closestDist <= _G.MaxFarmDistance) then
                    while true do
                        task.wait()
                        if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                            self.MobUtils.Kill(targetMob, _G.AutoFarmNear)
                        end
                        if not _G.AutoFarmNear or (not targetMob.Parent or targetMob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and targetMob.Humanoid.Health <= 0) or not humanoidRootPart.Parent then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    return
                else
                    return
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local farmDelay = 0.5

    if World2 then
        MainFarmTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Factory Raid",
            Description = "",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoFactory_Save', false),

        task.spawn(function()
            local factoryPos = CFrame.new(448.46756, 199.356781, -441.389252)
            while task.wait(farmDelay) do
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.AutoFactory then
                        local core = GetConnectionEnemies('Core')
                        if core and (core:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and core.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                            while true do
                                task.wait()
                                if not _G.AutoFactory or (not core or (not core.Parent or core.Humanoid.Health <= 0)) then
                                    break
                                end
                                if _G.SelectWeapon then
                                    EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
                                end
                                _tp(factoryPos)
                                if core.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or _G.AutoFactory == false then
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            _tp(factoryPos)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    if World3 then
        MainFarmTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Pirate Raid",
            Description = "",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoRaidCastle_Save', false),
            Callback = function(value)
                _G.AutoRaidCastle = value
                _G.SaveData.AutoRaidCastle_Save = value
                self:SaveSettings()
            end
        })

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(farmDelay) do
                if _G.AutoRaidCastle then
                    pcall(function()
                        local raidPos = CFrame.new(-5496.17432, 313.768921, -2841.53027, 0.924894512, 7.37058015e-9, 0.380223751, 3.5881019e-8, 1, -1.06665446e-7, -0.380223751, 1.12297109e-7, 0.924894512)
                        if (CFrame.new(-5539.3115234375, 313.80053710938, -2972.3723144531).Position - Root.Position).Magnitude <= 500 then
                            for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                if enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position - Root.Position).Magnitude <= 2000)) then
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait()
                                        self.MobUtils.Kill(enemy, _G.AutoRaidCastle)
                                    until not _G.AutoRaidCastle or (not enemy.Parent or enemy.Humanoid.Health <= 0) or not workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(enemy.Name)
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            local raidMobs = {
                                'Galley Pirate', 'Galley Captain', 'Raider', 'Mercenary',
                                'Vampire', 'Zombie', 'Snow Trooper', 'Winter Warrior',
                                'Lab Subordinate', 'Horned Warrior', 'Magma Ninja', 'Lava Pirate',
                                'Ship Deckhand', 'Ship Engineer', 'Ship Steward', 'Ship Officer',
                                'Arctic Warrior', 'Snow Lurker', 'Sea Soldier', 'Water Fighter'
                            }
                            for _, mobName in pairs(raidMobs) do
                                if replicated:FindFirstChild(mobName) then
                                    for _, obj in pairs(replicated:GetChildren()) do
                                        if table.find(raidMobs, obj.Name) then
                                            _tp(raidPos)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(farmDelay) do
            if _G.AutoBerry then
                local collectionService = game:GetService('CollectionService')
                local localPlayer7 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
                local berryBushes = collectionService:GetTagged('BerryBush')
                local foundBerry = false
                for _, bush in pairs(berryBushes) do
                    for attrName, attrValue in pairs(bush:GetAttributes()) do
                        if not BerryArray or table.find(BerryArray, attrValue) then
                            _tp(bush.Parent:GetPivot())
                            foundBerry = true
                            for _, bush2 in pairs(berryBushes) do
                                for _, child in pairs(bush2:GetChildren()) do
                                    if not BerryArray or table.find(BerryArray, child) then
                                        _tp(child.WorldPivot)
                                        fireproximityprompt(child.ProximityPrompt, math.huge)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if _G.AutoBerryHop and not foundBerry then
                    Window:Notify({
                        Title = "Server Hop",
                        Content = "Switching server...",
                        Duration = 10
                    })
                    servhop()
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local lastChest = nil

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(farmDelay) do
            if _G.AutoFarmChest or _G.AutoFarmChestBypass then
                pcall(function()
                    local collectionService = game:GetService('CollectionService')
                    local localPlayer7 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
                    local character = localPlayer7.Character or localPlayer7.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    local position = character:GetPivot().Position
                    local chests = collectionService:GetTagged('_ChestTagged')
                    local closestChest = nil
                    local closestDist = math.huge
                    for _, chest in pairs(chests) do
                        local dist = (chest:GetPivot().Position - position).Magnitude
                        if (not SelectedIsland or chest:IsDescendantOf(SelectedIsland)) and not chest:GetAttribute('IsDisabled') then
                            if dist < closestDist then
                                closestChest = chest
                                closestDist = dist
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if closestChest then
                        if closestChest ~= lastChest then
                            if not _G.ChestsColetados then
                                _G.ChestsColetados = 0
                            end
                            _G.ChestsColetados = _G.ChestsColetados + 1
                            lastChest = closestChest
                        end
                        if _G.AutoFarmChestBypass then
                            character:PivotTo(closestChest:GetPivot())
                        else
                            _tp(closestChest:GetPivot())
                        end
                        if _G.AutoChestHop and _G.ChestsColetados >= _G.AmountChestHop then
                            Window:Notify({
                                Title = "Server Hop",
                                Content = "Switching server...",
                                Duration = 10
                            })
                            servhop()
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    local function equipWeaponForBoss()
        local character = localPlayer7.Character
        if character then
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
            if humanoid then
                if _G.SelectWeapon then
                    local weapon = localPlayer7.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectWeapon) or character:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectWeapon)
                    if weapon and weapon.Parent ~= character then
                        humanoid:EquipTool(weapon)
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            return
        end
    end

    local function teleportToBoss(boss)
        local character = localPlayer7.Character
        local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
        if boss and (humanoidRootPart and humanoid) then
            local targetPart = boss:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') or boss:FindFirstChild('Torso')
            if targetPart then
                local targetPos = targetPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 22, 0)
                local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
                local dist = (humanoidRootPart.Position - targetPos).Magnitude
                equipWeaponForBoss()
                if dist <= 5 then
                    humanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
                    humanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                elseif _tp then
                    _tp(targetCFrame)
                else
                    humanoidRootPart.CFrame = targetCFrame
                end
            end
        else
            return
        end
    end

    local bossDropdown = MainFarmTab:AddDropdown({
        Name = "Select Boss",
        Options = Boss,

    local function refreshBossList()
        local sources = { workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies'), replicatedStorage3 }
        local bossNames = {}
        local foundAny = false
        for _, source in pairs(sources) do
            if source then
                for _, obj in pairs(source:GetChildren()) do
                    if obj:IsA('Model') and (obj:GetAttribute('IsBoss') == true and not table.find(bossNames, obj.Name)) then
                        table.insert(bossNames, obj.Name)
                        foundAny = true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if foundAny then
            table.sort(bossNames)
            bossDropdown:Refresh(bossNames)
        else
            bossDropdown:Refresh({ 'None' })
        end
    end

    MainFarmTab:AddButton({
        Name = "Refresh Boss List",
        Callback = refreshBossList
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.5) do
            if _G.FarmAllBoss and (not (_G.CurrentTargetBoss and _G.CurrentTargetBoss.Parent) or (not _G.CurrentTargetBoss:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') or _G.CurrentTargetBoss.Humanoid.Health <= 0)) then
                local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer7.Character and localPlayer7.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if humanoidRootPart then
                    local bossList = {}
                    local foundBoss = false

                    local function scanBosses(source)
                        if source then
                            for _, obj in pairs(source:GetChildren()) do
                                if obj:IsA('Model') and obj:GetAttribute('IsBoss') == true then
                                    local humanoid = obj:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                                    local part = obj:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') or obj:FindFirstChild('Torso')
                                    if humanoid and (humanoid.Health > 0 and part) then
                                        table.insert(bossList, {
                                            Model = obj,
                                            Distance = (humanoidRootPart.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                                        })
                                        foundBoss = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    scanBosses(workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies'))
                    if not foundBoss then
                        scanBosses(replicatedStorage3)
                    end

                    if #bossList > 0 then
                        table.sort(bossList, function(a, b)
                            return a.Distance < b.Distance
                        end)
                        _G.CurrentTargetBoss = bossList[1].Model
                    else
                        _G.CurrentTargetBoss = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if _G.AutoBoss and _G.FindBoss then
                    if QuestB then
                        QuestB()
                    end
                    local shouldAcceptQuest = false
                    local character = localPlayer7.Character
                    if not (character and character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')) then
                        return
                    end
                    if _G.AutoAcceptQuest and (Qname and (Qdata and PosQBoss)) then
                        local questVisible = localPlayer7.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild('Main')
                        if questVisible then
                            questVisible = localPlayer7.PlayerGui.Main:FindFirstChild('Quest')
                        end
                        if questVisible then
                            questVisible = questVisible.Visible
                        end
                        shouldAcceptQuest = not questVisible and true or shouldAcceptQuest
                    end
                    if shouldAcceptQuest then
                        if (PosQBoss.Position - character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 5 then
                            replicatedStorage3.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('StartQuest', Qname, Qdata)
                        elseif _tp then
                            _tp(PosQBoss)
                        else
                            character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = PosQBoss
                        end
                        return
                    end
                    local boss = workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies') and workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(_G.FindBoss) or workspace:FindFirstChild(_G.FindBoss)
                    if boss and (boss:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and boss.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                        teleportToBoss(boss)
                    elseif PosB then
                        if _tp then
                            _tp(PosB)
                        else
                            character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = PosB
                        end
                    end
                elseif _G.FarmAllBoss and _G.CurrentTargetBoss then
                    teleportToBoss(_G.CurrentTargetBoss)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.5) do
            if localPlayer7.Backpack and _G.ChooseWP then
                for _, tool in pairs(localPlayer7.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool.ToolTip == _G.ChooseWP then
                        _G.SelectWeapon = tool.Name
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local players5 = game:GetService('Players')
    local runService2 = game:GetService('RunService')
    local localPlayer8 = players5.LocalPlayer
    local aimbotEnabled = true
    local aimbotDelay = 0.1
    local aimbotRange = 1000
    local aimbotTarget = nil
    local aimbotPrediction = nil
    local aimbotConnections = {}

    _G.SelectIsland = _G.SelectIsland or 'Bone'

    local function getClosestPlayer(position)
        local closestPlayer = nil
        local closestDist = math.huge
        for _, player in ipairs(players5:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= localPlayer8 and player.Character then
                local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('Humanoid')
                local humanoidRootPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if humanoid and (humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoidRootPart) then
                    local dist = (humanoidRootPart.Position - position.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < closestDist and dist <= aimbotRange then
                        closestPlayer = player
                        closestDist = dist
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return closestPlayer
    end

    local function predictPosition(part)
        if part then
            local humanoid = part.Parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('Humanoid')
            if humanoid and (aimbotEnabled and humanoid.WalkSpeed >= 5) then
                return part.Position + part.Velocity * aimbotDelay
            else
                return part.Position
            end
        else
            return nil
        end
    end

    local function hasSkillCooldown(skillName)
        if not skillName then
            return false
        end
        local playerGui = localPlayer8:FindFirstChild('PlayerGui')
        local skills = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild('Main')
        if skills then
            skills = playerGui.Main:FindFirstChild('Skills')
        end
        if skills then
            skills = skills:FindFirstChild(skillName)
        end
        if not skills then
            return false
        end
        for _, key in ipairs({ 'Z', 'X', 'C', 'V' }) do
            local skill = skills:FindFirstChild(key)
            if skill and (skill:FindFirstChild('Cooldown') and skill.Cooldown.Size.X.Scale == 1) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    runService2.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if SilentAim_Enabled or (_G.LockMob or (_G.FarmMastery_Dev or _G.FarmMastery_G)) then
            local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer8.Character and localPlayer8.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            if humanoidRootPart then
                local currentTarget = nil
                if (_G.LockMob or (_G.FarmMastery_Dev or _G.FarmMastery_G)) and _G.CurrentMob and (_G.CurrentMob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (_G.CurrentMob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and _G.CurrentMob.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
                    currentTarget = _G.CurrentMob
                elseif SilentAim_Enabled then
                    if _G.TargetMode == 'Selected Player' then
                        currentTarget = players5:FindFirstChild(_G.PlayersList)
                    else
                        currentTarget = getClosestPlayer(humanoidRootPart)
                    end
                end
                if currentTarget then
                    local targetPart = currentTarget.Character and currentTarget.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if targetPart then
                        aimbotPrediction = predictPosition(targetPart)
                        if aimbotTarget and hasSkillCooldown(aimbotTarget.Name) then
                            local direction = (Vector3.new(aimbotPrediction.X, humanoidRootPart.Position.Y, aimbotPrediction.Z) - humanoidRootPart.Position).Unit
                            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoidRootPart.Position, humanoidRootPart.Position + direction)
                        end
                    else
                        aimbotPrediction = nil
                    end
                else
                    aimbotPrediction = nil
                end
            end
        else
            aimbotPrediction = nil
            return
        end
    end)

    localPlayer8.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        for _, connection in ipairs(aimbotConnections) do
            pcall(function()
                connection:Disconnect()
            end)
        end
        aimbotConnections = {}
        table.insert(aimbotConnections, character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child:IsA('Tool') then
                aimbotTarget = child
            end
        end))
        table.insert(aimbotConnections, character.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
            if child == aimbotTarget then
                aimbotTarget = nil
            end
        end))
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local meta = getrawmetatable(game)
        setreadonly(meta, false)
        local namecall = meta.__namecall
        meta.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            local args = { ... }
            if method ~= 'FireServer' and method ~= 'InvokeServer' or (not (_G.LockMob or (SilentAim_Enabled or (_G.FarmMastery_Dev or _G.FarmMastery_G))) or (not aimbotPrediction or typeof(args[1]) ~= 'Vector3')) then
                return namecall(...)
            end
            args[1] = aimbotPrediction
            return namecall(unpack(args))
        end)
        setreadonly(meta, true)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if _G.LockCam then
                    local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    local targetMob
                    if _G.CurrentMob and (_G.CurrentMob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (_G.CurrentMob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and _G.CurrentMob.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
                        targetMob = _G.CurrentMob
                    else
                        targetMob = nil
                    end
                    local targetPart = targetMob and targetMob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if targetPart then
                        currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentCamera.CFrame.Position, targetPart.Position)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local localPlayer9 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
    local runService3 = game:GetService('RunService')
    local replicatedStorage4 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
    local virtualInputManager = game:GetService('VirtualInputManager')
    local userInputService = game:GetService('UserInputService')

    local function resetWeaponCooldown(weapon)
        pcall(function()
            local cooldown = weapon:FindFirstChild('Cooldown')
            if cooldown then
                cooldown.Value = 0
            end
            local holding = weapon:FindFirstChild('Holding')
            if holding then
                holding.Value = true
            end
            weapon:SetAttribute('Overheat', 0)
            weapon:SetAttribute('LocalOverheat', 0)
        end)
    end

    local function stopAnimations()
        local character = localPlayer9.Character
        if character then
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
            if humanoid then
                humanoid = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass('Animator')
            end
            if humanoid then
                for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    local name = (track.Name or ''):lower()
                    if name:find('shoot') or (name:find('gun') or (name:find('fire') or name:find('attack'))) then
                        pcall(function()
                            track:Stop(0)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            return
        end
    end

    local centerScreen = nil
    task.spawn(function()
        while not centerScreen do
            task.wait()
            local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if currentCamera then
                centerScreen = currentCamera.ViewportSize / 2
            end
        end
    end)

    MainFarmTab:AddSeperator("Mastery")

    MainFarmTab:AddDropdown({
        Name = "Select Method",
        Options = { "Cake", "Bone" },
        Default = self:GetSetting('SelectMethod', _G.SelectIsland or 'Bone'),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.SaveData.SelectMethod = value
            self:SaveSettings()

        end
    })

    local function getMasteryTarget(targetMobs)
        local localPlayer9 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer9.Character and localPlayer9.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if not humanoidRootPart then
            return nil
        end
        local healthThreshold = (_G.MasteryHealthPercent or 70) / 100
        local lowHealthMob = nil
        local lowHealthDist = math.huge
        local highHealthMob = nil
        local highHealthDist = math.huge
        for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
            if table.find(targetMobs, enemy.Name) and (enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
                local dist = (enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                if enemy.Humanoid.Health > enemy.Humanoid.MaxHealth * healthThreshold then
                    if dist < highHealthDist then
                        highHealthMob = enemy
                        highHealthDist = dist
                    end
                else
                    if dist < lowHealthDist then
                        lowHealthMob = enemy
                        lowHealthDist = dist
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return lowHealthMob or highHealthMob
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local function killMaterialMob(mob, materialName)
            if mob:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (mob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (mob.Humanoid.Health > 0 and mob.Name == materialName)) then
                repeat
                    task.wait()
                    self.MobUtils.Kill(mob, getgenv().AutoMaterial)
                until not getgenv().AutoMaterial or (not mob.Parent or mob.Humanoid.Health <= 0)
            end
        end

        local function teleportToSpawn()
            for _, spawn in pairs(game:GetService('Workspace')._WorldOrigin.EnemySpawns:GetChildren()) do
                for _, mobName in ipairs(MMon) do
                    if string.find(spawn.Name, mobName) and (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - spawn.Position).Magnitude >= 10 then
                        _tp(spawn.CFrame * Pos)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        while task.wait() do
            if getgenv().AutoMaterial then
                pcall(function()
                    if getgenv().SelectMaterial then
                        MaterialMon(getgenv().SelectMaterial)
                        _tp(MPos)
                    end
                    local closestMob = nil
                    local closestDist = math.huge
                    for _, mobName in ipairs(MMon) do
                        for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                            if enemy.Name == mobName and (enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
                                local dist = (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist < closestDist then
                                    closestMob = enemy
                                    closestDist = dist
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if closestMob then
                        killMaterialMob(closestMob, closestMob.Name)
                    end
                    teleportToSpawn()
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

                    if not _G.AutoRipIndra then
                        return
                    end
                    local players6 = game:GetService('Players')
                    local replicatedStorage5 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
                    local workspace2 = game:GetService('Workspace')
                    local localPlayer10 = players6.LocalPlayer
                    local humanoidRootPart = (localPlayer10.Character or localPlayer10.CharacterAdded:Wait()):FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if not humanoidRootPart then
                        return
                    end
                    local ripIndra = nil
                    if workspace2:FindFirstChild('Enemies') then
                        for _, enemy in pairs(workspace2.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                            if enemy.Name == 'rip_indra' and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                                ripIndra = enemy
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if not ripIndra and replicatedStorage5:FindFirstChild('rip_indra True Form') then
                        for _, obj in pairs(workspace2:GetChildren()) do
                            if obj.Name == 'rip_indra' and (obj:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and obj.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                                ripIndra = obj
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if ripIndra and ripIndra:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                        while true do
                            task.wait()
                            if _tp then
                                _tp(ripIndra.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
                            else
                                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = ripIndra.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0)
                            end
                            if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                                self.MobUtils.Kill(ripIndra, true)
                            end
                            if not _G.AutoRipIndra or (not ripIndra.Parent or (ripIndra.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not humanoidRootPart.Parent)) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        return
                    else
                        return
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        function AuraSkin(skinName)
            local args = {
                {
                    StorageName = skinName,
                    Type = 'AuraSkin',
                    Context = 'Equip'
                }
            }
            replicated:WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RF/FruitCustomizerRF'):InvokeServer(unpack(args))
        end

        function VaildColor(part)
            if part and part.BrickColor then
                return tostring(part.BrickColor) == 'Lime green'
            end
            return false
        end

        function HakiCalculate(part)
            local colorMap = {
                ['Really red'] = 'Pure Red',
                Oyster = 'Snow White',
                ['Hot pink'] = 'Winter Sky'
            }
            if part and part.BrickColor then
                return colorMap[tostring(part.BrickColor)]
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(Sec) do
                if _G.AutoUnHaki then
                    pcall(function()
                        local summoner = workspace.Map['Boat Castle']:FindFirstChild('Summoner')
                        if summoner and summoner:FindFirstChild('Circle') then
                            for _, child in pairs(summoner:FindFirstChild('Circle'):GetChildren()) do
                                if child.Name == 'Part' then
                                    local part = child:FindFirstChild('Part')
                                    if not VaildColor(part) then
                                        AuraSkin(HakiCalculate(child))
                                        repeat
                                            task.wait()
                                            _tp(child.CFrame)
                                        until VaildColor(part) == true or not _G.AutoUnHaki
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local remotesFolders = {
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Util,
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Common,
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes,
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Assets,
        game.ReplicatedStorage.FX
    }
    local remoteEvent = nil
    local remoteId = nil

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            for _, folder in pairs(remotesFolders) do
                for _, obj in pairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                    if obj:IsA('RemoteEvent') and obj:GetAttribute('Id') then
                        remoteId = obj:GetAttribute('Id')
                        remoteEvent = obj
                    end
                end
                folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                    if child:IsA('RemoteEvent') and child:GetAttribute('Id') then
                        remoteId = child:GetAttribute('Id')
                        remoteEvent = child
                    end
                end)
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        local clonedRef = cloneref or function(obj) return obj end
        while true do
            while not _G.OtherAutoAttack do
                task.wait(1)
            end
            task.wait(0.01)
            pcall(function()
                local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if humanoidRootPart then
                    local targets = {}
                    local sources = { workspace.Enemies, workspace.Characters }
                    for _, source in ipairs(sources) do
                        for _, obj in ipairs(source and (source:GetChildren() or {}) or {}) do
                            local targetPart = obj:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                            local humanoid = obj:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                            if obj ~= character and (targetPart and (humanoid and (humanoid.Health > 0 and (targetPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 60))) then
                                for _, part in pairs(obj:GetChildren()) do
                                    if part:IsA('BasePart') then
                                        table.insert(targets, { obj, part })
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
                    if #targets > 0 and (tool and (tool:GetAttribute('WeaponType') == 'Melee' or tool:GetAttribute('WeaponType') == 'Sword')) then
                        require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Net):RemoteEvent('RegisterHit', true)
                        game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Net['RE/RegisterAttack']:FireServer()
                        local head = targets[1][1]:FindFirstChild('Head')
                        if head and (remoteEvent and remoteId) then
                            game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Net['RE/RegisterHit']:FireServer(head, targets, {}, tostring(game.Players.LocalPlayer.UserId):sub(2, 4) .. tostring(coroutine.running()):sub(11, 15))
                            clonedRef(remoteEvent):FireServer(string.gsub('RE/RegisterHit', '.', function(char)
                                return string.char(bit32.bxor(string.byte(char), math.floor(workspace:GetServerTimeNow() / 10 % 10) + 1))
                            end), bit32.bxor(remoteId + 909090, game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Net.seed:InvokeServer() * 2), head, targets)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

                screenGui2.Name = 'WhiteOverlay'
                screenGui2.IgnoreGuiInset = true
                screenGui2.Parent = game:GetService('CoreGui')
                local frame2 = Instance.new('Frame')
                frame2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
                frame2.Parent = screenGui2
            else
                local coreGui2 = game:GetService('CoreGui')
                if coreGui2:FindFirstChild('WhiteOverlay') then
                    coreGui2.WhiteOverlay:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    StackFarmTab:AddDropdown({
        Name = "Select Island",
        Options = islandNames,
        Default = self:GetSetting('SelectIsland', islandNames[1]),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.SaveData.SelectIsland = value
            self:SaveSettings()
            _G.SelectIsland = value
        end
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if not _G.AutoKillMob then
                    return
                end
                local humanoidRootPart = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if not humanoidRootPart then
                    return
                end
                local targetMob = nil
                for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                    if enemy.Name == _G.SelectMob and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 and enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart'))) then
                        targetMob = enemy
                        break
                    end
                end
                if targetMob then
                    while true do
                        task.wait()
                        if targetMob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                            _tp(targetMob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 7, 0))
                        end
                        if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                            self.MobUtils.Kill(targetMob, _G.AutoKillMob)
                        end
                        if not _G.AutoKillMob or (not targetMob.Parent or (targetMob.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not humanoidRootPart.Parent)) then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    return
                else
                    if self.specialMobs and self.specialMobs[_G.SelectMob] then
                        _tp(self.specialMobs[_G.SelectMob])
                    else
                        for _, data in pairs(islandData) do
                            for _, mobName in pairs(data.Mobs or {}) do
                                if mobName == _G.SelectMob then
                                    local targetCFrame = not data.CFrame and data.CFrames
                                    if targetCFrame then
                                        targetCFrame = data.CFrames[1]
                                    end
                                    if targetCFrame then
                                        _tp(targetCFrame)
                                    end
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    _G.TurtleIndex = 1
    _G.SpecialMobCFrame = {
        ['Forest Pirate'] = CFrame.new(-13340.9004, 427.772522, -7953.85059),
        ['Mythological Pirate'] = CFrame.new(-13236.0215, 520.658691, -9940.7373),
        ['Jungle Pirate'] = CFrame.new(-11652.0732, 382.960083, -10469.7217),
        ['Musketeer Pirate'] = CFrame.new(-13268.3154, 484.197815, -9853.70605),
        ['Fishman Raider'] = CFrame.new(-10784.6094, 564.63269, -8945.54395),
        ['Fishman Captain'] = CFrame.new(-10784.6094, 564.63269, -8945.54395)
    }

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if not _G.AutoFarmIsland then
                    return
                end
                local humanoidRootPart = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if not humanoidRootPart then
                    return
                end
                local island = islandData[_G.SelectIsland]
                if not island then
                    return
                end
                local targetCFrame = island.CFrame
                if island.CFrames then
                    targetCFrame = island.CFrames[_G.TurtleIndex]
                end
                local targetMob = nil
                for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                    local isTarget = false
                    for _, mobName in pairs(island.Mobs or {}) do
                        if enemy.Name == mobName then
                            isTarget = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if isTarget and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 and enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart'))) then
                        targetMob = enemy
                        break
                    end
                end
                if targetMob then
                    while true do
                        task.wait()
                        if targetMob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                            _tp(targetMob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 7, 0))
                        end
                        if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                            self.MobUtils.Kill(targetMob, _G.AutoFarmIsland)
                        end
                        if not _G.AutoFarmIsland or (not targetMob.Parent or (targetMob.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not humanoidRootPart.Parent)) then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    return
                else
                    if island.CFrames then
                        _G.TurtleIndex = _G.TurtleIndex % #island.CFrames + 1
                    end
                    _tp(targetCFrame)
                    return
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

                            _tp(CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488))
                        until not _G.TravelDres or Root.Position == CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488).Position
                    elseif workspace.Map.Ice.Door.CanCollide ~= false or workspace.Map.Ice.Door.Transparency ~= 1 then
                        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('TravelDressrosa')
                    elseif Enemies:FindFirstChild('Ice Admiral') then
                        for _, enemy in pairs(Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                            if enemy.Name == 'Ice Admiral' and self.MobUtils.Alive(enemy) then
                                repeat
                                    task.wait()
                                    self.MobUtils.Kill(enemy, _G.TravelDres)
                                until _G.TravelDres == false or enemy.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('TravelDressrosa')
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        _tp(CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

                        replicated.Remotes.CommE:FireServer('Ken', true)

                end

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(Sec) do
                if _G.AutoKenVTWO then
                    pcall(function()
                        local citizenPos = CFrame.new(-12444.78515625, 332.40396118164, -7673.1806640625)
                        local fruitBowlPos = CFrame.new(-10920.125, 624.20275878906, -10266.995117188)
                        local forestPos = CFrame.new(-13277.568359375, 370.34185791016, -7821.1572265625)
                        local captainPos = CFrame.new(-13493.12890625, 318.89553833008, -8373.7919921875)

                        if self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible ~= true or not string.find(self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, 'Defeat 50 Forest Pirates') then
                            if self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible ~= true then
                                if self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false then
                                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('CitizenQuestProgress', 'Citizen')
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('StartQuest', 'CitizenQuest', 1)
                                end
                            else
                                local captain = GetConnectionEnemies('Captain Elephant')
                                if captain then
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait()
                                        self.MobUtils.Kill(captain, _G.AutoKenVTWO)
                                    until not _G.AutoKenVTWO or (captain.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false)
                                else
                                    _tp(captainPos)
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            local forestPirate = GetConnectionEnemies('Forest Pirate')
                            if forestPirate then
                                repeat
                                    task.wait()
                                    self.MobUtils.Kill(forestPirate, _G.AutoKenVTWO)
                                until not _G.AutoKenVTWO or (forestPirate.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or self.localPlayer3.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false)
                            else
                                _tp(forestPos)
                            end
                        end

                        if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('CitizenQuestProgress', 'Citizen') == 2 then
                            _tp(CFrame.new(-12513.51953125, 340.11373901367, -9873.048828125))
                        end

                        if not (self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Fruit Bowl') and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Fruit Bowl')) then

                                    if GetBP('Banana') then
                                        if not GetBP('Pineapple') then
