                                            for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                                                if obj.Name == 'Pineapple' then
                                                    obj.Handle.CFrame = self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 10)
                                                    task.wait()
                                                    firetouchinterest(self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart, obj.Handle, 0)
                                                    task.wait()
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    else
                                        _tp(CFrame.new(2286.0078125, 73.133918762207, -7159.8090820312))
                                        for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                                            if obj.Name == 'Banana' then
                                                obj.Handle.CFrame = self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 10)
                                                task.wait()
                                                firetouchinterest(self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart, obj.Handle, 0)
                                                task.wait()
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                else
                                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(-12471.169921875, 374.94024658203, -7551.677734375))
                                    for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                                        if obj.Name == 'Apple' then
                                            obj.Handle.CFrame = self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 10)
                                            task.wait()
                                            firetouchinterest(self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart, obj.Handle, 0)
                                            task.wait()
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Banana') and (self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Apple') and self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Pineapple')) or self.localPlayer3:FindFirstChild('Banana') and (self.localPlayer3:FindFirstChild('Apple') and self.localPlayer3:FindFirstChild('Pineapple')) then
                                repeat
                                    task.wait()
                                    _tp(citizenPos)
                                until _G.AutoKenVTWO or self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame == citizenPos
                                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('CitizenQuestProgress', 'Citizen')
                            end
                            if self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Fruit Bowl') or self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Fruit Bowl') then
                                if self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame ~= fruitBowlPos then
                                    _tp(fruitBowlPos)
                                elseif self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame == fruitBowlPos then

                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('KenTalk2', 'Buy')
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

                                    end
                                end
                                for _, enemy in pairs(Enemies:GetChildren()) do

                                end)

                        end
                    end
                end)
            end

    StackFarmTab:AddToggle({

    local itemConfig = require(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').ItemConfig)

    local function hasYamaInGC()
        for _, obj in pairs(getgc(true)) do
            if type(obj) == 'table' and rawget(obj, 'Tiles') then
                local tiles = obj.Tiles
                if type(tiles) == 'table' then
                    for _, tile in pairs(tiles) do
                        if type(tile) == 'table' and tile.ItemId then
                            local item = itemConfig.tryGet(tile.ItemId)
                            if item and string.find(item.Index.DebugLabel, 'Yama') then
                                return true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local lastEliteCheck = 0
        local eliteProgress = 0
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if _G.AutoYama then
                    if not yamaNotified then
                        if hasYamaInGC() then
                            Window:Notify({
                                Title = "Auto Yama",
                                Content = "Done",
                                Duration = 5,
                                Image = "rbxassetid://124914698428562"
                            })
                            _G.AutoYama = false
                            yamaNotified = true
                            return
                        end
                        yamaNotified = true
                    end

                    local replicatedStorage5 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
                    local enemies = game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies
                    local virtualInputManager2 = game:GetService('VirtualInputManager')
                    local localPlayer11 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local humanoidRootPart = (localPlayer11.Character or localPlayer11.CharacterAdded:Wait()):WaitForChild('HumanoidRootPart')

                    if tick() - lastEliteCheck > 3 then
                        lastEliteCheck = tick()
                        local progress = replicatedStorage5.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('EliteHunter', 'Progress')
                        eliteProgress = tonumber(progress) or 0
                    end

                    if eliteProgress < 30 then
                        _G.FarmEliteHunt = true
                        if not replicatedStorage5:FindFirstChild('Diablo') and (not replicatedStorage5:FindFirstChild('Deandre') and (not replicatedStorage5:FindFirstChild('Urban') and (not enemies:FindFirstChild('Diablo') and (not enemies:FindFirstChild('Deandre') and (not enemies:FindFirstChild('Urban') and tick() - yamaLastCheck > 10))))) then
                            yamaLastCheck = tick()
                            Window:Notify({
                                Title = "Auto Yama",
                                Content = "Waiting Elite Hunter...",
                                Duration = 5,
                                Image = "rbxassetid://124914698428562"
                            })
                        end
                    else
                        _G.FarmEliteHunt = false
                        local yamaPos = CFrame.new(5248.48828, 16.9484062, 453.278839, 0.0454210564, -9.33278059e-8, -0.998967946, -4.74314845e-8, 1, -9.61904192e-8, 0.998968005, 5.12186276e-8, 0.0454210676)
                        if (humanoidRootPart.Position - yamaPos.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                            _tp(yamaPos)
                        else
                            local sealedKatana = workspace.Map.Waterfall:FindFirstChild('SealedKatana')
                            if sealedKatana and sealedKatana:FindFirstChild('Hitbox') then
                                local hitbox = sealedKatana.Hitbox
                                hitbox.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                fireclickdetector(hitbox.ClickDetector)
                                virtualInputManager2:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 0)
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                virtualInputManager2:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 0)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

        ItemsTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Farm Cursed Captain",
            Description = "",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoCursedCaptain_Save', false),
            Callback = function(value)
                _G.AutoCursedCaptain = value
                _G.SaveData.AutoCursedCaptain_Save = value
                self:SaveSettings()
            end
        })

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait() do
                pcall(function()
                    if not _G.AutoCursedCaptain then
                        return
                    end
                    local players6 = game:GetService('Players')
                    local replicatedStorage5 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
                    local workspace2 = game:GetService('Workspace')
                    local localPlayer11 = players6.LocalPlayer
                    local humanoidRootPart = (localPlayer11.Character or localPlayer11.CharacterAdded:Wait()):FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if not humanoidRootPart then
                        return
                    end
                    local entrancePos = Vector3.new(923, 126, 32852)
                    local function isAtEntrance()
                        return (humanoidRootPart.Position - entrancePos).Magnitude < 2000
                    end
                    if not isAtEntrance() then
                        replicatedStorage5.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', entrancePos)
                        task.wait(1)
                    end
                    local cursedCaptain = nil
                    if workspace2:FindFirstChild('Enemies') then
                        for _, enemy in pairs(workspace2.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                            if enemy.Name == 'Cursed Captain' and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                                cursedCaptain = enemy
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if not cursedCaptain then
                        while true do
                            task.wait(1)
                            if not isAtEntrance() then
                                replicatedStorage5.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', entrancePos)
                            end
                            if workspace2:FindFirstChild('Enemies') then
                                for _, enemy in pairs(workspace2.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                    if enemy.Name == 'Cursed Captain' and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                                        cursedCaptain = enemy
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if cursedCaptain or not _G.AutoCursedCaptain then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if cursedCaptain and cursedCaptain:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                        while true do
                            task.wait()
                            if not isAtEntrance() then
                                replicatedStorage5.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', entrancePos)
                                task.wait(1)
                            end
                            if _tp then
                                _tp(cursedCaptain.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
                            else
                                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cursedCaptain.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0)
                            end
                            if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                                self.MobUtils.Kill(cursedCaptain, true)
                            end
                            if not _G.AutoCursedCaptain or (not cursedCaptain.Parent or (cursedCaptain.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not humanoidRootPart.Parent)) then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        return
                    else
                        return
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

                            FruitPrice = {}
                            FruitStore = {}
                            local fruits = replicated:WaitForChild('Remotes').CommF_:InvokeServer('GetFruits')
                            for _, fruit in pairs(fruits) do
                                if fruit.Price >= 1000000 then
                                    table.insert(FruitPrice, fruit.Name)
                                end
                            end
                            local inventory = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventoryFruits')
                            for _, data in pairs(inventory) do
                                for _, item in pairs(data) do
                                    if item == 'Name' then
                                        table.insert(FruitStore, item)
                                    end
                                end
                                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Cousin', 'Buy')
                                for _, priceFruit in pairs(FruitPrice) do
                                    for _, storeFruit in pairs(FruitStore) do
                                        if priceFruit == storeFruit and replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('GetUnlockables').FlamingoAccess == nil then
                                            _G.StoreF = false
                                            if self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild(FruitStore) then
                                                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('TalkTrevor', '1')
                                                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('TalkTrevor', '2')
                                                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('TalkTrevor', '3')
                                            else
                                                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('LoadFruit', tostring(priceFruit))
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('GetUnlockables').FlamingoAccess ~= nil then
                                    _G.StoreF = true
                                    _G.Auto_DonAcces = false
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

            return _G.Shark and CheckShark() and true or (_G.TerrorShark and CheckTerrorShark() and true or (_G.MobCrew and CheckFishCrew() and true or (_G.Piranha and CheckPiranha() and true or (_G.FishBoat and CheckEnemiesBoat() and true or (_G.SeaBeast1 and CheckSeaBeast() and true or (_G.PGB and CheckPirateGrandBrigade() and true or (_G.HCM and CheckHauntedCrew() and true or (_G.Leviathan1 and CheckLeviathan() and true or false))))))))
        end

                        local localPlayer11 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                        if localPlayer11.Character and (localPlayer11.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (localPlayer11.Character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and localPlayer11.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
                            local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer11.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                            local boat = CheckBoat()
                            if boat and boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat') and (humanoidRootPart.Position - boat.VehicleSeat.Position).Magnitude <= 2500 then
                                _G.HadBoat = true
                            else
                                boat = false
                            end
                            if not hasActiveSeaEvent() then
                                if boat then
                                    if localPlayer11.Character.Humanoid.Sit ~= false then
                                        local targetPos
                                        if _G.DangerSc == 'Lv 1' then
                                            targetPos = CFrame.new(-21998.375, 30.0006084, -682.309143)
                                        elseif _G.DangerSc ~= 'Lv 2' then
                                            if _G.DangerSc == 'Lv 3' then
                                                targetPos = CFrame.new(-31171.957, 30.0001011, -2256.93774)
                                            elseif _G.DangerSc == 'Lv 4' then
                                                targetPos = CFrame.new(-34054.6875, 30.2187767, -2560.12012)
                                            elseif _G.DangerSc ~= 'Lv 5' then
                                                if _G.DangerSc == 'Lv 6' then
                                                    targetPos = CFrame.new(-44541.7617, 30.0003204, -1244.8584)
                                                elseif _G.DangerSc == 'Lv Infinite' then
                                                    targetPos = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)
                                                else
                                                    targetPos = CFrame.new(-21998.375, 30.0006084, -682.309143)
                                                end
                                            else
                                                targetPos = CFrame.new(-38887.5547, 30.0004578, -2162.99023)
                                            end
                                        else
                                            targetPos = CFrame.new(-26779.5215, 30.0005474, -822.858032)
                                        end
                                        if (not _G.FishBoat and CheckEnemiesBoat() or (not _G.PGB and CheckPirateGrandBrigade() or not _G.TerrorShark and CheckTerrorShark())) and true or false then
                                            local pos = targetPos * CFrame.new(0, 150, 0)
                                            if (humanoidRootPart.Position - pos.Position).Magnitude > 150 then
                                                _tp(pos)
                                            end
                                        elseif (humanoidRootPart.Position - targetPos.Position).Magnitude > 150 then
                                            _tp(targetPos)
                                        end
                                    else
                                        local seatPos = boat.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)
                                        if (humanoidRootPart.Position - boat.VehicleSeat.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                                            humanoidRootPart.CFrame = seatPos
                                            task.wait(0.1)
                                        else
                                            _tp(seatPos)
                                        end
                                    end
                                elseif _G.ResetWhenBoatBreaks and _G.HadBoat then
                                    localPlayer11.Character.Humanoid.Health = 0
                                    _G.HadBoat = false
                                    task.wait(2)
                                else
                                    local spawnPos = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)
                                    TeleportToTarget(spawnPos)
                                    if (spawnPos.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 15 then
                                        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('BuyBoat', _G.SelectedBoat)
                                        task.wait(1)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            return
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        for _, folder in pairs(folders) do

    local function isTargetAlive(target)
        local humanoid = target and target:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
        if humanoid then
            return target.Humanoid.Health > 0
        end
        return false
    end

    local function getTargetsInRange(range)
        local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer11.Character and localPlayer11.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if not humanoidRootPart then
            return nil, nil
        end
        local targets = {}
        local primaryPart = nil

        local function processTargets(source)
            for _, target in ipairs(source:GetChildren()) do
                if target ~= localPlayer11.Character then
                    if target:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat') and (target:FindFirstChild('Engine') and target:FindFirstChild('Health')) then
                        if (target.Engine.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= range and target.Health.Value > 0 then
                            table.insert(targets, target)
                            if not primaryPart then
                                primaryPart = target.Engine
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        local part = target:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                        if part and isTargetAlive(target) and (part.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= range then
                            table.insert(targets, target)
                            if not primaryPart then
                                primaryPart = part
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        processTargets(enemies)
        processTargets(characters)

        if workspace:FindFirstChild('SeaBeasts') then
            for _, seaBeast in ipairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
                if seaBeast:FindFirstChild('Health') then
                    local part = seaBeast:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if part and (part.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= range and seaBeast.Health.Value > 0 then
                        table.insert(targets, seaBeast)
                        if not primaryPart then
                            primaryPart = part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return targets, primaryPart
    end

    local function getHitData(range)
        local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer11.Character and localPlayer11.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
        if not humanoidRootPart then
            return {}, nil
        end
        local hitData = {}
        local primaryPart = nil

        local function processSource(source)
            for _, target in ipairs(source:GetChildren()) do
                local head = target:FindFirstChild('Head')
                if head and isTargetAlive(target) and (target ~= localPlayer11.Character and (humanoidRootPart.Position - head.Position).Magnitude <= range) then
                    local possibleParts = {
                        'RightLowerArm',
                        'RightUpperArm',
                        'LeftLowerArm',
                        'LeftUpperArm',
                        'RightHand',
                        'LeftHand'
                    }
                    local part = target:FindFirstChild(possibleParts[math.random(#possibleParts)]) or (target.PrimaryPart or head)
                    if part then
                        table.insert(hitData, { target, part })
                        if not primaryPart then
                            primaryPart = part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        processSource(enemies)
        processSource(characters)
        return hitData, primaryPart
    end

    local function resetWeaponCooldown2(weapon)
        pcall(function()
            local cooldown = weapon:FindFirstChild('Cooldown')
            if cooldown then
                cooldown.Value = 0
            end
            local holding = weapon:FindFirstChild('Holding')
            if holding then
                holding.Value = true
            end
            weapon:SetAttribute('Overheat', 0)
            weapon:SetAttribute('LocalOverheat', 0)
        end)
    end

    local function stopAnimations2()
        pcall(function()
            local character = localPlayer11.Character
            if character then
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass('Animator')
                end
                if humanoid then
                    for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                        local name = (track.Name or ''):lower()
                        if name:find('shoot') or (name:find('gun') or (name:find('fire') or name:find('attack'))) then
                            track:Stop(0)
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                return
            end
        end)
    end

    local centerScreen2 = nil

    task.spawn(function()
        while not centerScreen2 do
            task.wait()
            pcall(function()
                local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if currentCamera then
                    centerScreen2 = currentCamera.ViewportSize / 2
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local lastAttackTime = 0

    runService4.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if _G.UseDragonstorm or _G.UseM1Fruit then
            pcall(function()
                local character = localPlayer11.Character
                if isTargetAlive(character) then
                    local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if humanoidRootPart then
                        local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
                        if tool then
                            local weaponType = tool:GetAttribute('WeaponType') or ''
                            local toolTip = tool.ToolTip or ''
                            local isGun = weaponType == 'Gun' or toolTip == 'Gun'
                            local isMelee = weaponType == 'Melee' or weaponType == 'Sword'
                            local isFruit = toolTip == 'Blox Fruit'

                            if isGun then
                                local target, targetPart = getTargetsInRange(4500)
                                if not (target and targetPart) then
                                    return
                                end
                                resetWeaponCooldown2(tool)
                                stopAnimations2()
                                local mousePos = tool:FindFirstChild('MousePos')
                                if mousePos then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        mousePos.Value = targetPart.Position
                                    end)
                                end
                                if centerScreen2 then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        virtualInputManager2:SendMouseButtonEvent(centerScreen2.X, centerScreen2.Y, 0, true, game, 0)
                                        virtualInputManager2:SendMouseButtonEvent(centerScreen2.X, centerScreen2.Y, 0, false, game, 0)
                                    end)
                                end
                                if tick() - lastAttackTime > 0.05 then
                                    lastAttackTime = tick()
                                    local shootEvent = net:FindFirstChild('RE/ShootGunEvent')
                                    if shootEvent then
                                        pcall(function()
                                            local targets = { targetPart }
                                            shootEvent:FireServer(targetPart.Position, targets)
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            elseif isMelee then
                                local hitData, primaryPart = getHitData(60)
                                if #hitData == 0 or not primaryPart then
                                    return
                                end
                                stopAnimations2()
                                if centerScreen2 then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        virtualInputManager2:SendMouseButtonEvent(centerScreen2.X, centerScreen2.Y, 0, true, game, 0)
                                        virtualInputManager2:SendMouseButtonEvent(centerScreen2.X, centerScreen2.Y, 0, false, game, 0)
                                    end)
                                end
                                if tick() - lastAttackTime > 0.05 then
                                    lastAttackTime = tick()
                                    pcall(function()
                                        registerAttack:FireServer(0)
                                        registerHit:FireServer(primaryPart, hitData)
                                    end)
                                    pcall(function()
                                        require(modules.Net):RemoteEvent('RegisterHit', true)
                                        if remoteEvent2 then
                                            cloneref(remoteEvent2):FireServer(string.gsub('RE/RegisterHit', '.', function(char)
                                                return string.char(bit32.bxor(string.byte(char), math.floor(workspace:GetServerTimeNow() / 10 % 10) + 1))
                                            end), bit32.bxor(remoteId2 + 909090, net.seed:InvokeServer() * 2), primaryPart, hitData)
                                        end
                                    end)
                                end
                            elseif isFruit then
                                local target, targetPart = getTargetsInRange(60)
                                local leftClickRemote = tool:FindFirstChild('LeftClickRemote')
                                if leftClickRemote and (target and targetPart) then
                                    stopAnimations2()
                                    if tick() - lastAttackTime > 0.05 then
                                        lastAttackTime = tick()
                                        pcall(function()
                                            leftClickRemote:FireServer(Vector3.new(0.01, -500, 0.01), 1, true)
                                            leftClickRemote:FireServer(false)
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        return
                    end
                else
                    return
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    local function hasSelectedSkills(weaponType)
        if _G.SelectedSkills and _G.SelectedSkills[weaponType] then
            for _, enabled in pairs(_G.SelectedSkills[weaponType]) do
                if enabled == true then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function useSkillsForWeapon(weaponType)
        if not (_G.UseDragonstorm or _G.UseM1Fruit) then
            if hasSelectedSkills(weaponType) then
                weaponSc(weaponType)
                task.wait(0.05)
                local skillKeys = { 'Z', 'X', 'C', 'V', 'F' }
                for _, key in ipairs(skillKeys) do
                    if _G.SelectedSkills[weaponType] and _G.SelectedSkills[weaponType][key] then
                        Useskills(weaponType, key)
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                local localPlayer12 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                local character = localPlayer12.Character
                local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                if humanoidRootPart and humanoid then
                    local seaMobs = {
                        { Var = 'Shark', Name = 'Shark', Check = CheckShark, KillType = 'Normal' },
                        { Var = 'TerrorShark', Name = 'Terrorshark', Check = CheckTerrorShark, KillType = 'Sea' },
                        { Var = 'Piranha', Name = 'Piranha', Check = CheckPiranha, KillType = 'Normal' },
                        { Var = 'MobCrew', Name = 'Fish Crew Member', Check = CheckFishCrew, KillType = 'Normal' },
                        { Var = 'HCM', Name = 'Haunted Crew Member', Check = CheckHauntedCrew, KillType = 'Normal' }
                    }
                    local maxDist = 4500

                    for _, mobConfig in ipairs(seaMobs) do
                        if _G[mobConfig.Var] and mobConfig.Check() then
                            for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                if enemy.Name == mobConfig.Name and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= maxDist))) then
                                    if humanoid.Sit then
                                        humanoid.Sit = false
                                        task.wait(0.1)
                                    end
                                    while true do
                                        task.wait()
                                        if not enemy or (not enemy.Parent or (not enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') or enemy.Humanoid.Health <= 0)) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                        if humanoid.Sit then
                                            humanoid.Sit = false
                                        end
                                        if _G.UseDragonstorm then
                                            weaponSc('Gun')
                                        elseif _G.UseM1Fruit then
                                            weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
                                        end
                                        if mobConfig.KillType ~= 'Sea' then
                                            pcall(function()
                                                self.MobUtils.Kill(enemy, _G[mobConfig.Var])
                                            end)
                                        else
                                            pcall(function()
                                                self.MobUtils.KillSea(enemy, _G[mobConfig.Var])
                                            end)
                                        end
                                        if not _G[mobConfig.Var] or (not enemy.Parent or (not enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') or enemy.Humanoid.Health <= 0)) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if _G.SeaBeast1 and workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('SeaBeast1') then
                        for _, seaBeast in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
                            if seaBeast:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (seaBeast:FindFirstChild('Health') and (seaBeast.Health.Value > 0 and (seaBeast.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= maxDist)) then
                                if humanoid.Sit then
                                    humanoid.Sit = false
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                end
                                while true do
                                    task.wait()
                                    if not seaBeast or (not seaBeast.Parent or seaBeast.Health.Value <= 0) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    if humanoid.Sit then
                                        humanoid.Sit = false
                                    end
                                    task.spawn(function()
                                        _tp(CFrame.new(seaBeast.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, workspace.Map['WaterBase-Plane'].Position.Y + 200, seaBeast.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
                                    end)
                                    if localPlayer12:DistanceFromCharacter(seaBeast.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position) <= 500 then
                                        MousePos = seaBeast.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                        if _G.UseDragonstorm then
                                            weaponSc('Gun')
                                        elseif _G.UseM1Fruit then
                                            weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
                                        else
                                            useSkillsForWeapon('Melee')
                                            useSkillsForWeapon('Sword')
                                            useSkillsForWeapon('Blox Fruit')
                                            useSkillsForWeapon('Gun')
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if not _G.SeaBeast1 or (not seaBeast.Parent or seaBeast.Health.Value <= 0) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if _G.Leviathan1 and workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan') then
                        for _, leviathan in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
                            local segment = leviathan:FindFirstChild('Leviathan Segment')
                            if leviathan:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (segment and (leviathan:FindFirstChild('Health') and (leviathan.Health.Value > 0 and (leviathan.HumanoidRootPart.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= maxDist))) then
                                if humanoid.Sit then
                                    humanoid.Sit = false
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                end
                                while true do
                                    task.wait()
                                    if not leviathan or (not leviathan.Parent or leviathan.Health.Value <= 0) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    if humanoid.Sit then
                                        humanoid.Sit = false
                                    end
                                    task.spawn(function()
                                        _tp(CFrame.new(leviathan.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, workspace.Map['WaterBase-Plane'].Position.Y + 200, leviathan.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
                                    end)
                                    if localPlayer12:DistanceFromCharacter(leviathan.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position) <= 500 then
                                        MousePos = segment.Position
                                        if _G.UseDragonstorm then
                                            weaponSc('Gun')
                                        elseif _G.UseM1Fruit then
                                            weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
                                        else
                                            useSkillsForWeapon('Melee')
                                            useSkillsForWeapon('Sword')
                                            useSkillsForWeapon('Blox Fruit')
                                            useSkillsForWeapon('Gun')
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if not _G.Leviathan1 or (not leviathan.Parent or leviathan.Health.Value <= 0) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    local boatMobs = {
                        { Var = 'FishBoat', Check = CheckEnemiesBoat, Name = 'FishBoat', Dist = 150, Offset = CFrame.new(0, -50, -25) },
                        { Var = 'PGB', Check = CheckPirateGrandBrigade, Name = 'PirateBrigade', Dist = 150, Offset = CFrame.new(0, -30, -10) },
                        { Var = 'PGB', Check = CheckPirateGrandBrigade, Name = 'PirateGrandBrigade', Dist = 150, Offset = CFrame.new(0, -50, -50) }
                    }

                    for _, boatConfig in ipairs(boatMobs) do
                        if _G[boatConfig.Var] and boatConfig.Check() then
                            for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                if enemy.Name == boatConfig.Name and (enemy:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat') and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Engine') and (enemy.Health.Value > 0 and (enemy.Engine.Position - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= maxDist))) then
                                    if humanoid.Sit then
                                        humanoid.Sit = false
                                        task.wait(0.1)
                                    end
                                    while true do
                                        task.wait()
                                        if not enemy or (not enemy.Parent or (not enemy:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat') or enemy.Health.Value <= 0)) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                        if humanoid.Sit then
                                            humanoid.Sit = false
                                        end
                                        task.spawn(function()
                                            _tp(enemy.Engine.CFrame * boatConfig.Offset)
                                        end)
                                        if localPlayer12:DistanceFromCharacter(enemy.Engine.CFrame.Position) <= boatConfig.Dist then
                                            MousePos = enemy.Engine.Position
                                            if _G.UseDragonstorm then
                                                weaponSc('Gun')
                                            elseif _G.UseM1Fruit then
                                                weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
                                            else
                                                useSkillsForWeapon('Melee')
                                                useSkillsForWeapon('Sword')
                                                useSkillsForWeapon('Blox Fruit')
                                                useSkillsForWeapon('Gun')
                                            end
                                        end
                                        if not _G[boatConfig.Var] or (not enemy:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat') or enemy.Health.Value <= 0) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

        local mirageFixedY = nil

        local function fixMirageBoatY(boat)
            if boat then
                local seat = boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat')
                if seat then
                    if not mirageFixedY then
                        mirageFixedY = seat.Position.Y + 175
                    end
                    local pos = Vector3.new(seat.Position.X, mirageFixedY, seat.Position.Z)
                    seat.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    seat.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    seat.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
                end
            else
                return
            end
        end

                                self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
                                task.wait(0.2)
                            end
                            _tp(workspace.Map.MysticIsland.Center.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 300, 0))
                            return
                        else
                            local boat = CheckBoat()
                            if not boat then
                                mirageFixedY = nil
                                local spawnPos = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)
                                TeleportToTarget(spawnPos)
                                if (spawnPos.Position - self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
                                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('BuyBoat', _G.SelectedBoat)
                                end
                                return
                            end
                            if self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false then
                                local seatPos = boat.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)
                                _tp(seatPos)
                                return
                            end
                            while true do
                                task.wait()
                                local targetPos = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)
                                if CheckEnemiesBoat() or (CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade()) then
                                    fixMirageBoatY(boat)
                                else
                                    _tp(targetPos)
                                end
                                if not _G.FindMirage or (workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Mirage Island') or self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false) then
                                    self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

                            if not character then

                            local chests = collectionService:GetTagged('_ChestTagged')
                            local closestChest = nil
                            local closestDist = math.huge
                            for _, chest in pairs(chests) do
                                local dist = (chest:GetPivot().Position - position).Magnitude
                                if not SelectedIsland or chest:IsDescendantOf(SelectedIsland) then
                                    if not chest:GetAttribute('IsDisabled') then
                                        if dist < closestDist then
                                            closestChest = chest
                                            closestDist = dist
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if closestChest then
                                _tp(closestChest:GetPivot())
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        RaceTab:AddToggle({

                                        local swanPirate = GetConnectionEnemies('Swan Pirate')

                                            until self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Flower 3') or (not swanPirate.Parent or (swanPirate.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or Auto_Human == false))
                                        else
                                            tp(CFrame.new(980, 121, 1287))
                                        end
                                    end

        if World2 then
            RaceTab:AddSeperator("Ghoul Race")

            RaceTab:AddToggle({
                Name = "Auto Ghoul",
                Default = self:GetSetting('AutoGhoul', false),
                Callback = function(value)
                    _G.SaveData.AutoGhoul = value
                    self:SaveSettings()
                    _G.AutoGhoul = value
                    if value then
                        local function getMaterials()
                            local success, inventory = pcall(function()
                                return game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')
                            end)
                            if not success or typeof(inventory) ~= 'table' then
                                return {}
                            end
                            local materials = {}
                            for _, item in pairs(inventory) do
                                if typeof(item) == 'table' and (item.Name and item.Type == 'Material') then
                                    materials[item.Name] = item.Count or (item.Amount or 1)
                                end
                            end
                            return materials
                        end

                        task.spawn(function()
                            while _G.AutoGhoul do
                                task.wait(0.5)
                                pcall(function()
                                    local ectoplasm = getMaterials().Ectoplasm or 0
                                    local localPlayer12 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                                    local character = localPlayer12.Character
                                    if not character then
                                        return
                                    end
                                    local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                                    local torch = localPlayer12.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Hellfire Torch') or character:FindFirstChild('Hellfire Torch')
                                    if ectoplasm < 100 then
                                        getgenv().SelectMaterial = 'Ectoplasm'
                                        getgenv().AutoMaterial = true
                                        _G.AutoCursedCaptain = false
                                    else
                                        getgenv().AutoMaterial = false
                                        if torch then
                                            _G.AutoCursedCaptain = false
                                            local experimic = workspace:FindFirstChild('Experimic')
                                            if experimic then
                                                if localPlayer12.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Hellfire Torch') then
                                                    localPlayer12.Humanoid:EquipTool(torch)
                                                end
                                                if (humanoidRootPart.Position - experimic.Position).Magnitude > 15 then
                                                    if _tp then
                                                        _tp(experimic.CFrame)
                                                    else
                                                        humanoidRootPart.CFrame = experimic.CFrame
                                                    end
                                                else
                                                    game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Ectoplasm', 'BuyCheck', 4)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        else
                                            _G.AutoCursedCaptain = true
                                            local entrancePos = Vector3.new(923, 126, 32852)
                                            if (humanoidRootPart.Position - entrancePos).Magnitude > 2000 then
                                                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', entrancePos)
                                                task.wait(0.5)
                                            end
                                            local cursedCaptain = nil
                                            if workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies') then
                                                for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                                    if enemy.Name == 'Cursed Captain' and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                                                        cursedCaptain = enemy
                                                        break
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                            if cursedCaptain and cursedCaptain:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                                                if _tp then
                                                    _tp(cursedCaptain.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
                                                else
                                                    humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cursedCaptain.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0)
                                                end
                                                if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                                                    self.MobUtils.Kill(cursedCaptain, true)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                            getgenv().AutoMaterial = false
                            _G.AutoCursedCaptain = false
                        end)
                    else
                        getgenv().AutoMaterial = false
                        _G.AutoCursedCaptain = false
                    end
                end
            })
        end

    end

        RaceTab:AddButton({
            Name = "Teleport to the top of the big tree",
            Callback = function()
                local tweenService4 = game:GetService('TweenService')
                local localPlayer12 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                if localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                    local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer12.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                    local targetPos = CFrame.new(3026.89453125, 2281.0227050781, -7328.9663085938)
                    local duration = (humanoidRootPart.Position - targetPos.Position).Magnitude / 300
                    local tween = tweenService4:Create(humanoidRootPart, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                        CFrame = targetPos
                    })
                    for _, part in pairs(localPlayer12.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                        if part:IsA('BasePart') then
                            part.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                    tween:Play()
                    tween.Completed:Connect(function()
                        if localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                            localPlayer12.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = true
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        })

        RaceTab:AddButton({
            Name = "Teleport to Temple of Time",
            Callback = function()
                local replicatedStorage6 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
                local workspace2 = game:GetService('Workspace')
                local localPlayer12 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
                local templePos = Vector3.new(28286, 14897, 103)
                local templeCFrame = CFrame.new(templePos)

                local function spawnTemple()
                    pcall(function()
                        local templeOfTime = replicatedStorage6:WaitForChild('MapStash', 5):FindFirstChild('Temple of Time')
                        if templeOfTime and not workspace2:FindFirstChild('Temple of Time') then
                            local map = workspace2:FindFirstChild('Map')
                            if map then
                                templeOfTime.Parent = map
                            else
                                templeOfTime.Parent = workspace2
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end

                task.spawn(function()
                    local commF = replicatedStorage6:WaitForChild('Remotes'):WaitForChild('CommF_')
                    while true do
                        pcall(function()
                            commF:InvokeServer('RaceV4Progress', 'Check')
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            commF:InvokeServer('RaceV4Progress', 'Teleport')
                        end)
                        task.wait(2)
                    end
                end)

                task.spawn(function()
                    spawnTemple()
                    local teleported = false
                    while not teleported do
                        task.wait()
                        pcall(function()
                            local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                            if humanoidRootPart then
                                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = templeCFrame
                                if (humanoidRootPart.Position - templePos).Magnitude < 3 then
                                    teleported = true
                                    for _, child in pairs(humanoidRootPart:GetChildren()) do
                                        if child:IsA('BodyVelocity') or (child:IsA('BodyPosition') or child:IsA('BodyGyro')) then
                                            child:Destroy()
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end
        })

        task.spawn(function()
            local replicatedStorage6 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
            local workspace2 = game:GetService('Workspace')
            local replicatedStorage62 = replicatedStorage6
            local commF = replicatedStorage6.WaitForChild(replicatedStorage62, 'Remotes'):WaitForChild('CommF_')
            local templePos = Vector3.new(28286, 14897, 103)
            local templeCFrame = CFrame.new(templePos)

            local function spawnTemple()
                pcall(function()
                    local templeOfTime = replicatedStorage6:WaitForChild('MapStash', 5):FindFirstChild('Temple of Time')
                    if templeOfTime and not workspace2:FindFirstChild('Temple of Time') then
                        local map = workspace2:FindFirstChild('Map')

                        else
                            templeOfTime.Parent = workspace2

                    pcall(function()
                        local localPlayer12 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                        local humanoidRootPart = localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                        if humanoidRootPart then

                                local race = tostring(localPlayer12.Data.Race.Value)
                                if race == 'Mink' then
                                    _tp(CFrame.new(29020.66015625, 14889.426757812, -379.2682800293))
                                elseif race == 'Fishman' then
                                    _tp(CFrame.new(28224.056640625, 14889.426757812, -210.58720397949))
                                elseif race == 'Cyborg' then
                                    _tp(CFrame.new(28492.4140625, 14894.426757812, -422.11001586914))
                                elseif race == 'Skypiea' then
                                    _tp(CFrame.new(28967.408203125, 14918.075195312, 234.31198120117))
                                elseif race == 'Ghoul' then
                                    _tp(CFrame.new(28672.720703125, 14889.127929688, 454.59616088867))
                                elseif race == 'Human' then
                                    _tp(CFrame.new(29237.294921875, 14889.426757812, -206.94955444336))
                                end
                            else
                                commF:InvokeServer('RaceV4Progress', 'Check')
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                commF:InvokeServer('RaceV4Progress', 'Teleport')
                                spawnTemple()
                                humanoidRootPart.CFrame = templeCFrame
                                for _, child in pairs(humanoidRootPart:GetChildren()) do
                                    if child:IsA('BodyVelocity') or (child:IsA('BodyPosition') or child:IsA('BodyGyro')) then
                                        child:Destroy()
                                    end
                                end
                                task.wait(1)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        function MoveCamtoMoon()
            local lighting32 = self.lighting3
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, lighting32:GetMoonDirection() + workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position)
            local lighting33 = self.lighting3
            self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, lighting33:GetMoonDirection() + self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position)
        end

        function GetSeaBeastTrial()
            if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('FishmanTrial') then
                return nil
            end
            if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Trial of Water') then
                FishmanTrial = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Trial of Water')
            end
            if FishmanTrial then
                for _, seaBeast in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
                    if seaBeast:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and ((seaBeast.HumanoidRootPart.Position - FishmanTrial.Position).Magnitude <= 1500 and seaBeast.Health.Value > 0) then
                        return seaBeast
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(Sec) do
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.Complete_Trials and tostring(self.localPlayer3.Data.Race.Value) == 'Mink' then
                        notween(workspace.Map.MinkTrial.Ceiling.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -20, 0))
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(Sec) do
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.Complete_Trials and (tostring(self.localPlayer3.Data.Race.Value) == 'Fishman' and GetSeaBeastTrial()) then
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                            task.spawn(function()
                                _tp(CFrame.new(GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, game:GetService('Workspace').Map['WaterBase-Plane'].Position.Y + 300, GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
                            end)
                            MousePos = GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position
                            Useskills('Melee', 'Z')
                            Useskills('Melee', 'X')
                            Useskills('Melee', 'C')
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            Useskills('Sword', 'Z')
                            Useskills('Sword', 'X')
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'Z')
                            Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'X')
                            Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'C')
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            Useskills('Gun', 'Z')
                            Useskills('Gun', 'X')
                        until _G.Complete_Trials == false or not GetSeaBeastTrial()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(Sec) do
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.Complete_Trials and tostring(self.localPlayer3.Data.Race.Value) == 'Cyborg' then
                        _tp(workspace.Map.CyborgTrial.Floor.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 500, 0))

        task.spawn(function()

                    end

        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(0.1) do
                pcall(function()
                    local mob = _G.Complete_Trials and (tostring(self.localPlayer3.Data.Race.Value) == 'Human' or tostring(self.localPlayer3.Data.Race.Value) == 'Ghoul') and GetConnectionEnemies({ 'Ancient Vampire', 'Ancient Zombie' })
                    if mob then
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                            self.MobUtils.Kill(mob, _G.Complete_Trials)
                        until _G.Complete_Trials == false or (not mob.Parent or mob.Humanoid.Health <= 0)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        RaceTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Kill Player After Trial",
            Description = "turn on for kill player after the race trials",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoKillPlayerAfterTrial', false),
            Callback = function(value)
                _G.SaveData.AutoKillPlayerAfterTrial = value
                self:SaveSettings()
                _G.Defeating = value
            end
        })

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(Sec) do
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.Defeating then
                        for _, character in pairs(workspace.Characters:GetChildren()) do
                            if character.Name ~= self.localPlayer3.Name then
                                local player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
                                if player and (player.Team ~= self.localPlayer3.Team and (character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (character.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (Root.Position - character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 250)))) then
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait()
                                        self.MobUtils.Kill(character, _G.Defeating)
                                    until _G.Defeating == false or (character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not (character.Parent and (character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid'))))
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
