    WebhookTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Enable Webhook Messages",
        Description = "Automatically send your account info to the webhook",
        Default = self:GetSetting('EnableWebhookMessages', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.SaveData.EnableWebhookMessages = value
            self:SaveSettings()
            _G.WebhookEnabled = value
            if value then
                task.spawn(function()
                    while _G.WebhookEnabled do
                        pcall(function()
                            local webhookURL = _G.WebhookURL
                            if webhookURL and webhookURL ~= '' then
                                local localPlayer12 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
                                local data = localPlayer12:WaitForChild('Data')
                                local level = data.Level.Value
                                local beli = data.Beli.Value
                                local fragments = data.Fragments.Value

                                local function formatNumber(num)
                                    return tostring(num):reverse():gsub('(%d%d%d)', '%1,'):reverse():gsub('^,', '')
                                end

                                local fightingStyles = {}
                                local styleConfigs = {
                                    { cmd = 'BuyBlackLeg', name = 'Dark Step' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuyFishmanKarate', name = 'Fishman Karate' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuyElectro', name = 'Electro' },
                                    { name = 'Dragon Breath', type = 'dragonclaw' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuyDeathStep', name = 'Death Step' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuySharkmanKarate', name = 'Sharkman Karate' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuyElectricClaw', name = 'Electric Claw' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuyDragonTalon', name = 'Dragon Talon' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuySuperHuman', name = 'Superhuman' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuyGodhuman', name = 'Godhuman' },
                                    { cmd = 'BuySanguineArt', name = 'Sanguine Art' }
                                }
                                for _, style in ipairs(styleConfigs) do
                                    local hasStyle = false
                                    if style.type == 'dragonclaw' then
                                        local success1, result1 = pcall(function()
                                            return game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('BlackbeardReward', 'DragonClaw', '1')
                                        end)
                                        local success2, result2 = pcall(function()
                                            return game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('BlackbeardReward', 'DragonClaw', '2')
                                        end)
                                        hasStyle = success1 and (result1 == 1 or result1 == 2) and true or (success2 and (result2 == 1 or result2 == 2) and true or false)
                                    else
                                        local success, result = pcall(function()
                                            return game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(style.cmd, true)
                                        end)
                                        hasStyle = success and (result == 1 or result == 2) and true or false
                                    end
                                    if hasStyle then
                                        table.insert(fightingStyles, style.name)
                                    end
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                end

                                local function getInventoryByType(itemType)
                                    local success, inventory = pcall(function()
                                        return game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')
                                    end)
                                    if not success or typeof(inventory) ~= 'table' then
                                        return {}
                                    end
                                    local items = {}
                                    for _, item in pairs(inventory) do
                                        if typeof(item) == 'table' and (item.Name and item.Type == itemType) then
                                            local name = item.Name
                                            if string.find(name, '-') then
                                                name = string.split(name, '-')[1]
                                            end
                                            items[name] = item.Count or (item.Amount or 1)
                                        end
                                    end
                                    return items
                                end

                                local fruits = getInventoryByType('Blox Fruit')
                                local materials = getInventoryByType('Material')

                                local function getInventoryNames(itemType)
                                    local success, inventory = pcall(function()
                                        return game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')
                                    end)
                                    if not success or typeof(inventory) ~= 'table' then
                                        return {}
                                    end
                                    local items = {}
                                    for _, item in pairs(inventory) do
                                        if typeof(item) == 'table' and (item.Name and item.Type == itemType) then
                                            table.insert(items, item.Name)
                                        end
                                    end
                                    return items
                                end

                                local swords = getInventoryNames('Sword')
                                local guns = getInventoryNames('Gun')

                                local stylesText = table.concat(fightingStyles, '\n')
                                local fruitsText = ''
                                for name, count in pairs(fruits) do
                                    fruitsText = fruitsText .. name .. ' x' .. count .. '\n'
                                end
                                local materialsText = ''
                                for name, count in pairs(materials) do
                                    materialsText = materialsText .. name .. ' x' .. count .. '\n'
                                end
                                local swordsText = table.concat(swords, '\n')
                                local gunsText = table.concat(guns, '\n')

                                local avatarUrl = 'https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/1477493123746893968/1499217390645415966/file_00000000c354720e861b670dd045b116.png'
                                local timestamp = os.time()
                                local description = '** <:NGHT:1459367521399476490> NIGHT MYSTIC NOTIFICATION <:NGHT:1459367521399476490> **\n\n' ..
                                    '> **User Information:**\n```\nUser Name: ' .. localPlayer12.Name .. '\nLevel: ' .. level .. '\nBeli: ' .. formatNumber(beli) .. '\nFragments: ' .. formatNumber(fragments) .. '\n```\n' ..
                                    '> **Fighting Styles:**\n```\n' .. stylesText .. '```\n' ..
                                    '> **Fruits In Inventory:**\n```\n' .. fruitsText .. '```\n' ..
                                    '> **Materials:**\n```\n' .. materialsText .. '```\n' ..
                                    '> **Swords:**\n```\n' .. swordsText .. '```\n' ..
                                    '> **Guns:**\n```\n' .. gunsText .. '```\n' ..
                                    '<t:' .. timestamp .. ':f>'

                                local mentionText = _G.WebhookEveryone and '@everyone ' or ''
                                if _G.WebhookMentionID ~= '' then
                                    if _G.WebhookMentionType == 'role' then
                                        mentionText = mentionText .. '<@&' .. _G.WebhookMentionID .. '>'
                                    else
                                        mentionText = mentionText .. '<@' .. _G.WebhookMentionID .. '>'
                                    end
                                end

                                local payload = game:GetService('HttpService'):JSONEncode({
                                    username = 'Night Mystic | Webhook',
                                    avatar_url = avatarUrl,
                                    content = mentionText,
                                    embeds = {
                                        {
                                            description = description,
                                            thumbnail = {
                                                url = 'https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/1469370641185505331/1499443203592556694/IMG_20260430_131143.png'
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                })

                                local requestFunc = http_request or request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request)
                                if requestFunc then
                                    requestFunc({
                                        Url = webhookURL,
                                        Method = 'POST',
                                        Headers = {
                                            ['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'
                                        },
                                        Body = payload
                                    })
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait((_G.WebhookDelay or 5) * 60)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    local function getFruitName(fruit)
        if type(fruit) ~= 'string' then
            return 'Unknown'
        end
        if string.find(fruit, '-') then
            fruit = string.split(fruit, '-')[1]
        end
        return fruit
    end

    _G.WH_Frozen = self:GetSetting('WebhookFrozenDimensionIsland', false)
    _G.WH_Mirage = self:GetSetting('WebhookMirageIsland', false)
    _G.WH_Kitsune_Island = self:GetSetting('WebhookKitsuneIsland', false)

    function SendStoreFruitWebhook(fruitName)
        pcall(function()
            local webhookURL = _G.WebhookURL
            if webhookURL and webhookURL ~= '' then
                local timestamp = os.time()
                local playerName = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name
                local world = World1 and 'World 1' or World2 and 'World 2' or (World3 and 'World 3' or 'Unknown')
                local mentionText = _G.WebhookEveryone and '@everyone ' or ''
                if _G.WebhookMentionID and _G.WebhookMentionID ~= '' then
                    if _G.WebhookMentionType ~= 'user' then
                        mentionText = mentionText .. '<@&' .. _G.WebhookMentionID .. '>'
                    else
                        mentionText = mentionText .. '<@' .. _G.WebhookMentionID .. '>'
                    end
                end

                local payload = {
                    username = 'Night Mystic | Webhook',
                    avatar_url = 'https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/1477493123746893968/1499217390645415966/file_00000000c354720e861b670dd045b116.png'
                }
                if mentionText == '' or not mentionText then
                    mentionText = nil
                end
                payload.content = mentionText
                payload.embeds = {
                    {
                        title = '** <:NGHT:1459367521399476490> NIGHT MYSTIC NOTIFICATION <:NGHT:1459367521399476490> **',
                        description = '> **Main Status:**\n> **Username:** ||' .. playerName .. '||\n> **World:** ' .. world .. '\n\n> **Storege Fruit:**\n```\n' .. fruitName .. '\n```\n> **Time:**\n> <t:' .. tostring(timestamp) .. ':f>\n\n> **PlaceId:**\n```\n' .. tostring(game.PlaceId) .. '\n```',
                        image = {
                            url = 'https://cdn.discordapp.com/attachments/1469370641185505331/1500293109945466950/Night_Mystic.png?ex=69f7e857&is=69f696d7&hm=1850f5eda68e87165d9bf25f5e7526cd93732afc17c6e76c1dbecf475a26f25a&'
                        }
                    }
                }
                payload.attachments = {}

                local jsonPayload = game:GetService('HttpService'):JSONEncode(payload)
                local requestFunc = http_request or request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request)
                if requestFunc then
                    requestFunc({
                        Url = webhookURL,
                        Method = 'POST',
                        Headers = {
                            ['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'
                        },
                        Body = jsonPayload
                    })
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function SendIslandWebhook(islandName)

    local islandNotified = {}

    task.spawn(function()
        local islandConfigs = {
            { name = 'Prehistoric Island', flag = function() return _G.WH_Prehistoric end, check = function() return pcall(function() return game.Workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Prehistoric Island') ~= nil end) end },
            { name = 'Frozen Dimension', flag = function() return _G.WH_Frozen end, check = function() return pcall(function() return game.Workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Frozen Dimension') ~= nil end) end },
            { name = 'Mirage Island', flag = function() return _G.WH_Mirage end, check = function() return pcall(function() return game.Workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Mirage Island') ~= nil end) end },
            { name = 'Kitsune Island', flag = function() return _G.WH_Kitsune_Island end, check = function() return pcall(function() return game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('KitsuneIsland') ~= nil end) end }
        }

        while task.wait(3) do
            for _, config in ipairs(islandConfigs) do
                pcall(function()
                    local found = config.check()
                    if found and not islandNotified[config.name] then
                        islandNotified[config.name] = true
                        if config.flag() then
                            task.spawn(function()
                                SendIslandWebhook(config.name)
                            end)
                        end
                    elseif not found then
                        islandNotified[config.name] = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

        DragonTab:AddSeperator("Only in Sea 3")
    end

    if World3 then

        local dragonHunterToggle = nil
        dragonHunterToggle = DragonTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Dragon Hunter",
            Description = "turn on for farm blaze ember And auto collect blaze ember",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoDragonHunter', false),
            Callback = function(value)
                if value and (not _G.SelectedBoat or _G.SelectedBoat == '') then
                    Window:Notify({
                        Title = "Night Mystic",
                        Content = "Please select a boat",
                        Duration = 5,
                        Image = "rbxassetid://124914698428562"
                    })
                    task.defer(function()
                        dragonHunterToggle:SetValue(false)
                    end)
                    _G.SaveData.AutoDragonHunter = false
                    self:SaveSettings()
                    _G.FarmBlazeEM = false
                else
                    _G.SaveData.AutoDragonHunter = value
                    self:SaveSettings()
                    _G.FarmBlazeEM = value
                    if value then
                        getgenv().UsePortals = false
                    end
                end
            end
        })

        function checkQuesta()
            local args = { { Context = 'Check' } }
            local result = nil
            pcall(function()
                local requestArgs = { { Context = 'RequestQuest' } }
                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RF/DragonHunter'):InvokeServer(unpack(requestArgs))
            end)
            pcall(function()
                result = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RF/DragonHunter'):InvokeServer(unpack(args))
            end)
            local hasQuest = false
            local mobName = nil
            local killCount = nil
            local questType = nil
            if result and result.Text then
                hasQuest = true
                local text = result.Text
                if string.find(tostring(text), 'Defeat') then
                    local countStr = string.sub(tostring(text), 8, 9)
                    killCount = tonumber(countStr)
                    for _, mob in pairs({ 'Hydra Enforcer', 'Venomous Assailant' }) do
                        if string.find(text, mob) then
                            mobName = mob
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    questType = 1
                elseif string.find(tostring(text), 'Destroy') then
                    mobName = nil
                    killCount = 10
                    questType = 2
                else
                    mobName = nil
                end
            else
                mobName = nil
                hasQuest = false
            end
            return hasQuest, mobName, killCount, questType
        end

        function BackTODoJo()
            for _, notification in pairs(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Notifications:GetChildren()) do
                if notification.Name == 'NotificationTemplate' and string.find(notification.Text, 'Head back to the Dojo to complete more tasks') then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        function DragonMobClear(autoFarm, mobName, targetPos)
            local targetMob = nil
            for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                if enemy.Name == mobName and self.MobUtils.Alive(enemy) then
                    targetMob = enemy
                    break
                end
            end
            if targetMob then
                if autoFarm then
                    self.MobUtils.Kill(targetMob, autoFarm)
                end
            else
                _tp(targetPos)
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait() do
                if _G.FarmBlazeEM then
                    pcall(function()
                        local hasQuest, mobName, killCount, questType = checkQuesta()
                        if hasQuest == true and not BackTODoJo() then
                            if questType == 1 then
                                if mobName == 'Hydra Enforcer' or mobName == 'Venomous Assailant' then
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait()
                                        DragonMobClear(true, mobName, CFrame.new(4620.6157226562, 1002.2954711914, 399.08688354492))
                                    until not _G.FarmBlazeEM or (not hasQuest or BackTODoJo())
                                end
                                return
                            end
                            if questType ~= 2 then
                                return
                            end
                            local destroyPositions = {
                                CFrame.new(5265.47314, 1004.37518, 352.914764),
                                CFrame.new(5256.42334, 1005.57532, 422.089752),
                                CFrame.new(5318.1333, 1004.37518, 439.733185),
                                CFrame.new(5343.82959, 1004.37518, 361.999359)
                            }
                            while true do
                                task.wait()
                                for _, pos in pairs(destroyPositions) do
                                    if not _G.FarmBlazeEM or (not hasQuest or BackTODoJo()) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    _tp(pos)
                                    task.wait(0.3)
                                    Useskills('Melee', 'Z')
                                    Useskills('Melee', 'X')
                                    Useskills('Melee', 'C')
                                    task.wait(0.3)
                                    Useskills('Sword', 'Z')
                                    Useskills('Sword', 'X')
                                    task.wait(0.3)
                                    Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'Z')
                                    Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'X')
                                    Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'C')
                                    task.wait(0.3)
                                    Useskills('Gun', 'Z')
                                    Useskills('Gun', 'X')
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                end
                                if not _G.FarmBlazeEM or (not hasQuest or BackTODoJo()) then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            _tp(CFrame.new(5813, 1208, 884))
                            DragonMobClear(false, nil, nil)
                            return
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        DragonTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Trial Draco",
            Description = "",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoTrialDracoBeta', false),
            Callback = function(value)
                _G.SaveData.AutoTrialDracoBeta = value
                self:SaveSettings()
                _G.Relic123 = value
                if not value then
                    _G.DracoTrialRunning = false
                end
            end
        })

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(1) do
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.Relic123 then
                        if _G.DracoTrialRunning then
                            return
                        elseif workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('DracoTrial') then
                            _G.DracoTrialRunning = true
                            task.spawn(function()
                                pcall(function()
                                    local function isTrialActive()
                                        return _G.Relic123 and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('DracoTrial') ~= nil
                                    end

                                    local function getRootPart()
                                        local character = self.localPlayer3.Character
                                        return character and character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                                    end

                                    local function findProximityPrompt(obj)
                                        for _, child in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
                                            if child:IsA('ProximityPrompt') then
                                                return child
                                            end
                                        end
                                        return nil
                                    end

                                    local function findLavaPart()
                                        for _, child in ipairs(workspace._WorldOrigin:GetChildren()) do
                                            if child:IsA('BasePart') and (child.Material == Enum.Material.Neon and (child.Size.X > 1000 and child.Color == Color3.fromRGB(170, 79, 0))) then
                                                return child
                                            end
                                        end
                                        return nil
                                    end

                                    local function getLavaHeight()
                                        local dracoTrial = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('DracoTrial')
                                        local center = dracoTrial and dracoTrial:FindFirstChild('Center', true)
                                        if center then
                                            return center.Position.Y - 20
                                        else
                                            return -math.huge
                                        end
                                    end

                                    local function waitForLavaToDrop()
                                        local lavaPart = findLavaPart()
                                        if not lavaPart then
                                            return
                                        end
                                        local lavaHeight = getLavaHeight()
                                        if lavaPart.Position.Y <= lavaHeight then
                                            return
                                        end
                                        while isTrialActive() do
                                            local lava = findLavaPart()
                                            if not lava or lava.Position.Y <= lavaHeight then
                                                task.wait(2)
                                                break
                                            end
                                            task.wait(0.5)
                                        end
                                    end

                                    local function getPartPosition(part)
                                        if part then
                                            if part:IsA('BasePart') then
                                                return part.Position
                                            elseif part.PrimaryPart then
                                                return part.PrimaryPart.Position
                                            else
                                                local basePart = part:FindFirstChildOfClass('BasePart')
                                                if basePart then
                                                    return basePart.Position
                                                else
                                                    return part:GetPivot().Position
                                                end
                                            end
                                        else
                                            return Vector3.zero
                                        end
                                    end

                                    local function teleportToPosition(position, tolerance)
                                        local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(position + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0))
                                        local tol = tolerance or 12
                                        for i = 1, 12 do
                                            if not isTrialActive() then
                                                return false
                                            end
                                            waitForLavaToDrop()
                                            if not isTrialActive() then
                                                return false
                                            end
                                            _tp(targetCFrame)
                                            task.wait(1.2)
                                            local root = getRootPart()
                                            if root then
                                                if (root.Position - position).Magnitude <= tol then
                                                    return true
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                        local root = getRootPart()
                                        return root and (root.Position - position).Magnitude <= tol
                                    end

                                    local function interactPrompt(part)
                                        for i = 1, part or 3 do
                                            vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'E', false, game)
                                            task.wait(0.15)
                                            vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, 'E', false, game)
                                            task.wait(0.3)
                                        end
                                    end

                                    local function interactWithPart(part)
                                        if part then
                                            local prompt = findProximityPrompt(part)
                                            if prompt and fireproximityprompt then
                                                pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)
                                                task.wait(0.5)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        interactPrompt(4)
                                    end

                                    local function hasVisibleParts(obj)
                                        for _, child in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
                                            if (child:IsA('BasePart') or child:IsA('MeshPart')) and child.Transparency < 0.9 then
                                                return true
                                            end
                                        end
                                        return false
                                    end

                                    local function waitForRelicPickup(part, timeout)
                                        local startTime = tick()
                                        while isTrialActive() and tick() - startTime < (timeout or 7) do
                                            local prompt = findProximityPrompt(part)
                                            if not (prompt and (prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled)) then
                                                return
                                            end
                                            if not hasVisibleParts(part) then
                                                return
                                            end
                                            if self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Relic') or self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Relic') then
                                                return
                                            end
                                            interactWithPart(part)
                                            task.wait(0.4)
                                        end
                                    end

                                    local function waitForRelicDelivery(part, timeout)
                                        local startTime = tick()
                                        while isTrialActive() and tick() - startTime < (timeout or 7) do
                                            local prompt = findProximityPrompt(part)
                                            if not (prompt and (prompt.Parent and prompt.Enabled)) then
                                                return
                                            end
                                            if not (self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Relic') or self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Relic')) then
                                                return
                                            end
                                            interactWithPart(part)
                                            task.wait(0.4)
                                        end
                                    end

                                    for relicIndex = 1, 3 do
                                        local success = false
                                        if isTrialActive() then
                                            local dracoTrial = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('DracoTrial')
                                            if dracoTrial then
                                                local relicPart = dracoTrial:FindFirstChild('Relic' .. relicIndex, true)
                                                local endRelic = dracoTrial:FindFirstChild('EndRelic' .. relicIndex, true)
                                                if relicPart and endRelic then
                                                    local relicPos = getPartPosition(relicPart)
                                                    local endPos = getPartPosition(endRelic)

                                                    teleportToPosition(relicPos)
                                                    if isTrialActive() then
                                                        interactWithPart(relicPart)
                                                        waitForRelicPickup(relicPart, 7)
                                                        if isTrialActive() then
                                                            teleportToPosition(endPos)
                                                            if isTrialActive() then
                                                                interactWithPart(endRelic)
                                                                waitForRelicDelivery(endRelic, 7)
                                                                if _G.Relic123 then
                                                                    Window:Notify({
                                                                        Title = "Night Mystic",
                                                                        Content = "Draco Trial: Relic " .. relicIndex .. "/3 delivered!",
                                                                        Duration = 5
                                                                    })
                                                                    success = true
                                                                else
                                                                    success = true
                                                                end
                                                            end
                                                        end
                                                    end
                                                else
                                                    task.wait(1)
                                                    success = true
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                        if not success then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end)
                                if _G.Relic123 then
                                    Window:Notify({
                                        Title = "Night Mystic",
                                        Content = "Draco Trial: All 3 relics delivered! Waiting for trial to end.",
                                        Duration = 6
                                    })
                                    while workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('DracoTrial') and _G.Relic123 do
                                        task.wait(1)
                                    end
                                end
                                _G.DracoTrialRunning = false
                            end)
                        else
                            local prehistoricIsland = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('PrehistoricIsland')
                            local teleportPart = prehistoricIsland and prehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild('TrialTeleport')
                            if teleportPart then
                                notween(CFrame.new(teleportPart.Position))
                                task.wait(1.5)
                                pcall(function()
                                    replicated.Remotes.DracoTrial:InvokeServer()
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        return
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        DragonTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto to train race draco",
            Description = "",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoToTrainRaceDraco', false),
            Callback = function(value)
                _G.SaveData.AutoToTrainRaceDraco = value
                self:SaveSettings()
                _G.TrainDrago = value
            end
        })

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait() do
                pcall(function()
                    if not _G.TrainDrago then
                        return
                    end
                    local boneMobs = {
                        'Reborn Skeleton',
                        'Posessed Mummy',
                        'Demonic Soul',
                        'Living Zombie'
                    }
                    local character = self.localPlayer3.Character or self.localPlayer3.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if not humanoidRootPart then
                        return
                    end

                    local function getNearestBoneMob()
                        local closest = nil
                        local closestDist = math.huge
                        if workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies') then
                            for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                if table.find(boneMobs, enemy.Name) and (enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0)) then
                                    local dist = (humanoidRootPart.Position - enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                    if dist < closestDist then
                                        closest = enemy
                                        closestDist = dist
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return closest
                    end

                    if character:FindFirstChild('RaceEnergy').Value == 1 then
                        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, 'Y', false, game)
                        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('UpgradeRace', 'Buy', 2)
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                            _tp(CFrame.new(-9495.68, 453.58, 5977.34))
                        until not _G.TrainDrago or character:FindFirstChild('RaceEnergy') and character.RaceEnergy.Value ~= 1
                        return
                    end

                    if character:FindFirstChild('RaceTransformed').Value == false then
                        local mob = getNearestBoneMob()
                        if mob then
                            while true do
                                task.wait()
                                if mob:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                                    _tp(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
                                end
                                if self.MobUtils and self.MobUtils.Kill then
                                    self.MobUtils.Kill(mob, _G.TrainDrago)
                                end
                                if not _G.TrainDrago or (not mob.Parent or mob.Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        _tp(CFrame.new(-9495.68, 453.58, 5977.34))
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

                                    else

        local prehistoricToggle = nil
        prehistoricToggle = VolcanoTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Find Prehistoric Island",
            Description = "",
            Default = self:GetSetting('PrehistoricFinder_Save', false),
            Callback = function(value)
                if value and (not _G.SelectedBoat or _G.SelectedBoat == '') then
                    Window:Notify({
                        Title = "Night Mystic",

                        Image = "rbxassetid://124914698428562"
                    })

                        prehistoricToggle:SetValue(false)
                    end)
                    _G.SaveData.PrehistoricFinder_Save = false
                    self:SaveSettings()
                    _G.Prehis_Find = false
                else
                    _G.Prehis_Find = value
                    _G.SaveData.PrehistoricFinder_Save = value
                    self:SaveSettings()
                end
            end
        })

        local prehistoricFixedY = nil

        local function fixPrehistoricBoatY(boat)
            if boat then
                local seat = boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat')
                if seat then
                    if not prehistoricFixedY then
                        prehistoricFixedY = seat.Position.Y + 175
                    end
                    local pos = Vector3.new(seat.Position.X, prehistoricFixedY, seat.Position.Z)
                    seat.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    seat.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    seat.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
                end
            else
                return
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait() do
                if _G.Prehis_Find then
                    pcall(function()
                        if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Prehistoric Island', true) then
                            local prehistoricIsland = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Prehistoric Island')
                            if self.localPlayer3.Character and (self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit == true) then
                                self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
                                task.wait(0.2)
                            end
                            if (prehistoricIsland.CFrame.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude >= 2000 then
                                _tp(prehistoricIsland.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 50, 0))
                            end
                            if workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('PrehistoricIsland', true) or workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Prehistoric Island', true) then
                                local activationPrompt = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild('ActivationPrompt')
                                if activationPrompt and activationPrompt:FindFirstChild('ProximityPrompt') then
                                    if self.localPlayer3:DistanceFromCharacter(activationPrompt.Position) <= 150 then
                                        fireproximityprompt(activationPrompt.ProximityPrompt, math.huge)
                                        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 'E', false, game)
                                        task.wait(1.5)
                                        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 'E', false, game)
                                    end
                                    _tp(activationPrompt.CFrame)
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            local boat = CheckBoat()
                            if boat and (boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat') and self.localPlayer3.Character) and (self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - boat.VehicleSeat.Position).Magnitude > 2500) then
                                boat = nil
                                prehistoricFixedY = nil
                            end
                            if boat then
                                if self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit ~= false then
                                    local targetPos = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)
                                    if CheckEnemiesBoat() or (CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade()) then
                                        fixPrehistoricBoatY(boat)
                                    else
                                        _tp(targetPos)
                                    end
                                    if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Prehistoric Island') or not _G.Prehis_Find then
                                        self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
                                    end
                                else
                                    local seatPos = boat.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)
                                    _tp(seatPos)
                                end
                            else
                                prehistoricFixedY = nil
                                local spawnPos = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)
                                TeleportToTarget(spawnPos)
                                if (spawnPos.Position - self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
                                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('BuyBoat', _G.SelectedBoat)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait(1.5) do
                if _G.Prehis_Skills then
                    pcall(function()
                        local prehistoricIsland = game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('PrehistoricIsland')
                        if prehistoricIsland then
                            local core = prehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild('Core')
                            if core and core:FindFirstChild('InteriorLava') then
                                core.InteriorLava:Destroy()
                            end
                            for _, child in pairs(prehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()) do
                                if (child:IsA('Part') or child:IsA('MeshPart')) and (child.Name == 'Lava' or child.Name == 'LavaPart' or child.Name:lower():find('magma')) then
                                    child:Destroy()
                                end
                                if child.Name == 'TouchInterest' and child.Parent and not child.Parent.Name:find('TrialTeleport') then
                                    child.Parent:Destroy()
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait() do
                if _G.Prehis_Skills then
                    pcall(function()
                        local localPlayer12 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                        if workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild('Lava Golem') then
                            local lavaGolem = nil
                            pcall(function()
                                if GetConnectionEnemies then
                                    lavaGolem = GetConnectionEnemies('Lava Golem')
                                else
                                    lavaGolem = workspace.Enemies['Lava Golem']
                                end
                            end)
                            if lavaGolem then
                                if lavaGolem.FindFirstChild(lavaGolem, 'Humanoid') and lavaGolem.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                                    while true do
                                        task.wait()
                                        if lavaGolem.FindFirstChild(lavaGolem, 'HumanoidRootPart') and localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                                            _tp(lavaGolem.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
                                            localPlayer12.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                        end
                                        pcall(function()
                                            sethiddenproperty(localPlayer12, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
                                            lavaGolem:BreakJoints()
                                            lavaGolem.Humanoid.Health = 0
                                            lavaGolem.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                                        end)
                                        if not _G.Prehis_Skills or (not lavaGolem.Parent or lavaGolem.Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        local core = game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('PrehistoricIsland')
                        if core then
                            core = game.Workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild('Core')
                        end
                        if not (core and core:FindFirstChild('VolcanoRocks')) then
                            return
                        end
                        for _, rock in pairs(core.VolcanoRocks:GetChildren()) do
                            if rock:FindFirstChild('VFXLayer') then
                                local vfx = rock.VFXLayer
                                if vfx:FindFirstChild('At0') and (vfx.At0:FindFirstChild('Glow') and vfx.At0.Glow.Enabled == true) then
                                    while true do
                                        task.wait()
                                        if localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                                            local targetPos = vfx.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
                                            _tp(targetPos)
                                            localPlayer12.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                        end
                                        if localPlayer12:DistanceFromCharacter(vfx.CFrame.Position) <= 200 then
                                            MousePos = vfx.CFrame.Position
                                            if Useskills then
                                                Useskills('Melee', 'Z')
                                                Useskills('Melee', 'X')
                                                Useskills('Melee', 'C')
                                                task.wait(0.3)
                                                Useskills('Sword', 'Z')
                                                Useskills('Sword', 'X')
                                                task.wait(0.3)
                                                Useskills('Gun', 'Z')
                                                Useskills('Gun', 'X')
                                                task.wait(0.3)
                                                Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'Z')
                                                Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'X')
                                                Useskills('Blox Fruit', 'C')
                                                task.wait(0.5)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        if not _G.Prehis_Skills or (vfx.At0.Glow.Enabled == false or not rock.Parent) then
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                else
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            end
        end)

        VolcanoTab:AddSeperator("Fully Event Prehistoric Island")

        local function getMaterials()
            local success, inventory = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')
            end)
            if not success or typeof(inventory) ~= 'table' then
                return {}
            end
            local materials = {}
            for _, item in pairs(inventory) do
                if typeof(item) == 'table' and (item.Name and item.Type == 'Material') then
                    materials[item.Name] = item.Count or (item.Amount or 1)
                end
            end
            return materials
        end

                        _G.Prehis_Find = true
                        _G.PrehistoricEvent = true
                        _G.Prehis_Skills = true
                        _G.ResetPH = true
                        _G.Collecting = true
                        if getgenv().IgnoreDinoBones then
                            _G.Prehis_DB = false

                            _G.Prehis_DB = true
                        end
                        if getgenv().IgnoreEggs then
                            _G.Prehis_DE = false

                        _G.Prehis_DE = false

        local frozenFixedY = nil

        local function fixFrozenBoatY(boat)
            if boat then
                local seat = boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat')
                if seat then
                    if not frozenFixedY then
                        frozenFixedY = seat.Position.Y + 175
                    end
                    local pos = Vector3.new(seat.Position.X, frozenFixedY, seat.Position.Z)
                    seat.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    seat.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    seat.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos)
                end
            else
                return
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait() do
                if _G.FrozenFind then
                    pcall(function()
                        if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Frozen Dimension', true) then
                            _G.FrozenFind = false
                            _G.SaveData.FrozenFind_Save = false
                            self:SaveSettings()
                            if frozenFindToggle and frozenFindToggle.Set then
                                frozenFindToggle:Set(false)
                            end
                            if self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') then
                                self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
                                task.wait(0.1)
                            end
                            return
                        else
                            local ownerName = _G.FrozenFindOwner or 'None'
                            local boat = nil
                            if ownerName == 'None' then
                                boat = CheckBoat()
                            else
                                for _, b in pairs(workspace.Boats:GetChildren()) do
                                    if b:FindFirstChild('Owner') and (tostring(b.Owner.Value) == ownerName and b:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat')) then
                                        boat = b
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if not boat then
                                frozenFixedY = nil
                                if ownerName == 'None' then
                                    local spawnPos = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)
                                    if (spawnPos.Position - self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 8 then
                                        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('BuyBoat', _G.SelectedBoat)
                                        task.wait(1)
                                    else
                                        _tp(spawnPos)
                                    end
                                else
                                    Window:Notify({
                                        Title = "Night Mystic",
                                        Content = ownerName .. " doesn't have a boat spawned!",
                                        Duration = 5,
                                        Image = "rbxassetid://12598594243"
                                    })
                                    task.wait(3)
                                end
                                return
                            end
                            local seat = boat.VehicleSeat
                            if self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false then
                                local root = self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                                if root then
                                    if ownerName == 'None' then
                                        _tp(seat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0))
                                    else
                                        local targetPos = seat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0)
                                        local dist = (root.Position - targetPos.Position).Magnitude
                                        local duration = math.max(0.3, dist / (getgenv().TweenSpeedNear or 300))
                                        if getgenv().currentTween then
                                            getgenv().currentTween:Cancel()
                                        end
                                        local tween = self.tweenService:Create(root, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                                            CFrame = targetPos
                                        })
                                        getgenv().currentTween = tween
                                        getgenv().currentTarget = targetPos
                                        tween:Play()
                                    end
                                end
                                return
                            end
                            local targetPos = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)
                            while true do
                                task.wait()
                                if CheckEnemiesBoat() or (CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade()) then
                                    fixFrozenBoatY(boat)
                                else
                                    _tp(targetPos)
                                end
                                if not _G.FrozenFind or (workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Frozen Dimension', true) or self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false) then
                                    if getgenv().currentTween then
                                        getgenv().currentTween:Cancel()
                                        getgenv().currentTween = nil
                                        getgenv().currentTarget = nil
                                    end
                                    if not _G.FrozenFind and self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') then
                                        self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
                                        task.wait(0.1)
                                    end
                                    return
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
