            end
        })

        SeaEventsTab:AddDropdown({
            Name = "Leviathan Fruit Skills",
            Options = { 'Z', 'X', 'C', 'V', 'F' },
            Default = self:GetSetting('LeviathanFruitSkills', { 'Z', 'X', 'C' }),
            Callback = function(value)
                _G.SaveData.LeviathanFruitSkills = value
                self:SaveSettings()
                _G.LeviathanSkills['Blox Fruit'] = value
            end
        })

        local function equipDragonstorm()
            pcall(function()
                local character = self.localPlayer3.Character
                local backpack = self.localPlayer3.Backpack
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                if humanoid then
                    if not (backpack:FindFirstChild('Dragonstorm') or character:FindFirstChild('Dragonstorm')) then
                        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('LoadItem', 'Dragonstorm')
                        task.wait(0.8)
                    end
                    local dragonstorm = backpack:FindFirstChild('Dragonstorm')
                    if dragonstorm then
                        humanoid:EquipTool(dragonstorm)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        _G.LeviathanSkills = _G.LeviathanSkills or {
            Melee = { 'Z', 'X', 'C' },
            Sword = { 'Z', 'X' },
            Gun = { 'Z', 'X' },
            ['Blox Fruit'] = { 'Z', 'X', 'C' }
        }
        _G.LevSkillHold = _G.LevSkillHold or {
            Melee = { Z = 0.5, X = 0.5, C = 0.5 },
            Sword = { Z = 0.5, X = 0.5 },
            Gun = { Z = 0.5, X = 0.5 },
            ['Blox Fruit'] = { Z = 0.5, X = 0.5, C = 0.5 }
        }

        local function useSkillWithHold(skill, holdTime)
            vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, skill, false, game)
            task.wait(holdTime or 0.5)
            vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, skill, false, game)
        end

        local function hasLeviathanSkills(weaponType)
            local skills = _G.LeviathanSkills
            if skills then
                skills = _G.LeviathanSkills[weaponType]
            end
            return skills and #skills > 0
        end

        local function useLeviathanWeaponSkills(weaponType)
            if hasLeviathanSkills(weaponType) then
                weaponSc(weaponType)
                task.wait(0.05)
                local skills = _G.LeviathanSkills[weaponType] or {}
                for _, skill in ipairs(skills) do
                    local holdTime = _G.LevSkillHold and _G.LevSkillHold[weaponType] and (_G.LevSkillHold[weaponType][skill] or 0.5) or 0.5
                    useSkillWithHold(skill, holdTime)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end
        end

        local function getLeviathanSegments()
            local segments = {}
            for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name == 'Leviathan Segment' and (child:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (child:FindFirstChild('Health') and child.Health.Value > 0)) then
                    table.insert(segments, child)
                end
            end
            return segments
        end

        local function getClosestSegment(segments, position)
            local closest = nil
            local closestDist = math.huge
            for _, segment in ipairs(segments) do
                if segment:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                    local dist = (segment.HumanoidRootPart.Position - position).Magnitude
                    if dist < closestDist then
                        closest = segment
                        closestDist = dist
                    end
                end
            end
            return closest
        end

        _G.SeaSkillHold = _G.SeaSkillHold or {
            Melee = { Z = 0, X = 0, C = 0 },
            Sword = { Z = 0, X = 0 },
            Gun = { Z = 0, X = 0 },
            ['Blox Fruit'] = { Z = 0, X = 0, C = 0, V = 0, F = 0 }
        }

        local function hasSeaSkills(weaponType)
            if _G.SelectedSkills and _G.SelectedSkills[weaponType] then
                for _, enabled in pairs(_G.SelectedSkills[weaponType]) do
                    if enabled == true then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function useSeaSkills(weaponType)
            if _G.UseM1Fruit and weaponType == 'Blox Fruit' then
                weaponSc(weaponType)
            elseif hasSeaSkills(weaponType) then
                weaponSc(weaponType)
                task.wait(0.05)
                local skillKeys = { 'Z', 'X', 'C', 'V', 'F' }
                for _, key in ipairs(skillKeys) do
                    if _G.SelectedSkills[weaponType][key] then
                        local holdTime = _G.SeaSkillHold and _G.SeaSkillHold[weaponType] and (_G.SeaSkillHold[weaponType][key] or 0) or 0
                        useSkillWithHold(key, holdTime)
                        task.wait(0.05)
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait() do
                pcall(function()
                    local character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                    local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                    if humanoidRootPart and humanoid then
                        if (_G.Leviathan1 or (_G.LevUseM1Fruit or _G.LevUseDragonstorm)) and workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan') then
                            if humanoid.Sit then
                                humanoid.Sit = false
                                task.wait(0.2)
                            end
                            local segments = getLeviathanSegments()
                            if #segments <= 0 then
                                local leviathan = workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan')
                                if leviathan and (leviathan:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (leviathan:FindFirstChild('Health') and leviathan.Health.Value > 0)) then
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait()
                                        pcall(function()
                                            local leviathan2 = workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan')
                                            if leviathan2 and (leviathan2:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (leviathan2:FindFirstChild('Health') and leviathan2.Health.Value > 0)) then
                                                local pos = leviathan2.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                                local dist = (pos - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                                _tp(CFrame.new(pos.X, workspace.Map['WaterBase-Plane'].Position.Y + 200, pos.Z))
                                                if dist <= 500 then
                                                    MousePos = pos
                                                    if _G.LevUseDragonstorm then
                                                        equipDragonstorm()
                                                    elseif _G.LevUseM1Fruit then
                                                        weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
                                                    elseif _G.UseM1Fruit then
                                                        useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Blox Fruit')
                                                    else
                                                        useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Melee')
                                                        useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Sword')
                                                        useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Blox Fruit')
                                                        useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Gun')
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end)
                                    until not (_G.Leviathan1 or (_G.LevUseM1Fruit or _G.LevUseDragonstorm)) or (not workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan') or #getLeviathanSegments() > 0 or workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan') ~= nil and (workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan'):FindFirstChild('Health') ~= nil and workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan').Health.Value <= 0))
                                end
                            else
                                local targetSegment = getClosestSegment(segments, humanoidRootPart.Position)
                                repeat
                                    task.wait()
                                    pcall(function()
                                        segments = getLeviathanSegments()
                                        if #segments > 0 then
                                            local closest = getClosestSegment(segments, humanoidRootPart.Position)
                                            if closest and (closest:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (closest:FindFirstChild('Health') and closest.Health.Value > 0)) then
                                                targetSegment = closest
                                            end
                                        end
                                        if targetSegment and (targetSegment:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (targetSegment:FindFirstChild('Health') and targetSegment.Health.Value > 0)) then
                                            local pos = targetSegment.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                            local dist = (pos - humanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                            _tp(CFrame.new(pos.X, pos.Y + 80, pos.Z))
                                            if dist <= 300 then
                                                MousePos = pos
                                                if _G.LevUseDragonstorm then
                                                    equipDragonstorm()
                                                elseif _G.LevUseM1Fruit then
                                                    weaponSc('Blox Fruit')
                                                elseif _G.UseM1Fruit then
                                                    useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Blox Fruit')
                                                else
                                                    useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Melee')
                                                    useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Sword')
                                                    useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Blox Fruit')
                                                    useLeviathanWeaponSkills('Gun')
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end)
                                until not (_G.Leviathan1 or (_G.LevUseM1Fruit or _G.LevUseDragonstorm)) or (not workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild('Leviathan') or #getLeviathanSegments() == 0)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    local removeLeviathanToggle = nil
    removeLeviathanToggle = SeaEventsTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Auto Remove Cooldown Leviathan",
        Description = "Automatically farms Sea Events to remove the Leviathan cooldown",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.AutoRemoveLeviathan = value
            if value then
                task.spawn(function()
                    while _G.AutoRemoveLeviathan do
                        pcall(function()
                            if game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('InfoLeviathan', '1') == -1 then
                                _G.SelectedBoat = 'Guardian'
                                _G.DangerSc = 'Lv Infinite'
                                _G.PendingSeaEvents = {
                                    ['Sea Beast'] = true,
                                    ['Pirate Brigade'] = true,
                                    Shark = true,
                                    ['Terror Shark'] = true,
                                    Piranha = true,
                                    ['Fish Crew'] = true,
                                    ['Haunted Crew'] = true,
                                    ['Fish Boat'] = true
                                }
                                _G.SailBoats = true
                                if _G.SaveData and _G.SaveData.UseM1Fruit then
                                    _G.UseM1Fruit = true
                                    _G.Seriality = true
                                    _G.UseDragonstorm = false
                                    _G.ChooseWP = 'Blox Fruit'
                                    if SelectWeapon then
                                        SelectWeapon:Set('Blox Fruit')
                                    end
                                elseif _G.SaveData and _G.SaveData.UseDragonstorm then
                                    _G.UseDragonstorm = true
                                    _G.UseM1Fruit = false
                                    _G.ChooseWP = 'Gun'
                                    if SelectWeapon then
                                        SelectWeapon:Set('Gun')
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                _G.AutoRemoveLeviathan = false
                                _G.SailBoats = false
                                task.defer(function()
                                    removeLeviathanToggle:SetValue(false)
                                end)
                                Window:Notify({
                                    Title = "Night Mystic",
                                    Content = "Leviathan cooldown has been removed!",
                                    Duration = 7,
                                    Image = "rbxassetid://73539136006332",
                                    IconColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                                })
                            end
                        end)
                        task.wait(5)
                    end
                end)
            else
                _G.SailBoats = false
            end
        end
    })

    if World3 then
        _G.DriveBoatOwner = 'None'
        SeaEventsTab:AddSeperator("Boat Navigation")

        local boatOwnerList = { 'None' }
        for _, player in ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player.Name ~= self.localPlayer3.Name then
                table.insert(boatOwnerList, player.Name)
            end
        end

        local boatOwnerDropdown = SeaEventsTab:AddDropdown({
            Name = "Select Boat Owner To Drive To Tiki Island Or Hydra Island",
            Options = boatOwnerList,
            Default = self:GetSetting('SelectBoatOwnerToDriveToTikiIslandOrHydraIsland', 'None'),
            Callback = function(value)
                _G.SaveData.SelectBoatOwnerToDriveToTikiIslandOrHydraIsland = value
                self:SaveSettings()
                _G.DriveBoatOwner = value
            end
        })

        game.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
            if player.Name ~= self.localPlayer3.Name then
                table.insert(boatOwnerList, player.Name)
            end
        end)

        SeaEventsTab:AddButton({
            Name = "Refresh Players",
            Callback = function()
                local newList = { 'None' }
                for _, player in ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if player.Name ~= self.localPlayer3.Name then
                        table.insert(newList, player.Name)
                    end
                end
                boatOwnerList = newList
                boatOwnerDropdown:Refresh(newList)
                Window:Notify({
                    Title = "Night Mystic",
                    Content = "Player list refreshed!",
                    Duration = 3,
                    Image = "rbxassetid://11570018242"
                })
            end
        end)

        local function getTargetBoatSeat()
            local ownerName = _G.DriveBoatOwner or 'None'
            if ownerName == 'None' or not ownerName then
                ownerName = self.localPlayer3.Name
            end
            for _, boat in pairs(workspace.Boats:GetChildren()) do
                if boat:FindFirstChild('Owner') and tostring(boat.Owner.Value) == ownerName then
                    local seat = boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat')
                    if seat then
                        return seat
                    end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function sitInBoat(seat, autoFarmVar)
            local character = self.localPlayer3.Character
            if not character then
                return false
            end
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
            if not humanoid then
                return false
            end
            if humanoid.Sit then
                return true
            end
            local startTime = tick()
            while true do
                task.wait(0.1)
                if autoFarmVar and not _G[autoFarmVar] then
                    break
                end
                local char = self.localPlayer3.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                if not (char and hum) then
                    return false
                end
                if not hum.Sit and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                    _tp(seat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1.5, 0))
                end
                if char and (hum and hum.Sit) or tick() - startTime > 30 then
                    local isSitting = self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                    if isSitting then
                        isSitting = self.localPlayer3.Character.Humanoid.Sit
                    end
                    return isSitting
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        SeaEventsTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Drive Boat To Tiki",
            Description = "Drives the boat automatically through the Tiki Island",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoDriveBoatToTiki', false),
            Callback = function(value)
                _G.SaveData.AutoDriveBoatToTiki = value
                self:SaveSettings()
                _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki = value
                if _G.AutoDriveBoatTikiConn then
                    _G.AutoDriveBoatTikiConn:Disconnect()
                    _G.AutoDriveBoatTikiConn = nil
                end
                if value then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local routeUrl = 'https://pastefy.app/dYFA1WLM/raw'
                        local success, routeData = pcall(function()
                            return loadstring(game:HttpGet(routeUrl) .. '\nreturn ROUTE')()
                        end)
                        if success and (routeData and #routeData >= 2) then
                            local function getSeat()
                                local ownerName = _G.DriveBoatOwner or 'None'
                                local name
                                if ownerName == 'None' or not ownerName then
                                    name = self.localPlayer3.Name
                                else
                                    name = ownerName
                                end
                                local char = self.localPlayer3.Character
                                if char then
                                    local hum = char:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
                                    if ownerName == 'None' and (hum and hum.SeatPart) and hum.SeatPart:IsA('VehicleSeat') then
                                        return hum.SeatPart
                                    end
                                end
                                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                                local closestSeat = nil
                                local closestDist = math.huge
                                for _, boat in pairs(workspace.Boats:GetChildren()) do
                                    if boat:FindFirstChild('Owner') and tostring(boat.Owner.Value) == name then
                                        local seat = boat:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat')
                                        if seat then
                                            if ownerName ~= 'None' then
                                                return seat
                                            end
                                            if root then
                                                local dist = (seat.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                                                if dist < closestDist then
                                                    closestSeat = seat
                                                    closestDist = dist
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                return closestSeat
                            end

                            local seat = getTargetBoatSeat()
                            if seat then
                                if sitInBoat(seat, 'AutoDriveBoatTiki') then
                                    local currentSeat = getSeat()
                                    if currentSeat then
                                        local waypoints = {}
                                        local times = {}
                                        for i = 1, #routeData do
                                            waypoints[i] = routeData[i].cf
                                        end
                                        times[1] = 0
                                        for i = 2, #waypoints do
                                            local dist = (waypoints[i].Position - waypoints[i - 1].Position).Magnitude
                                            times[i] = times[i - 1] + dist / (_G.SetSpeedBoat or 350)
                                        end

                                        local startDist = (waypoints[1].Position - currentSeat.Position).Magnitude
                                        local startDuration = math.max(startDist / (_G.SetSpeedBoat or 350), 0.016)
                                        local startTime = tick()
                                        local startCFrame = currentSeat.CFrame

                                        while tick() - startTime < startDuration do
                                            if not _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki then
                                                return
                                            end
                                            local progress = math.clamp((tick() - startTime) / startDuration, 0, 1)
                                            local newSeat = getSeat()
                                            if newSeat then
                                                pcall(function()
                                                    newSeat.CFrame = startCFrame:Lerp(waypoints[1], progress)
                                                end)
                                            end
                                            task.wait()
                                        end

                                        local finalSeat = getSeat()
                                        if finalSeat then
                                            local driveStart = tick()
                                            local waypointIndex = 1
                                            _G.AutoDriveBoatTikiConn = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                                                if _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki then
                                                    local elapsed = tick() - driveStart
                                                    while waypointIndex < #waypoints and times[waypointIndex + 1] <= elapsed do
                                                        waypointIndex = waypointIndex + 1
                                                    end
                                                    if waypointIndex < #waypoints then
                                                        local t1 = times[waypointIndex]
                                                        local t2 = times[waypointIndex + 1]
                                                        local progress = t2 == t1 and 0 or math.clamp((elapsed - t1) / (t2 - t1), 0, 1)
                                                        local newSeat2 = getSeat()
                                                        if newSeat2 then
                                                            pcall(function()
                                                                newSeat2.CFrame = waypoints[waypointIndex]:Lerp(waypoints[waypointIndex + 1], progress)
                                                            end)
                                                        end
                                                    else
                                                        pcall(function()
                                                            finalSeat.CFrame = waypoints[#waypoints]
                                                        end)
                                                        _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki = false
                                                        _G.AutoDriveBoatTikiConn:Disconnect()
                                                        _G.AutoDriveBoatTikiConn = nil
                                                        Window:Notify({
                                                            Title = "Night Mystic",
                                                            Content = "Arrived at Tiki Outpost!",
                                                            Duration = 5,
                                                            Image = "rbxassetid://12598594243"
                                                        })
                                                    end
                                                else
                                                    _G.AutoDriveBoatTikiConn:Disconnect()
                                                    _G.AutoDriveBoatTikiConn = nil
                                                    return
                                                end
                                            end)
                                        else
                                            _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki = false
                                        end
                                    else
                                        _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki = false
                                        return
                                    end
                                else
                                    _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki = false
                                    return
                                end
                            else
                                local ownerName = _G.DriveBoatOwner or 'None'
                                if ownerName ~= 'None' then
                                    Window:Notify({
                                        Title = "Night Mystic",
                                        Content = ownerName .. " doesn't have a boat spawned!",
                                        Duration = 5,
                                        Image = "rbxassetid://12598594243"
                                    })
                                end
                                _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki = false
                                return
                            end
                        else
                            _G.AutoDriveBoatTiki = false
                            return
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        })

        SeaEventsTab:AddToggle({
            Name = "Auto Drive Boat To Hydra Island",
            Description = "Drives the boat automatically through the Hydra Island route at speed 350",
            Default = self:GetSetting('AutoDriveBoatToHydraIsland', false),

                        end)

                                else

                                        end

                                            local dist = (waypoints[i].Position - waypoints[i - 1].Position).Magnitude
                                            times[i] = times[i - 1] + dist / (_G.SetSpeedBoat or 400)
                                        end

                                        local startDist = (waypoints[1].Position - currentSeat.Position).Magnitude
                                        local startDuration = math.max(startDist / (_G.SetSpeedBoat or 400), 0.016)
                                        local startTime = tick()
                                        local startCFrame = currentSeat.CFrame

                                        while tick() - startTime < startDuration do

                                                return
                                            end

                                            local newSeat = getSeat()
                                            if newSeat then
                                                pcall(function()
                                                    newSeat.CFrame = startCFrame:Lerp(waypoints[1], progress)
                                                end)
                                            end
                                            task.wait()
                                        end

                                        local finalSeat = getSeat()
                                        if finalSeat then
                                            local driveStart = tick()
                                            local waypointIndex = 1
                                            _G.AutoDriveBoatHydraConn = runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                                                if _G.AutoDriveBoatHydra then
                                                    local elapsed = tick() - driveStart
                                                    while waypointIndex < #waypoints and times[waypointIndex + 1] <= elapsed do
                                                        waypointIndex = waypointIndex + 1
                                                    end
                                                    if waypointIndex < #waypoints then
                                                        local t1 = times[waypointIndex]
                                                        local t2 = times[waypointIndex + 1]
                                                        local progress = t2 == t1 and 0 or math.clamp((elapsed - t1) / (t2 - t1), 0, 1)
                                                        local newSeat2 = getSeat()
                                                        if newSeat2 then
                                                            pcall(function()
                                                                newSeat2.CFrame = waypoints[waypointIndex]:Lerp(waypoints[waypointIndex + 1], progress)
                                                            end)
                                                        end
                                                    else
                                                        pcall(function()
                                                            finalSeat.CFrame = waypoints[#waypoints]
                                                        end)
                                                        _G.AutoDriveBoatHydra = false
                                                        _G.AutoDriveBoatHydraConn:Disconnect()
                                                        _G.AutoDriveBoatHydraConn = nil
                                                        Window:Notify({
                                                            Title = "Night Mystic",
                                                            Content = "Arrived at Hydra Island!",
                                                            Duration = 5,
                                                            Image = "rbxassetid://12598594243"
                                                        })
                                                    end
                                                else
                                                    _G.AutoDriveBoatHydraConn:Disconnect()
                                                    _G.AutoDriveBoatHydraConn = nil
                                                    return
                                                end
                                            end)
                                        else
                                            _G.AutoDriveBoatHydra = false
                                        end
                                    else
                                        _G.AutoDriveBoatHydra = false
                                        return
                                    end
                                else
                                    _G.AutoDriveBoatHydra = false
                                    return
                                end
                            else
                                local ownerName = _G.DriveBoatOwner or 'None'
                                if ownerName ~= 'None' then
                                    Window:Notify({
                                        Title = "Night Mystic",
                                        Content = ownerName .. " doesn't have a boat spawned!",
                                        Duration = 5,
                                        Image = "rbxassetid://12598594243"
                                    })
                                end
                                _G.AutoDriveBoatHydra = false
                                return
                            end
                        else
                            _G.AutoDriveBoatHydra = false
                            return
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        })

        local itemConfig2 = require(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').ItemConfig)

        local function countAzureEmber()
            local count = 0
            for _, obj in pairs(getgc(true)) do
                if type(obj) == 'table' and rawget(obj, 'Tiles') then
                    local tiles = obj.Tiles
                    if type(tiles) == 'table' then
                        for _, tile in pairs(tiles) do
                            if type(tile) == 'table' and tile.ItemId then
                                local item = itemConfig2.tryGet(tile.ItemId)
                                if item and (item.Index and (item.Index.DebugLabel and string.find(item.Index.DebugLabel, 'Azure Ember'))) then
                                    count = count + 1
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return count
                    end
                end
            end
            return count
        end

    _G._LastFishNotify = 0

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.5) do
            if _G.AutoFishing then
                pcall(function()
                    local localPlayer12 = players7.LocalPlayer
                    local character = localPlayer12.Character or localPlayer12.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if humanoidRootPart then
                        local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
                        if _G.SelectedRod and (not tool or tool.Name ~= _G.SelectedRod) then
                            tool = localPlayer12.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod)
                            if not tool then
                                return
                            end
                            character.Humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
                        end
                        if tool then
                            local maxLaunchDistance = fishConfig.Rod.MaxLaunchDistance
                            local waterHeight = getWaterHeight(humanoidRootPart.Position)
                            local position = character.Head.Position
                            local direction = humanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * maxLaunchDistance
                            local ignoreList = { character, workspace2.Characters, workspace2.Enemies }
                            local hit, hitPoint = workspace2:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(position, direction), ignoreList)
                            if hitPoint then
                                hitPoint = Vector3.new(hitPoint.X, math.max(hitPoint.Y, waterHeight), hitPoint.Z)
                            end
                            local state = tool:GetAttribute('State')
                            local serverState = tool:GetAttribute('ServerState')
                            if hitPoint and (state == 'ReeledIn' or serverState == 'ReeledIn') then
                                fishingRequest:InvokeServer('StartCasting')
                                task.wait()
                                fishingRequest:InvokeServer('CastLineAtLocation', hitPoint, 100, true)
                            elseif serverState == 'Biting' then
                                fishingRequest:InvokeServer('Catching', true)
                                task.wait(0.1)
                                local success, result = pcall(function()
                                    return fishingRequest:InvokeServer('Catch', 1)
                                end)
                                if success and (result ~= false and tick() - _G._LastFishNotify > 3) then
                                    _G._LastFishNotify = tick()
                                    Window:Notify({
                                        Title = "Night Mystic",
                                        Content = "New Fish Caught!",
                                        Duration = 3,
                                        Image = "rbxassetid://127664059821666"
                                    })
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

                                    local tickPos = tick.AbsolutePosition

                                    if tickPos.X < greenPos.X + greenSize.X and greenPos.X < tickPos.X + tickSize.X and (tickPos.Y < greenPos.Y + greenSize.Y and greenPos.Y < tickPos.Y + tickSize.Y) then
                                        firesignal(slapButton.Activated)
                                    end
                                    local continueScreen = playerGui:FindFirstChild('ContinueScreen')
                                    if continueScreen then
                                        local continueBtn = continueScreen:FindFirstChild('Continue')
                                        if continueBtn and continueBtn.Visible then
                                            firesignal(continueBtn.Activated)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                return
                            end
                        else
                            return
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)

    local function countFish()
        local count = 0
        local success, inventory = pcall(function()
            return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory')
        end)
        if not success or type(inventory) ~= 'table' then
            return 0
        end
        for _, item in pairs(inventory) do
            if type(item) == 'table' and item.Type == 'Fish' then
                count = count + (item.Count or (item.Amount or 1))
            end
        end
        return count
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(5) do
            if _G.AutoSellFish then
                pcall(function()
                    local before = countFish()
                    rFJobsRemoteFunction:InvokeServer('FishingNPC', 'SellFish')
                    task.wait(0.8)
                    local after = before - countFish()
                    if after > 0 then
                        Window:Notify({
                            Title = "Night Mystic",
                            Content = after .. " fish sold successfully!",
                            Duration = 4,
                            Image = "rbxassetid://128182524998174"
                        })
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    RaidTab:AddSeperator("Raid")
    _G.SelectChip = _G.SelectChip or 'Flame'
    _G.AutoRaid = _G.AutoRaid or false
    _G.KillH = _G.KillH or false
    local raidDelay = 0
    local raidCheckDelay = 0.1

    RaidTab:AddDropdown({
        Name = "Select Chip",
        Description = "Choose the raid type",
        Options = {
            'Flame', 'Ice', 'Quake', 'Light', 'Dark', 'String',

        Callback = function(value)
            _G.SaveData.AutoRaid = value
            self:SaveSettings()
            _G.AutoRaid = value
        end

    RaidTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Instant Kill (Islands 4-5)",
        Description = "One-shots enemies only on the 4th and 5th islands",
        Default = self:GetSetting('InstantKillIslands45', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.SaveData.InstantKillIslands45 = value
            self:SaveSettings()
            _G.KillH = value
        end
    })

    local function isRaidMob(obj)
        local name = obj.Name:lower()
        if name:find('raid') or (name:find('microchip') or name:find('island')) then
            return true
        end
        if obj:GetAttribute('IsRaid') or (obj:GetAttribute('RaidMob') or obj:GetAttribute('IsBoss')) then
            return true
        end
        local humanoid = obj:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
        return humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed == 0 and true or (obj.Parent and tostring(obj.Parent):lower():find('_worldorigin') and true or false)
    end

    local function isInRaid()
        local success, visible = pcall(function()
            return game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible
        end)
        return success and visible == true
    end

    local function getRaidIsland(number)
        local success, locations = pcall(function()
            return workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations
        end)
        if not (success and locations) then
            return nil
        end
        for _, location in pairs(locations:GetChildren()) do
            if location.Name == 'Island ' .. number then
                return location
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function getHighestRaidIsland()
        for i = 5, 1, -1 do
            local island = getRaidIsland(i)
            if island then
                return island, i
            end
        end
        return nil, 0
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(raidCheckDelay) do
            if _G.KillH and isInRaid() then
                local _, islandNumber = getHighestRaidIsland()
                if islandNumber == 4 or islandNumber == 5 then
                    for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                            pcall(function()
                                sethiddenproperty(game.Players.LocalPlayer, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
                                enemy:BreakJoints()
                                enemy.Humanoid.Health = 0
                                if enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                                    enemy.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local inRaid = false
    local wasInRaid = false

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            if _G.AutoRaid then
                pcall(function()
                    local localPlayer12 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
                    local isRaidActive = isInRaid()

                    if not wasInRaid and isRaidActive then
                        local root = localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                        if root then
                            local island, _ = getHighestRaidIsland()
                            if island and (root.Position - island.CFrame.Position).Magnitude < 1500 then
                                inRaid = true
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    if wasInRaid and not isRaidActive then
                        inRaid = false
                    end
                    wasInRaid = isRaidActive

                    if inRaid and isRaidActive then
                        local root = localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                        if not root then
                            return
                        end
                        local island, islandNumber = getHighestRaidIsland()

                        if _G.KillH and (islandNumber == 4 or islandNumber == 5) then
                            if island then
                                _tp(island.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0))
                            end
                        else
                            local targetMob = nil
                            local closestDist = 1900
                            for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                if enemy:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (enemy.Humanoid.Health > 0 and (enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and not isRaidMob(enemy))) then
                                    local dist = (root.Position - enemy.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                    if dist < closestDist then
                                        targetMob = enemy
                                        closestDist = dist
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if targetMob then
                                _tp(targetMob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 12, 0))
                                pcall(function()
                                    self.MobUtils.Kill(targetMob, _G.AutoRaid)
                                end)
                            elseif island then
                                _tp(island.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0))
                            end
                        end
                    elseif not (inRaid or isRaidActive) then
                        local chip = self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip') or (self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip'))
                        if not chip then
                            game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('RaidsNpc', 'Select', _G.SelectChip)
                            if not (self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip') or self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip')) then
                                local fruits = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('GetFruits')
                                for _, fruit in pairs(fruits or {}) do
                                    if (self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip') or self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip')) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    local rarity = tostring(fruit.Rarity or ''):lower()
                                    if (fruit.Price or 0) <= 1150000 or (rarity == 'common' or (rarity == 'uncommon' or rarity == 'rare')) then
                                        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('LoadFruit', fruit.Name)
                                        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('RaidsNpc', 'Select', _G.SelectChip)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        local chip2 = self.localPlayer3.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip') or (self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip'))
                        if chip2 then
                            local placeId = game.PlaceId
                            local raidPos = nil
                            local clickDetector = nil
                            if placeId == 4442272183 or placeId == 79091703265657 then
                                raidPos = CFrame.new(-6521.95215, 307.734985, -4813.02441)
                                if workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('CircleIsland') then
                                    clickDetector = workspace.Map.CircleIsland.RaidSummon2.Button.Main:FindFirstChild('ClickDetector')
                                end
                            elseif placeId == 7449423635 or placeId == 100117331123089 then
                                raidPos = CFrame.new(-5034.36523, 315.004669, -2949.36304)
                                if workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('Boat Castle') then
                                    clickDetector = workspace.Map['Boat Castle'].RaidSummon2.Button.Main:FindFirstChild('ClickDetector')
                                end
                            end
                            if raidPos and self.localPlayer3.Character and self.localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                                repeat
                                    task.wait()
                                    _tp(raidPos)
                                until not _G.AutoRaid or (self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - raidPos.Position).Magnitude < 2
                                if _G.AutoRaid and ((self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - raidPos.Position).Magnitude < 5 and (clickDetector and tick() - raidDelay >= 5)) then
                                    fireclickdetector(clickDetector)
                                    raidDelay = tick()
                                    local timeout = tick() + 30
                                    repeat
                                        task.wait(0.5)
                                    until isInRaid() or (timeout < tick() or not _G.AutoRaid)
                                    if isInRaid() then
                                        inRaid = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    PlayerTab:AddSeperator("Players")

    local function getPlayerList()
        local list = {}
        for _, player in pairs(game:GetService('Players'):GetPlayers()) do
            if player.Name ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name and not table.find(list, player.Name) then
                table.insert(list, player.Name)
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    local function refreshPlayerList()
        local list = getPlayerList()
        if #list <= 0 then
            playerDropdown:Refresh({ 'No Players Found' })
        else
            table.sort(list)
            playerDropdown:Refresh(list)
        end
    end

    local aimbotSkillsEnabled = false

    PlayerTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Aimbot Skills",
        Description = "",
        Default = self:GetSetting('AimbotSkills', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.SaveData.AimbotSkills = value
            self:SaveSettings()
            aimbotSkillsEnabled = value
        end
    })

    local players8 = game:GetService('Players')
    local runService6 = game:GetService('RunService')
    local localPlayer12 = players8.LocalPlayer
    local aimbotEnabled2 = true
    local aimbotDelay2 = 0.1
    local aimbotRange2 = 1000
    local aimbotTarget2 = nil
    local aimbotPrediction2 = nil
    local aimbotConnections2 = {}

    function getClosestPlayer(position)
        local closest = nil
        local closestDist = math.huge
        for _, player in ipairs(players8:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= localPlayer12 and player.Character then
                local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('Humanoid')
                local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if humanoid and (humanoid.Health > 0 and root) then
                    local dist = (root.Position - position.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < closestDist and dist <= aimbotRange2 then
                        closest = player
                        closestDist = dist
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return closest
    end

    local function predictPosition(part)
        if part then
            local humanoid = part.Parent:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('Humanoid')
            if humanoid and (aimbotEnabled2 and humanoid.WalkSpeed >= 5) then
                return part.Position + part.Velocity * aimbotDelay2
            else
                return part.Position
            end
        else
            return nil
        end
    end

    local function hasSkillCooldown2(skillName)
        if not skillName then
            return false
        end
        local playerGui = localPlayer12:FindFirstChild('PlayerGui')
        local skills = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild('Main')
        if skills then
            skills = playerGui.Main:FindFirstChild('Skills')
        end
        if skills then
            skills = skills:FindFirstChild(skillName)
        end
        if not skills then
            return false
        end
        for _, key in ipairs({ 'Z', 'X', 'C', 'V' }) do
            local skill = skills:FindFirstChild(key)
            if skill and (skill:FindFirstChild('Cooldown') and skill.Cooldown.Size.X.Scale == 1) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function setupAimbot()
        if not aimbotTarget2 then
            aimbotTarget2 = runService6.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if aimbotSkillsEnabled then
                    local root = localPlayer12.Character and localPlayer12.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    if root then
                        local target
                        if _G.TargetMode == 'Selected Player' then
                            target = game:GetService('Players'):FindFirstChild(_G.PlayersList)
                        else
                            target = getClosestPlayer(root)
                        end
                        if target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                            aimbotPrediction2 = predictPosition(target.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                            if aimbotTarget2 and hasSkillCooldown2(aimbotTarget2.Name) then
                                local direction = (Vector3.new(aimbotPrediction2.X, root.Position.Y, aimbotPrediction2.Z) - root.Position).Unit
                                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, root.Position + direction)
                            end
                        else
                            aimbotPrediction2 = nil
                        end
                    end
                else
                    return
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function cleanupAimbot()
        if aimbotTarget2 then
            aimbotTarget2:Disconnect()
            aimbotTarget2 = nil
        end
    end

    localPlayer12.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        for _, conn in ipairs(aimbotConnections2) do
            pcall(function()
                conn:Disconnect()
            end)
        end
        aimbotConnections2 = {}
        table.insert(aimbotConnections2, character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child:IsA('Tool') then
                aimbotTarget2 = child
            end
        end))
        table.insert(aimbotConnections2, character.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
            if child == aimbotTarget2 then
                aimbotTarget2 = nil
            end
        end))
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local meta = getrawmetatable(game)
        setreadonly(meta, false)
        local namecall = meta.__namecall
        meta.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            local args = { ... }
            if method ~= 'FireServer' and method ~= 'InvokeServer' or (not aimbotSkillsEnabled or (not aimbotPrediction2 or typeof(args[1]) ~= 'Vector3')) then
                return namecall(...)
            end
            args[1] = aimbotPrediction2
            return namecall(unpack(args))
        end)
        setreadonly(meta, true)
    end)

    runService6.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if aimbotSkillsEnabled then
            setupAimbot()
        else
            cleanupAimbot()
        end
    end)

    PlayerTab:AddSeperator("Speed/Jump")

    local runService7 = game:GetService('RunService')
    local localPlayer13 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
    local playerHumanoid
    if localPlayer13.Character then
        playerHumanoid = localPlayer13.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid') or nil
    else
        playerHumanoid = nil
    end

    localPlayer13.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        playerHumanoid = character:WaitForChild('Humanoid')
    end)

    local walkSpeedToggle = self:GetSetting('WalkSpeed_Toggle', false)
    local jumpPowerToggle = self:GetSetting('JumpPower_Toggle', false)
    local walkSpeedValue = self:GetSetting('WalkSpeed', 200)
    local jumpPowerValue = self:GetSetting('JumpPower', 200)

    local function setupSpeedHooks()
        if playerHumanoid then
            playerHumanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal('WalkSpeed'):Connect(function()
                if walkSpeedToggle and playerHumanoid.WalkSpeed ~= walkSpeedValue then
                    playerHumanoid.WalkSpeed = walkSpeedValue
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function applySpeedValues()
        if playerHumanoid then
            if walkSpeedToggle then
                playerHumanoid.WalkSpeed = walkSpeedValue
            end
            if jumpPowerToggle then
                playerHumanoid.JumpPower = jumpPowerValue
            end
        end
    end

    runService7.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if playerHumanoid then
            if walkSpeedToggle and playerHumanoid.WalkSpeed ~= walkSpeedValue then
                playerHumanoid.WalkSpeed = walkSpeedValue
            end
            if jumpPowerToggle and playerHumanoid.JumpPower ~= jumpPowerValue then
                playerHumanoid.JumpPower = jumpPowerValue
            end
        end
    end)

    localPlayer13.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        playerHumanoid = character:WaitForChild('Humanoid')
        setupSpeedHooks()
        applySpeedValues()
    end)

    setupSpeedHooks()
    applySpeedValues()

    local fruitList = {}
    local commF2 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_

    local function updateFruitList()
        local normalFruits = commF2:InvokeServer('GetFruits', false)
        local permFruits = commF2:InvokeServer('GetFruits', true)
        local fruitMap = {}
        for _, fruit in pairs(normalFruits) do
            fruitMap[fruit.Name] = true
        end
        for _, fruit in pairs(permFruits) do
            fruitMap[fruit.Name] = true
        end
        fruitList = {}
        for name, _ in pairs(fruitMap) do
            table.insert(fruitList, name)
        end
        table.sort(fruitList)
    end
    updateFruitList()

            _G.SaveData.SelectFruit = value

            self:SaveSettings()

        end

    local function isFruitOnSale(fruitList, fruitName)
        for _, fruit in pairs(fruitList) do
            if fruit.Name == fruitName and fruit.OnSale then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(1) do
            if _G.SniperEnabled and _G.SelectedFruit then
                local normalFruits = commF2:InvokeServer('GetFruits', false)
                local permFruits = commF2:InvokeServer('GetFruits', true)
                if isFruitOnSale(normalFruits, _G.SelectedFruit) or isFruitOnSale(permFruits, _G.SelectedFruit) then
                    commF2:InvokeServer('PurchaseRawFruit', _G.SelectedFruit)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local notifyRange = 15

    RaidTab:AddToggle({
        Name = "Notify Fruit Spawn/Drop",
        Description = "Notifies and distinguishes spawned vs dropped fruits",
        Default = self:GetSetting('NotifyFruit_Save', false),
        Callback = function(value)
            _G.NotifyFruit = value
            _G.SaveData.NotifyFruit_Save = value
            self:SaveSettings()
        end
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if _G.NotifyFruit then
                pcall(function()
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    if obj.Parent and string.find(obj.Name, 'Fruit') and (obj:IsA('Tool') or obj:FindFirstChild('Handle')) then
                        local pos = nil
                        if obj:FindFirstChild('Handle') then
                            pos = obj.Handle.Position
                        elseif obj:IsA('BasePart') then
                            pos = obj.Position
                        end
                        local dropped = false
                        local playerName = 'Unknown'
                        if pos then
                            for _, player in pairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if player.Character and (player.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - pos).Magnitude <= notifyRange) then
                                    playerName = player.Name
                                    dropped = true
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        local title, text
                        if dropped then
                            title = 'Fruit Dropped'
                            text = obj.Name .. ' dropped by ' .. playerName
                        else
                            title = 'Fruit Spawned'
                            text = obj.Name .. ' spawned on map!'
                        end
                        Window:Notify({
                            Title = title,
                            Content = text,
                            Duration = 10,
                            Image = "rbxassetid://124914698428562"
                        })
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end)

    local function setupAutoBuyStyle(styleName, enabled)
        local toggleName = 'AutoBuy' .. styleName:gsub(' ', '')
        _G[toggleName] = enabled
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G[toggleName] do
                task.wait()
                pcall(function()
                    local styleData = fightingStyles[styleName]
                    if styleData and styleData.Pos[currentWorld] then
                        local targetPos = styleData.Pos[currentWorld]
                        local root = players9.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                        if root then
                            if (root.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 20 then
                                if _tp then
                                    _tp(CFrame.new(targetPos))
                                end
                            else
                                local success, result = pcall(function()
                                    return replicatedStorage7.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(unpack(styleData.Args))
                                end)
                                if success and result ~= false then
                                    Window:Notify({
                                        Title = "Night Mystic",
                                        Content = styleName .. " successfully purchased!",
                                        Duration = 5,
                                        Image = "rbxassetid://134556077676632"
                                    })
                                    _G[toggleName] = false
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
