    local localPlayer15 = game.Players.LocalPlayer

    local function isAlive2(character)
        if not character then
            return false
        end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
        if humanoid then
            return humanoid.Health > 0
        end
        return false
    end

    local function getNearbyTargets(position, range)
        local enemies = game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren()
        local players = game:GetService('Players'):GetPlayers()
        local targets = {}
        for _, enemy in ipairs(enemies) do
            local root = enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
            if root and (isAlive2(enemy) and (root.Position - position).Magnitude <= range) then
                table.insert(targets, enemy)
            end
        end
        for _, player in ipairs(players) do
            if player ~= localPlayer15 and player.Character then
                local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                if root and (isAlive2(player.Character) and (root.Position - position).Magnitude <= range) then
                    table.insert(targets, player.Character)
                end
            end
        end
        return targets
    end

    function AttackNoCoolDown()
        local localPlayer16 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
        local character = localPlayer16.Character
        if not character then
            return
        end
        local tool = nil
        for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA('Tool') then
                tool = child
                break
            end
        end
        if tool then
            local targets = getNearbyTargets(character, 60)
            if #targets ~= 0 then
                local replicatedStorage8 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
                local modules = replicatedStorage8:FindFirstChild('Modules')
                if modules then
                    local rERegisterAttack2 = replicatedStorage8:WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RE/RegisterAttack')
                    local rERegisterHit2 = replicatedStorage8:WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RE/RegisterHit')
                    if rERegisterAttack2 and rERegisterHit2 then
                        local hitData = {}
                        local primaryPart = nil
                        for _, target in ipairs(targets) do
                            if not target:GetAttribute('IsBoat') then
                                local possibleParts = {
                                    'RightLowerArm',
                                    'RightUpperArm',
                                    'LeftLowerArm',
                                    'LeftUpperArm',
                                    'RightHand',
                                    'LeftHand'
                                }
                                local part = target:FindFirstChild(possibleParts[math.random(#possibleParts)]) or target.PrimaryPart
                                if part then
                                    table.insert(hitData, { target, part })
                                    primaryPart = part
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if primaryPart then
                            rERegisterAttack2:FireServer(0)
                            local playerScripts = localPlayer16:FindFirstChild('PlayerScripts')
                            if playerScripts then
                                local script = playerScripts:FindFirstChildOfClass('LocalScript')
                                while not script do
                                    playerScripts.ChildAdded:Wait()
                                    script = playerScripts:FindFirstChildOfClass('LocalScript')
                                end
                                local sendHits = nil
                                if getsenv then
                                    local success, env = pcall(getsenv, script)
                                    if success and env then
                                        sendHits = env._G.SendHitsToServer
                                    end
                                end
                                local success, flag = pcall(function()
                                    return require(modules.Flags).COMBAT_REMOTE_THREAD or false
                                end)
                                if success and (flag and sendHits) then
                                    sendHits(primaryPart, hitData)
                                elseif success and not flag then
                                    rERegisterHit2:FireServer(primaryPart, hitData)
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            return
                        end
                    else
                        return
                    end
                else
                    return
                end
            else
                return
            end
        else
            return
        end
    end

    CameraShakerR = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Util.CameraShaker)
    CameraShakerR:Stop()

    function get_Monster()
        for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
            local part = enemy:FindFirstChild('UpperTorso') or enemy:FindFirstChild('Head')
            if enemy:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart', true) and (part and (enemy.Head.Position - self.localPlayer3.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 50) then
                return true, part.Position
            end
        end
        for _, seaBeast in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
            if seaBeast:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (seaBeast:FindFirstChild('Health') and seaBeast.Health.Value > 0) then
                return true, seaBeast.HumanoidRootPart.Position
            end
        end
        for _, enemy in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
            if enemy:FindFirstChild('Health') and (enemy.Health.Value > 0 and enemy:FindFirstChild('VehicleSeat')) then
                return true, enemy.Engine.Position
            end
        end
    end

    function Actived()
        local tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
        if not tool then return end
        local connections = getconnections(tool.Activated)
        for _, conn in pairs(connections) do
            if typeof(conn.Function) == 'function' then
                getupvalues(conn.Function)
            end
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        RunSer.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            pcall(function()
                if _G.Seriality then
                    AttackNoCoolDown()
                    local tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
                    if tool then
                        local toolTip = tool.ToolTip
                        local hasMonster, _ = get_Monster()
                        local leftClickRemote = toolTip == 'Blox Fruit' and (hasMonster and tool:FindFirstChild('LeftClickRemote'))
                        if leftClickRemote then
                            Actived()
                            leftClickRemote:FireServer(Vector3.new(0.01, -500, 0.01), 1, true)
                            leftClickRemote:FireServer(false)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end)
    end)

    local fastAttackModule = {}
    local players10 = game:GetService('Players')
    local replicatedStorage8 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
    game:GetService('RunService')
    local localPlayer16 = players10.LocalPlayer

    local function safeWaitForChild(parent, name)
        local success, result = pcall(function()
            return parent:WaitForChild(name)
        end)
        if not (success and result) then
            warn('SafeWaitForChild failed: ' .. tostring(name))
        end
        return result
    end

    local net6 = safeWaitForChild(safeWaitForChild(replicatedStorage8, 'Modules'), 'Net')
    local fastAttackConfig = {
        AutoClick = true,
        ClickDelay = 0
    }
    local fastAttackSettings = {
        Distance = 100,
        attackMobs = true,
        attackPlayers = true,
        Equipped = nil
    }
    local registerAttack2 = safeWaitForChild(net6, 'RE/RegisterAttack')
    local registerHit2 = safeWaitForChild(net6, 'RE/RegisterHit')

    local function isTargetAlive2(target)
        local humanoid = target and target:FindFirstChild('Humanoid')
        if humanoid then
            return target.Humanoid.Health > 0
        end
        return false
    end

    local function collectTargets(targets, source)
        local primaryPart = nil
        for _, target in ipairs(source:GetChildren()) do
            local head = target:FindFirstChild('Head')
            if head and isTargetAlive2(target) and (localPlayer16:DistanceFromCharacter(head.Position) < fastAttackSettings.Distance and target ~= localPlayer16.Character) then
                table.insert(targets, { target, head })
                primaryPart = head
            end
        end
        return primaryPart
    end

    function fastAttackSettings.Attack(_, primaryPart, targets)
        if primaryPart and #targets ~= 0 then
            pcall(function()
                registerAttack2:FireServer(fastAttackConfig.ClickDelay or 0)
                registerHit2:FireServer(primaryPart, targets)
            end)
        end
    end

    function fastAttackSettings.AttackNearest()
        local targets = {}
        local enemies2 = workspace:FindFirstChild('Enemies')
        local characters2 = workspace:FindFirstChild('Characters')
        local primaryPart = nil
        if enemies2 then
            primaryPart = collectTargets(targets, enemies2)
        end
        if characters2 then
            primaryPart = collectTargets(targets, characters2) or primaryPart
        end
        local character = localPlayer16.Character
        if character then
            local tool = character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
            if tool and tool:FindFirstChild('LeftClickRemote') then
                for _, targetData in ipairs(targets) do
                    local target = targetData[1]
                    if target and (target:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and character:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')) then
                        local direction = (target.HumanoidRootPart.Position - character:GetPivot().Position).Unit
                        pcall(function()
                            tool.LeftClickRemote:FireServer(direction, 1)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            elseif #targets <= 0 then
                task.wait(0.1)
            else
                fastAttackSettings:Attack(primaryPart, targets)
            end
        end
    end

    function fastAttackSettings.BladeHits()
        local character = localPlayer16.Character
        local alive = isTargetAlive2(character)
        if alive then
            local tool = localPlayer16.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
            if tool and tool.ToolTip ~= 'Gun' then
                fastAttackSettings:AttackNearest()
            else
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(fastAttackConfig.ClickDelay) do
            if _G.Seriality then
                fastAttackSettings:BladeHits()
            end
        end
    end)

    task.defer(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        local tweenService4 = game:GetService('TweenService')
        local runService8 = game:GetService('RunService')
        local localPlayer17 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local kamuiId = '112019130454374'
        local kamuiHeight = 30000

        local function createKamuiIsland(position)
            local existing = workspace:FindFirstChild('RipFamilyIsland')
            if existing then
                existing:Destroy()
            end
            local success, objects = pcall(function()
                return game:GetObjects('rbxassetid://' .. kamuiId)
            end)
            if not (success and (objects and objects[1])) then
                return nil
            end
            local island = objects[1]
            island.Name = 'RipFamilyIsland'
            island.Parent = workspace
            island:PivotTo(CFrame.new(position.Position.X, kamuiHeight, position.Position.Z))
            return island
        end

        local function showKamuiNotification()
            local screenGui = Instance.new('ScreenGui')
            screenGui.Name = 'KamuiNotif'
            screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            screenGui.Parent = localPlayer17.PlayerGui
            local textLabel = Instance.new('TextLabel')
            textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60)
            textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.08, 0)
            textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            textLabel.Text = '< Rip Family Hideout >'
            textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            textLabel.TextSize = 36
            textLabel.TextTransparency = 1
            textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            textLabel.Parent = screenGui
            tweenService4:Create(textLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {
                TextTransparency = 0
            }):Play()
            task.wait(3)
            tweenService4:Create(textLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {
                TextTransparency = 1
            }):Play()
            task.wait(0.3)
            screenGui:Destroy()
        end

        local kamuiRunning = false

        local function teleportKamui()
            if not kamuiRunning then
                kamuiRunning = true
                local root = (localPlayer17.Character or localPlayer17.CharacterAdded:Wait()):WaitForChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                local island = nil
                local spawnPoint = nil

                task.spawn(function()
                    island = createKamuiIsland(root)
                    if island then
                        spawnPoint = island:FindFirstChild('Spawn', true)
                    end
                end)

                local screenGui = Instance.new('ScreenGui')
                screenGui.Name = 'KamuiEffect'
                screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
                screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
                screenGui.Parent = localPlayer17.PlayerGui

                local frame = Instance.new('Frame')
                frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 0, 0)
                frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
                frame.Parent = screenGui

                local imageLabel = Instance.new('ImageLabel')
                imageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 300)
                imageLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0.5, -150)
                imageLabel.Image = 'rbxassetid://120628431436132'
                imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                imageLabel.ImageTransparency = 1
                imageLabel.Parent = screenGui

                local sound = Instance.new('Sound')
                sound.SoundId = 'rbxassetid://132492029022208'
                sound.Volume = 3
                sound.Looped = true
                sound.Parent = localPlayer17.PlayerGui
                sound:Play()

                tweenService4:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 0.75
                }):Play()
                tweenService4:Create(imageLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {
                    ImageTransparency = 0
                }):Play()

                local startTime = tick()
                local rootPart = root
                local islandRef = island
                local spawnRef = spawnPoint
                local duration = 2.5
                local teleported = false

                while tick() - startTime < duration do
                    local progress = (tick() - startTime) / duration
                    imageLabel.Rotation = (tick() - startTime) * 600
                    local size = 300 + progress * 500
                    imageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
                    imageLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -size / 2, 0.5, -size / 2)
                    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.75 - progress * 0.35

                    if not teleported and (0.5 <= progress and islandRef) then
                        teleported = true
                        for i = 1, 5 do
                            if spawnRef then
                                rootPart.CFrame = spawnRef.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
                            else
                                rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(islandRef:GetPivot().Position + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0))
                            end
                            task.wait(0.03)
                        end
                        task.spawn(showKamuiNotification)
                    end
                    runService8.RenderStepped:Wait()
                end

                sound.Looped = false
                sound:Stop()
                tweenService4:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.8), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
                tweenService4:Create(imageLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.8), {
                    ImageTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
                task.wait(0.8)
                screenGui:Destroy()
                sound:Destroy()
                kamuiRunning = false
            end
        end

        local kamuiTool = Instance.new('Tool')
        kamuiTool.Name = 'Teleport: Rip Family Hideout'
        kamuiTool.RequiresHandle = false
        kamuiTool.CanBeDropped = false
        kamuiTool.Parent = localPlayer17.Backpack
        kamuiTool.Activated:Connect(teleportKamui)

        local introGui = Instance.new('ScreenGui')
        introGui.Name = 'KamuiIntro'
        introGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        introGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        introGui.Parent = localPlayer17.PlayerGui

        local introText = Instance.new('TextLabel')
        introText.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)
        introText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.08, 0)
        introText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        introText.RichText = true
        introText.Text = '<font color="#FF0000">Night Mystic</font> <font color="#FFFFFF">Give Kamui For Player!</font>'
        introText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        introText.TextSize = 24
        introText.TextTransparency = 1
        introText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        introText.Parent = introGui

        tweenService4:Create(introText, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {
            TextTransparency = 0
        }):Play()
        task.wait(3)
        tweenService4:Create(introText, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {
            TextTransparency = 1
        }):Play()
        task.wait(0.4)
        introGui:Destroy()
    end)

    Library:SetUIScale(1.0)

    Window:Notify({
        Title = "Night Mystic",
        Content = "Interface loaded successfully!",
        SubContent = "Enjoy using Night Mystic",
        Duration = 3,
        Image = "rbxassetid://73539136006332"
    })

    return {}
end

return Module
